**[00:03:08.980] Committee Secretary** (asr-000050)  
Good afternoon, everyone.

**[00:03:12.760] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000051)  
Thank you, ComSec.

**[00:03:14.820] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000052)  
Again, magandang hapon po sa ating mga kasamahan

**[00:03:18.020] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000053)  
sa Kongreso at sa pamunuan at mga kawani

**[00:03:21.240] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000054)  
ng Department of Migrant Workers.

**[00:03:23.420] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000055)  
Since its establishment in February 2022,

**[00:03:27.860] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000056)  
the DMW has been entrusted with a vital mandate

**[00:03:31.260] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000057)  
to protect the rights, promote the welfare,

**[00:03:34.900] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000058)  
and advance the interests of our overseas Filipino workers.

**[00:03:39.520] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000059)  
Our OFWs continue to make a significant contribution

**[00:03:44.020] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000060)  
to our economy.

**[00:03:45.340] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000061)  
In 2025, OFW remittances reached a record

**[00:03:50.360] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000062)  
of 35.63 billion U.S. dollars,

**[00:03:54.140] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000063)  
3.3% higher than the previous year.

**[00:03:57.700] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000064)  
These remittances account for nearly 8% of our GDP

**[00:04:01.800] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000065)  
and underscore the important role of our migrant workers

**[00:04:06.000] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000066)  
as pillars of the Philippine economy.

**[00:04:08.480] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000067)  
For fiscal year 2027, the DMW is proposing

**[00:04:13.280] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000068)  
a budget of 10.675 billion pesos,

**[00:04:17.400] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000069)  
equivalent to only 0.23% of the national government's proposed budget.

**[00:04:23.400] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000070)  
The budget will support the operations of 42

**[00:04:27.280] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000071)  
migrant workers’ offices worldwide,

**[00:04:29.720] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000072)  
DMW regional offices, and OWA regional offices.

**[00:04:34.160] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000073)  
It will also strengthen the 2 billion peso Action Fund,

**[00:04:39.240] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000074)  
which provides legal, medical, repatriation,

**[00:04:43.140] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000075)  
and other assistance to OFWs,

**[00:04:45.780] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000076)  
as well as the 978 million peso Emergency Repatriation Fund

**[00:04:52.800] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000077)  
for workers affected by conflicts, pandemics, calamities,

**[00:04:57.660] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000079)  
and other emergencies.

**[00:04:59.760] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000081)  
The budget will likewise support key programs,

**[00:05:03.120] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000082)  
including the OFW Hospital, National Reintegration Centers,

**[00:05:07.820] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000083)  
and Maritime Research Services.

**[00:05:11.740] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000084)  
As the Department continues to mature as an institution

**[00:05:15.180] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000085)  
under Republic Act No. 11641,

**[00:05:18.660] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000086)  
we must ensure that it is adequately equipped

**[00:05:21.880] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000087)  
to fully carry out the provisions of the law.

**[00:05:25.480] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000088)  
As mandated, to create a reorganization commission

**[00:05:29.460] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000089)  
that will evaluate accomplishments and impact

**[00:05:33.200] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000090)  
of the DMW Act.

**[00:05:35.500] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000091)  
At this point, I believe the Department has demonstrated

**[00:05:39.040] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000092)  
its commitment to fulfilling its obligation

**[00:05:41.960] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000093)  
and providing our OFWs with protection, assistance,

**[00:05:47.180] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000094)  
and services they deserve.

**[00:05:48.800] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000095)  
I myself, as representative of the 2nd District of Zamboanga del Norte,

**[00:05:54.140] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000096)  
witnessed this commitment,

**[00:05:55.800] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000097)  
in the Department's swift response

**[00:05:57.980] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000098)  
to the tragic case of our OFW constituent

**[00:06:01.920] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000099)  
who was slain in Lebanon.

**[00:06:03.340] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000100)  
In that difficult and heartbreaking situation,

**[00:06:07.140] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000101)  
Secretary Cacdac himself personally attended to the case,

**[00:06:11.480] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000102)  
delivered the remains of our kababayan back to their grieving family,

**[00:06:15.580] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000103)  
and preserved her dignity

**[00:06:17.800] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000104)  
until she was brought to her final resting place.

**[00:06:20.580] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000105)  
This only comes to show

**[00:06:22.740] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000106)  
that the Department understands

**[00:06:24.620] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000107)  
that behind every OFW case is a human being,

**[00:06:29.260] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000108)  
a family, and a community

**[00:06:31.360] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000109)  
waiting for government to act.

**[00:06:34.190] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000110)  
Our OFWs are truly

**[00:06:36.060] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000111)  
the makabagong bayani ng ating panahon.

**[00:06:39.300] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000112)  
It is therefore our responsibility

**[00:06:41.700] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000113)  
to ensure that when they leave our shores

**[00:06:44.680] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000114)  
to build a better future for their families,

**[00:06:47.600] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000115)  
they know that the Philippine government

**[00:06:49.780] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000116)  
will always stand behind them.

**[00:06:51.840] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000117)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[00:06:56.280] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000118)  
At this time,

**[00:06:57.720] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000119)  
I would like to recognize

**[00:07:00.000] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000120)  
the chairperson of the Committee on Overseas Workers' Affairs,

**[00:07:04.460] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000121)  
Honorable Bryan Revilla

**[00:07:06.620] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000122)  
from the AGIMAT Party-list

**[00:07:08.360] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000123)  
to give his opening statement.

**[00:07:10.340] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000124)  
Madam Chair Irene Labad-Labad,

**[00:07:12.640] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000125)  
maraming salamat po sa aking mga kasama

**[00:07:15.320] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000126)  
dito po sa

**[00:07:15.960] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000127)  
Committee of Appropriations.

**[00:07:17.600] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000128)  
Distinguished guests,

**[00:07:18.840] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000129)  
Mr. Secretary,

**[00:07:20.200] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000130)  
sa lahat po ng resource persons ngayong araw na ito,

**[00:07:22.840] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000131)  
magandang hapon po sa inyong lahat.

**[00:07:24.960] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000132)  
As chairman of the Committee on Overseas Workers' Affairs,

**[00:07:29.560] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000133)  
my mandate is clear.

**[00:07:31.300] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000134)  
Ensure that the DMW and OWA

**[00:07:33.860] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000135)  
have the resources to protect and support

**[00:07:36.760] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000136)  
our OFWs in every circumstance.

**[00:07:40.280] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000137)  
Not only when crisis strikes,

**[00:07:42.320] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000138)  
but in the ordinary days

**[00:07:44.320] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000139)  
when preparations matters most,

**[00:07:46.320] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000140)  
and in the difficult days

**[00:07:48.140] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000141)  
when recovery begins.

**[00:07:49.860] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000142)  
The continuing Middle East conflict

**[00:07:52.060] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000143)  
has shown us why this matters.

**[00:07:54.960] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000144)  
Thousands have been repatriated,

**[00:07:56.940] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000145)  
placing significant demands

**[00:07:59.040] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000146)  
on DMW and OWA.

**[00:08:02.300] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000147)  
This year,

**[00:08:03.260] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000148)  
the government provided

**[00:08:04.300] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000149)  
an additional three billion

**[00:08:06.340] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000150)  
for the Action Fund,

**[00:08:07.620] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000151)  
while OWA received one billion

**[00:08:09.640] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000152)  
in supplemental funding,

**[00:08:11.180] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000153)  
including seven hundred million

**[00:08:12.940] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000154)  
for emergency repatriation.

**[00:08:14.760] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000155)  
But the question remains,

**[00:08:17.340] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000156)  
how far can these funds truly go,

**[00:08:20.500] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000157)  
and are they enough

**[00:08:22.360] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000158)  
for whatever crisis

**[00:08:24.100] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000159)  
comes next?

**[00:08:25.970] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000160)  
But our responsibility to our OFWs

**[00:08:28.540] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000161)  
does not begin and end with a crisis.

**[00:08:32.350] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000162)  
I have personally seen

**[00:08:33.900] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000163)  
our DMW and OWA perform

**[00:08:38.120] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000164)  
and serve our kababayans,

**[00:08:40.160] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000165)  
especially in the OFW Servicio Caravan.

**[00:08:43.100] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000166)  
Having participated in a few of them,

**[00:08:46.180] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000167)  
these initiatives bring

**[00:08:47.940] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000168)  
frontline government services

**[00:08:49.620] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000169)  
directly to our OFWs on-site.

**[00:08:53.060] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000170)  
Saving them time, money,

**[00:08:54.680] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000171)  
and the difficult choice

**[00:08:56.040] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000172)  
between accessing government assistance

**[00:08:58.100] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000173)  
and reporting for work.

**[00:09:00.360] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000174)  
This is the kind of service

**[00:09:01.940] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000175)  
that meets our OFWs

**[00:09:03.480] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000176)  
where they are.

**[00:09:04.560] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000177)  
And if we want to bring these services

**[00:09:06.800] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000178)  
to more Filipinos abroad,

**[00:09:08.980] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000179)  
then we must also make sure

**[00:09:12.180] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000180)  
that the DMW and OWA

**[00:09:14.220] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000181)  
have the resources to sustain

**[00:09:16.300] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000182)  
and expand them.

**[00:09:17.920] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000183)  
The question, therefore,

**[00:09:19.620] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000184)  
is not simply how much

**[00:09:21.300] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000185)  
has been spent

**[00:09:22.160] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000186)  
or how much remains.

**[00:09:23.700] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000187)  
The real question is

**[00:09:25.500] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000188)  
whether we are giving

**[00:09:26.920] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000189)  
DMW and OWA enough resources

**[00:09:29.900] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000190)  
to protect our OFWs

**[00:09:32.260] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000191)  
not only in the moments of crisis

**[00:09:34.700] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000192)  
but every day

**[00:09:36.100] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000193)  
in every circumstance.

**[00:09:38.320] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000194)  
And finally,

**[00:09:39.260] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000195)  
we have to look beyond today's crisis

**[00:09:41.280] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000196)  
because Madam Chair,

**[00:09:42.940] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000197)  
this is not simply about

**[00:09:45.080] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000198)  
adding numbers to a spreadsheet.

**[00:09:47.040] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000199)  
Hindi natin hinihingi

**[00:09:49.060] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000200)  
ang karagdagang pondo

**[00:09:50.420] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000201)  
para lamang masabing

**[00:09:51.680] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000202)  
may pondo tayo.

**[00:09:52.720] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000203)  
Hinihingi natin dahil may

**[00:09:54.880] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000204)  
mga Pilipinong umaasa na

**[00:09:57.280] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000205)  
kapag dumating ang oras

**[00:09:58.760] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000206)  
ng kagipitan, handa ang

**[00:10:00.660] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000207)  
kanilang pamahalaan.

**[00:10:03.340] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000208)  
If our OFWs can go

**[00:10:05.880] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000209)  
thousands of miles away from home,

**[00:10:08.940] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000210)  
from their families,

**[00:10:10.280] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000211)  
and our nation,

**[00:10:12.260] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000212)  
the least we can do

**[00:10:13.760] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000213)  
is go the extra mile for them.

**[00:10:16.160] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-000214)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Chair.

Thank you,

Good afternoon,

**[00:10:40.360] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000225)  
distinguished colleagues,

**[00:10:41.680] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000226)  
Secretary Hans Leo Cacdac,

**[00:10:44.400] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000227)  
and officials of the Department

**[00:10:45.960] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000228)  
of Migrant Workers.

**[00:10:47.520] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000229)  
As we deliberate on the proposed budget

**[00:10:49.740] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000230)  
of the Department of Migrant Workers,

**[00:10:52.180] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000231)  
we recognize the very important responsibility

**[00:10:54.500] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000232)  
entrusted to this department.

**[00:10:57.340] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000233)  
Millions of Filipino migrant workers

**[00:10:59.380] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000234)  
sacrificed being away from their families

**[00:11:01.640] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000235)  
to provide them with a better future

**[00:11:03.580] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000236)  
while also contributing greatly

**[00:11:05.940] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000237)  
to our national economy.

**[00:11:07.720] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000238)  
Our responsibility in Congress

**[00:11:09.880] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000239)  
is to make sure that the DMW

**[00:11:12.240] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000240)  
has sufficient resources

**[00:11:13.460] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000241)  
to protect our OFWs,

**[00:11:16.080] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000242)  
respond quickly to those in distress,

**[00:11:18.960] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000243)  
strengthen reintegration programs,

**[00:11:21.200] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000244)  
and provide meaningful assistance

**[00:11:22.400] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000245)  
to their families here at home.

**[00:11:24.980] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000246)  
I look forward to hearing

**[00:11:26.260] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000247)  
how the proposed budget

**[00:11:27.420] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000248)  
will translate into faster,

**[00:11:29.120] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000249)  
more accessible,

**[00:11:30.320] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000250)  
and more effective services

**[00:11:31.640] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000251)  
for every Filipino migrant worker.

**[00:11:33.940] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000252)  
Thank you and I look forward

**[00:11:35.280] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000253)  
to a productive budget briefing.

**[00:11:37.140] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-000254)  
Thank you and good afternoon once again.

**[00:12:03.060] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000261)  
Maraming salamat po Madam Chair

**[00:12:05.480] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000262)  
and Honorable Vice Chairs

**[00:12:07.880] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000263)  
and our Chair of course

**[00:12:09.580] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000264)  
in our Overseas Workers Committee

**[00:12:12.420] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000265)  
and of course Deputy Majority

**[00:12:16.440] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000266)  
Leader, Assistant Majority Leaders

**[00:12:18.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000267)  
and Honorable Congressmen, Congresswomen, colleagues in

**[00:12:23.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000268)  
government. Magandang hapon po

**[00:12:24.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000269)  
sa inyong lahat.

**[00:12:25.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000270)  
Maraming salamat po muli

**[00:12:27.660] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000271)  
at binigyan niyo kaming

**[00:12:28.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000272)  
pagkakataon na ipanukala, ihandog sa inyo ang aming mga programa. Maikli

**[00:12:36.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000273)  
lang po ang ibabahagi

**[00:12:37.480] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000274)  
ko sa

**[00:12:37.760] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000275)  
panimula because

**[00:12:38.520] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000276)  
Usec Nikki Tutay will deliver the

**[00:12:40.840] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000277)  
proposed budget of our

**[00:12:43.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000278)  
department. Pero

**[00:12:44.720] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000279)  
ang gusto ko lang

**[00:12:45.380] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000280)  
pong sabihin ay

**[00:12:46.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000281)  
mula nung itinalaga kami ng ating mahal na

**[00:12:48.520] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000282)  
Pangulo sa

**[00:12:49.840] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000283)  
pamamagitan ng ating mahal

**[00:12:52.280] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000284)  
na kalihim, Secretary Tuts Ople, bilang tahanan

**[00:12:55.760] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000285)  
ng OFWs, ay naisakatuparan

**[00:12:59.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000286)  
at naisaisip at nasa loob

**[00:13:03.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000287)  
na po ng aming mga damdamin.

**[00:13:05.480] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000288)  
Ang ibig sabihin ng

**[00:13:07.640] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000289)  
pagiging tahanan will be

**[00:13:09.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000290)  
paninindigan, pagkalinga, pagmamahal,

**[00:13:13.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000291)  
at pagkakaroon ng emotional

**[00:13:16.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000292)  
bond, I

**[00:13:17.200] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000293)  
should say, sa ating

**[00:13:18.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000294)  
mga OFWs na nangangailangan

**[00:13:21.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000295)  
ng tulong. Ito

**[00:13:22.280] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000296)  
po ang pinakamahalaga at

**[00:13:23.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000297)  
ito na

**[00:13:23.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000298)  
po ang parating ipinahihiwatig ng ating mahal na Pangulo. Make

**[00:13:27.400] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000299)  
sure may impact kami

**[00:13:28.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000300)  
sa buhay ng mga OFWs natin. Nandiyan po ang mga numero. Yung ating Action Fund,

**[00:13:36.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000301)  
nakapagbigay ng 80,000 financial assistance, 14,000 legal assistance sa mga kaso

**[00:13:46.440] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000302)  
handled by our 25 legal retainers, law firms all around

**[00:13:50.200] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000303)  
the world, and 11 in -house counsel. And then

**[00:13:54.500] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000304)  
also around 5,000 given medical assistance dito sa nakaraan lamang

**[00:14:01.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000305)  
na mga hidwaan at emergencies sa Middle East kung saan

**[00:14:05.760] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000306)  
pinakamatinding pangangailangan ng ating mga OFWs ay

**[00:14:09.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000307)  
in tandem with the OWA, admin

**[00:14:12.200] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000308)  
PY and the team at OWA ay 12,000 ang napauwi nangangailangan ng

**[00:14:18.380] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000309)  
tulong na makauwi sa tinatawag niya theater of operations,

**[00:14:24.520] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000310)  
armed operations, military operations nung Hidwaan. Pitong bansa

**[00:14:30.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000311)  
po ang nasasangkot dito,

**[00:14:32.800] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000312)  
12,000 naka

**[00:14:34.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000313)  
-uwi. At dun sa mga hindi naka -uwi,

**[00:14:37.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000314)  
higit kumulang 20,000 naman po ang nabigyan ng

**[00:14:39.640] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000315)  
financial assistance sa pamamagitan ng 200

**[00:14:44.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000316)  
US dollar financial assistance dahil sila ay either

**[00:14:47.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000317)  
nabawasan ng sweldo, nabawasan ng

**[00:14:49.720] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000318)  
working hours,

**[00:14:50.400] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000319)  
nabawasan ng

**[00:14:51.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000320)  
work shift,

**[00:14:51.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000321)  
nabawasan ng OT sa gitna

**[00:14:53.840] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000322)  
ng sigalot

**[00:14:55.100] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000323)  
at yung mga

**[00:14:55.660] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000324)  
apektadong sektor

**[00:14:56.800] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000325)  
sa host

**[00:14:57.560] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000326)  
countries

**[00:14:58.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000327)  
sa gitna ng

**[00:14:59.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000328)  
sigalot.

**[00:15:01.110] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000329)  
Sa ating pagkakaroon

**[00:15:03.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000330)  
ng delivery of services

**[00:15:04.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000331)  
sa OFW Hospital

**[00:15:07.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000332)  
ay higit

**[00:15:09.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000333)  
kumulang mga 150,000

**[00:15:11.440] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000334)  
po ang nabigyan natin ng servisyo

**[00:15:13.240] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000335)  
over the last

**[00:15:15.060] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000336)  
3 years. Inpatient

**[00:15:17.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000337)  
outpatient services

**[00:15:18.240] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000338)  
sa OFW Hospital.

**[00:15:20.640] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000339)  
Sa ating reintegration services

**[00:15:23.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000340)  
higit kumulang

**[00:15:25.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000341)  
mga halos 50,000

**[00:15:26.640] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000342)  
sa pamamagitan ng ating

**[00:15:29.240] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000343)  
kalinga, kaalaman at kabuhayan

**[00:15:31.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000344)  
framework. Meron

**[00:15:33.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000345)  
din po tayong digitalization

**[00:15:34.880] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000346)  
services para gumaan naman

**[00:15:37.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000347)  
ang proseso para sa ating mga

**[00:15:38.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000348)  
OFWs. At higit kumulang

**[00:15:41.300] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000349)  
1.5 million na po

**[00:15:42.840] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000350)  
ang na-issue ng OFW passes

**[00:15:44.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000351)  
para gumaan ang proseso

**[00:15:46.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000352)  
through mobile phone apps

**[00:15:49.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000353)  
through egov.ph na pinag-utos

**[00:15:51.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000354)  
ng ating Pangulo

**[00:15:52.480] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000355)  
sa amin ni Secretary Henry Aguda

**[00:15:54.940] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000356)  
ng DICT.

**[00:15:55.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000357)  
1.5 million OFW

**[00:15:58.060] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000358)  
passes para gumaan na po

**[00:16:00.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000359)  
ang proseso para sa kanila

**[00:16:02.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000360)  
at patuloy pong linilinang

**[00:16:04.920] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000361)  
at sinisikap pa namin

**[00:16:06.820] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000362)  
gawing perpekto yung

**[00:16:08.460] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000363)  
sistema. Admittedly, it is still going

**[00:16:10.380] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000364)  
through some growing pains

**[00:16:12.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000365)  
as any average

**[00:16:14.380] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000366)  
normal IT system would

**[00:16:16.460] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000367)  
encounter. Gusto rin

**[00:16:18.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000368)  
ho namin sabihin na

**[00:16:20.500] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000369)  
sa mga programa namin,

**[00:16:22.280] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000370)  
katuwang ng OWWA, nandito

**[00:16:24.560] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000371)  
kami para sa mga OFWs.

**[00:16:26.580] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000372)  
na nangangailangan ng tulong.

**[00:16:28.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000373)  
But more than the statistics,

**[00:16:29.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000374)  
and I will close the introduction with this,

**[00:16:32.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000375)  
ang direct impact na ibig kubong

**[00:16:34.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000376)  
sabihin ay talagang

**[00:16:35.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000377)  
we make sure that we touch the lives

**[00:16:37.660] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000378)  
of OFWs in need.

**[00:16:39.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000379)  
By way of example,

**[00:16:40.920] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000380)  
meron po tayong mga seafarers

**[00:16:43.560] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000381)  
na sa kinasamaang-palad nasawi

**[00:16:45.760] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000382)  
sa gitna ng hidwaan dito sa

**[00:16:47.760] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000383)  
either Red Sea or Northern Black Sea

**[00:16:50.480] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000384)  
at sa Strait of Hormuz.

**[00:16:52.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000385)  
Pero ang sinasagawa po natin dun,

**[00:16:54.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000386)  
meron tayong mga regulasyon,

**[00:16:55.960] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000387)  
to make sure na napoproteksyonan

**[00:16:58.500] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000388)  
ang ating seafarers.

**[00:17:00.060] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000389)  
Pero it can happen.

**[00:17:01.940] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000390)  
Sa utos ng ating Pangulo,

**[00:17:03.880] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000391)  
sabi ng ating Pangulo, alagaan ang mga

**[00:17:05.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000392)  
pamilya, lalo na yung mga pamilyang

**[00:17:07.800] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000393)  
na wala na mahal sa buhay sa gitna ng hidwaan.

**[00:17:10.580] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000394)  
Kaya it can happen

**[00:17:12.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000395)  
for instance, na merong

**[00:17:13.880] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000396)  
panganay na anak o bunsong anak

**[00:17:15.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000397)  
na babae, katulad ng itong

**[00:17:17.580] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000398)  
isang pamilya na tinulungan namin.

**[00:17:20.100] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000399)  
Dahil sa kinaratna

**[00:17:22.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000400)  
na nasawi yung kanyang ama,

**[00:17:23.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000401)  
na-depress po yung bata,

**[00:17:25.420] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000402)  
at ayaw pong pumasok sa eskwela.

**[00:17:28.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000403)  
So nagkaroon ng

**[00:17:29.460] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000404)  
interventions, pumasok po

**[00:17:31.800] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000405)  
ang regional offices natin in partnership

**[00:17:33.700] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000406)  
with the OWA, DSWD,

**[00:17:35.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000407)  
para mabigyan ng

**[00:17:37.720] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000408)  
counseling yung bata at siya ay makabalik,

**[00:17:40.100] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000409)  
matulungan

**[00:17:42.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000410)  
ng bata makabalik sa eskwela.

**[00:17:43.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000411)  
Meron pong din tayong similar

**[00:17:45.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000412)  
situation, nakilala ko po

**[00:17:47.760] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000413)  
sa Istanbul, Turkey,

**[00:17:49.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000414)  
sa ospital, o isang OFW

**[00:17:51.820] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000415)  
natin sugatan, nandun yung

**[00:17:53.760] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000416)  
kanyang asawa, yung kanyang

**[00:17:55.840] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000417)  
esposa, at ang problema,

**[00:17:57.640] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000418)  
dahil nandun yung esposa para

**[00:17:59.560] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000419)  
bantayan yung kanyang asawa, walang naghahatid

**[00:18:01.880] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000420)  
sa kanyang anak

**[00:18:03.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000421)  
na grade 1 para pumasok

**[00:18:05.960] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000422)  
at therefore nahinto rin sa eskwela

**[00:18:07.920] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000423)  
yung bata. So through

**[00:18:09.820] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000424)  
our regional offices at sa LGU

**[00:18:11.880] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000425)  
involved, natutulungan po natin ngayon

**[00:18:13.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000426)  
ng bata na pumasok sa eskwela.

**[00:18:15.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000427)  
Binibigay ko lang po ang mga ehemplo

**[00:18:17.820] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000428)  
na ito dahil ito puro po

**[00:18:19.300] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000429)  
sa amin ang pinakamahalaga.

**[00:18:21.500] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000430)  
Ito yung ibig kong sabihin

**[00:18:23.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000431)  
dun sa tahanan bilang

**[00:18:25.640] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000432)  
emotional bond that links us

**[00:18:27.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000433)  
between ourselves and OFWs

**[00:18:30.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000434)  
and OFW families.

**[00:18:31.760] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000435)  
And I can tell you, every

**[00:18:33.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000436)  
centavo counts.

**[00:18:36.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000437)  
When we are in a position,

**[00:18:38.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000438)  
kami ni Admin PY, and we

**[00:18:39.940] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000439)  
are in a position to say

**[00:18:41.440] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000440)  
yes to a request,

**[00:18:43.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000441)  
ang laking bagay po niyan sa OFW.

**[00:18:45.940] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000442)  
When we are in a position

**[00:18:47.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000443)  
to not even wait for the

**[00:18:49.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000444)  
request for assistance, but volunteer

**[00:18:51.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000445)  
our services, ang laki pong

**[00:18:53.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000446)  
kumpiyansa sa amin dahil may

**[00:18:55.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000447)  
pondo kami, meron kaming resources

**[00:18:57.840] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000448)  
to back it up. Maraming salamat

**[00:19:00.280] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000449)  
po sa inyo sa tulong ninyong

**[00:19:02.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000450)  
naibigay na sa amin sa pondong

**[00:19:04.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000451)  
naibigay nyo sa amin. And I'm

**[00:19:06.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000452)  
sure in the course of our discussions,

**[00:19:08.060] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000453)  
meron din po kaming mga kahilingan

**[00:19:10.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000454)  
pa para po malo

**[00:19:12.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000455)  
pang mapalakas, and rest assured

**[00:19:14.480] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000456)  
you have our thanks,

**[00:19:16.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000457)  
our gratitude, and our assured commitment.

**[00:19:18.420] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000458)  
Maraming salamat po, and

**[00:19:20.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000459)  
if I may request, Usec Nikki, to

**[00:19:22.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000460)  
deliver our presentation.

**[00:24:58.240] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000621)

For OWA,

4.349

**[00:25:27.880] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000634)

**[00:25:57.740] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000651)

**[00:26:00.360] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000652)

**[00:26:02.180] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000653)

**[00:26:06.220] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000654)  
The DMW and OWA received

**[00:26:08.020] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000655)

**[00:26:10.120] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000656)

**[00:26:11.820] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000657)

**[00:26:14.200] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000658)

**[00:26:16.160] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000659)

**[00:26:18.140] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000660)

31, 2025,

**[00:26:21.180] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000662)  
and 47% as of July

**[00:26:24.300] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000663)

**[00:26:26.800] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000664)  
We received

**[00:26:29.680] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000666)

**[00:26:31.980] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000667)

**[00:26:36.120] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000669)  
Without the additional action

**[00:26:38.100] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000670)

**[00:26:40.220] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000671)

**[00:26:41.940] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000672)

**[00:26:43.940] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000673)  
We also achieved

**[00:26:47.600] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000675)

**[00:26:53.940] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000678)

**[00:26:55.580] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000679)  
2026. Meanwhile

**[00:26:58.340] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000680)  
for OWA,

**[00:26:59.380] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000681)  
utilization rate is also high

**[00:27:02.200] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000682)

**[00:27:04.080] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000683)

**[00:27:06.160] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000684)  
82% as of July of this

**[00:27:08.160] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000685)

**[00:27:10.140] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000686)

**[00:27:16.060] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-000689)  
That's for the financial side.

ang nagtatrabaho sa iba't-ibang

Nagsisimula ang proteksyon

**[00:28:43.200] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000720)

**[00:28:45.160] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000721)

**[00:28:47.880] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000722)

**[00:28:50.200] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000723)

**[00:28:51.980] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000724)  
ang manggagawa, binabantayan na

**[00:28:54.020] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000725)

**[00:28:56.080] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000726)

**[00:29:01.680] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000728)  
nagpapatuloy ang serbisyo.

**[00:29:04.100] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000729)  
May mga tanggapan

**[00:29:05.680] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000730)  
at taong maaaring lapitan

**[00:29:07.720] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000731)

**[00:29:11.360] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000732)

**[00:29:13.820] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000733)

**[00:29:15.920] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000734)  
sa pamamagitan ng kasunduan

**[00:29:18.060] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000735)  
at patuloy na koordinasyon.

**[00:29:23.280] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000736)  
Para sa domestic workers,

**[00:29:25.400] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000737)  
pinalakas ang safeguards

**[00:29:27.120] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000738)  
sa trabaho at welfare.

**[00:29:34.100] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000739)  
Pero, may mga krisis

**[00:29:36.140] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000740)  
na biglang nagbabago

**[00:29:37.720] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000741)  
sa buong buhay ng isang OFW.

**[00:29:44.400] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000742)  
Middle East Crisis.

**[00:29:45.800] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000743)  
Kumilos ang one-country team ng pamahalaan.

**[00:29:55.940] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000744)  
Nagsimula tayo doon sa mga direktang naapektuhan

**[00:29:59.340] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000745)  
ng kaguluhan. Ang pagsaklolo sa ating mga kababayang OFW na naipit sa digmaan, mahalagang misyon ito ng pamahalaan.

**[00:30:08.400] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000746)  
Sa pangunguna ng DFA, katuwang ang DMW-OA at iba pang ahensya para sa kaligtasan, agarang suporta at ligtas na pag-uwi ng ating mga kababayan.

**[00:30:20.920] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000747)  
Dahil ang repatriation ay hindi lang paglapag, kailangang may hatid pa-uwi at may tulong sa susunod na hakbang.

**[00:30:30.440] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000748)  
Sa Action Fund, naihahatid ang agarang tulong sa mga OFW na nasa distress. Ang tugon ay umaayon sa pangangailangan ng bawat OFW at pamilya.

**[00:30:43.260] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000749)  
Sa OWA Emergency Repatriation Fund, may suporta, bago, habang at pagkatapos ng pag-uwi.

**[00:30:54.900] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000750)  
Sabi ko ng ating Pangulo, hindi niyo po utang na loob ito sa amin, kundi bilang panukle at parangal namin sa inyong pagod at hirap sa abroad, kaya tuloy-tuloy pa ako ang servisyo natin sa inyong pagbabalik.

**[00:31:09.720] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000751)  
Sa dagat, may panganib ding walang pinipiling ruta. Sa bawat kaso, ipinagpapatuloy ang koordinasyon hanggang sa ligtas na pagbabalik.

**[00:31:20.400] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000752)  
Kasama rin sa proteksyon ang paghahanda para sa trabaho.

**[00:31:23.940] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000753)  
At buhay sa dagat, dahil ang pag-uwi ay iba-iba para sa bawat OFW. Para sa ilan, panahon ito para magsimulang muli.

**[00:31:34.460] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000754)  
Kaya pinagbubuklod ang mga ahensya ng pamahalaan. Ang reintegration ay hindi trabaho ng iisang tanggapan.

**[00:31:42.860] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000755)  
May kabuhayan para sa hanap buhay, kaalaman para sa bagong kakayahan, kalinga para sa kapakanan at kaagapan para maiugnay sa mga servisyo.

**[00:31:53.740] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000756)  
serbisyong kailangan. May puhunan, may skills, may pagkakataong makabalik sa profesyon o makapagtayo ng sariling kabuhayan. At hindi nawawala ang serbisyo sa pagitan ng mga yugto.

**[00:32:07.880] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000757)  
Naroon ito kung saan naroon ang OFW. Bago umalis, habang nasa abroad, sa oras ng krisis, sa pag-uwi, naroon ang pamahalaan upang maglingkod.

**[00:32:22.010] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000758)  
Sa ating pagkakaisa at pagtututunan.

**[00:32:24.980] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000759)  
Mapapanatili natin ang katatagan ng ating bansa. At maisusulong ang isang mas maunlad na kinabukasan para sa lahat sa ilalim ng isang bagong Pilipinas.

**[00:33:10.920] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000760)  
A couple of minutes, Madam Chair, to highlight. In accordance, if there's a review of our NEP, our Secretary mentioned po kung gaano kaimportante ang Emergency Repatriation Fund. Ito po'y sa OWA at ang Action Fund naman po sa DMW.

**[00:33:31.600] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000761)  
Sa NEP po namin, alos tatlong taon po na nasa 1.2 billion po ang Emergency Repatriation Fund po ng OWA.

**[00:33:41.800] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000762)  
Ito po ang pumupondo sa mga tickets, international, local. Patuwang ang Action Fund. At kasama rin po dyan po yung mga airport assistance, financial assistance, medical repatriation po para sa ating mga kababayan.

**[00:33:57.800] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000763)  
Everything related to repatriation. Ngunit sa NEP po namin ngayong taon, nabawasan po ito ng halos 300 million.

**[00:34:07.600] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000764)  
So, ito po ay nasa 900 million level, Madam Chair. Ngayon po na nasa gitna pa rin po tayo ng regional conflict at hindi lamang naman po sa gitnang silangan merong existing na nangyayaring gulo na apektado po ang ating mga OFWs.

**[00:34:27.880] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000765)  
Yan ho siguro para mailapit. Ang unang-una pong gusto naming ilapit po sa Kongreso.

**[00:34:36.480] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000766)  
Dahil napaka-importante po nito. Minsan po may taon na hindi ho ito mayuutilize. Nagpapasalamat ho tayo kung hindi ho ito mayuutilize.

**[00:34:47.260] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000767)  
Ibig sabihin, walang digmaan, walang pandemya, walang masamang nangyayari.

**[00:34:53.460] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000768)  
Ngunit may taon, katulad ng taon ito, na on the part of the ERF of OWA po, we are running already on the supplemental na hiningi po namin, additional 700.

**[00:35:06.320] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000770)  
Including po yung lahat po na-utilize na, nasa 1.7 billion na po dahil sa nangyaring digmaan sa rehiyon.

**[00:35:18.120] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000771)  
At ito po ay inaasahan namin na sana po ay mabigyan po kami ng additional budget sa Emergency Repatriation Fund.

**[00:35:28.480] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000772)  
Ito po ang cost of being ready ng gobyerno sa kung ano man po pwedeng mangyari sa ating mga OFWs.

**[00:35:35.820] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000773)  
Nasabi rin po ni,

**[00:35:38.680] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000774)  
na nabawasan po ang aming gas. Literally, we ran out of gas ang dating po.

**[00:35:47.260] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000775)  
Pwede ko po bang masabi rin kung bakit ito importante?

**[00:35:51.200] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000776)  
Kami po ay napaalalahanan ng COA na kailangan po ay itama natin or i-update natin ang sweldo ng mga JOs po.

**[00:36:01.980] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000777)  
Para po may konteksto, sa 1,600 po.

**[00:36:07.380] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000778)  
One power complement po ng OWA, 1,100 po ay JOs or over 60 percent, close to 70 percent.

**[00:36:16.520] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000779)  
Malaki pong tulong sila sa amin habang pong inaayos natin ang karagdagang plantilya positions.

**[00:36:23.480] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000780)  
Madam Chair, overseas plantilya positions of OWA, ibig sabihin po ang mga welfare officers namin, AS, 34 lang po.

**[00:36:32.640] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000781)  
Kaya humihingi po kami ng karagdagang plantilya positions.

**[00:36:37.120] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000782)  
Kasi ayon po sa batas, creating the Department of Migrant Workers, katuwang po kami ng mga labor attache sa abroad.

**[00:36:45.520] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000783)  
Sa masamang pala do, hindi yung lahat ng post, meron pa po kaming welfare officer.

**[00:36:50.180] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000784)  
Dahil po ay kinukuha na nga rin po namin sa central office at sa mga regional office, nagkakaroon po na rin ng pagkukulang ng tao.

**[00:36:59.240] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000785)  
Kaya napaka-importante po ng aming mga JOs.

**[00:37:03.320] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000786)  
At kung may ganun pong pag-a...

**[00:37:09.180] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000787)  
abiso ang COA na itaas at iayon sa current rates ang sweldo ng JO, supportado po ito ng management ng OWA.

**[00:37:17.580] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000788)  
At sana po, Madam Chair, mabigyan kami para naman po sulit na sulit ang kanila pong pagtatrabaho.

**[00:37:25.180] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000789)  
Sila po ay halos, ang operations po namin ng DMW ay 24-7 po, Madam Chair.

**[00:37:31.000] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000790)  
At ang pangatlo po, ay noong 1995 po, under Republic Act,

**[00:37:36.840] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000791)  
8042, ito po ang Migrant Workers Act.

**[00:37:39.600] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000792)  
Ito ho nagsimula ang lahat.

**[00:37:41.240] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000793)  
Meron ho ditong tinayo, ganun pa lang ho, siguro mga 30-31 years ago po.

**[00:37:48.840] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000794)  
Ang tawag po dito ay Congressional Migrant Workers Scholarship Program.

**[00:37:54.440] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000795)  
Ito po ay scholarship program para sa mga anak ng OFWs at mga OFWs mismo po.

**[00:38:01.060] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000796)  
Kung susumahin, halos 60,000 po per year at apat na taon po.

**[00:38:06.600] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000797)  
Yan po ang naibibigay na scholarship.

**[00:38:09.620] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000798)  
Ito po ay iba doon sa scholarship program ng OWA na pinupondohan ng trust fund o ibilang benepisyo.

**[00:38:17.320] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000799)  
Ngunit, since 1995 po, nung nagbigay ang Kongreso po ng 50 million noon,

**[00:38:25.080] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000800)  
hindi na po ito nabigyan ng pondo.

**[00:38:28.140] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000801)  
Gusto po sana namin din na hindi lamang po palakasin ito, lalo pong maramdaman ang ating mga kababayan.

**[00:38:35.880] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000802)  
Dahil po,

**[00:38:36.540] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000803)  
sa pananaw po namin, isa, if not, the major reason why our OFWs go abroad

**[00:38:42.240] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000804)  
ay para suportahan po ang pag-aaral ng kanilang mga anak.

**[00:38:45.900] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000805)  
So with this po, may I add as well, that when it comes to scholarships po,

**[00:38:51.160] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000806)  
ang OWA po ay meron ng 20,000 scholars na mga anak ng mga OFWs.

**[00:38:57.300] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000807)  
At sa suporta po ng aming mahal na Secretary at ng board,

**[00:39:01.100] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000808)  
magdadagdag pa po kami ng 10,000 na scholarship slots for this year alone.

**[00:39:06.960] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000809)  
Ito pong congressional scholarship, madadagdaga na naman po kung ito po'y malalagyan ng pondo at mapapalakas.

**[00:39:14.020] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000810)  
All in all po, alam po namin na limitado ang fiscal space.

**[00:39:19.580] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000811)  
Ngunit, ang aming pong hiling ay huwag naman hong kalimutan ang ating mga OFWs, lalong-lalo na po,

**[00:39:26.540] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000812)  
sila po ay araw-araw po yan, alam po ninyo, na humihingi ng tulong, yung iba po tulong ng repatriation,

**[00:39:34.920] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000813)  
yung iba po tulong ng livelihood.

**[00:39:37.120] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000814)  
Pagdating dito, yung iba po tulong para po sa kanilang pamilya at kanilang mga anak.

**[00:39:44.360] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000815)  
Panghuli na po, katuwang po ng DMW, alam nyo naman po na under the Action Fund,

**[00:39:50.760] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000816)  
ay may kinukuha po tayong mga legal retainers abroad.

**[00:39:55.140] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000817)  
Ito po yung mga lawyers na nagtatanggol po sa ating mga OFWs.

**[00:40:00.860] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000818)  
Gusto po sana namin na magkaroon din ng medical retainers or clinic retainers,

**[00:40:06.200] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000819)  
dahil ayon po sa aming datos, ay dumami na po yung health concerns or medical concerns ng ating mga kababayan.

**[00:40:15.080] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000820)  
And especially po, with special mention po, sa mga psychosocial counseling na kailangan ng ating mga kababayan,

**[00:40:25.020] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000821)  
lalong-lalo na po, yung pong mga distress natin, especially po, malapit sa puso namin ang isekhans,

**[00:40:33.380] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000822)  
ang ating pong mga domestic workers.

**[00:40:35.040] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000823)  
Na talaga naman po nga, kailangan po nating tulungan at bigyang pansin.

**[00:40:41.860] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000824)  
Yun lang po, Madam Chair, and we're open po, at baka meron pa pong mga tanong ang miyembro po ng committee.

**[00:40:47.780] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-000825)  
Thank you very much po, Madam Chair.

**[00:40:49.760] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000826)  
Thank you so much, OAA Administrator Patricia Yvonne Caunan.

**[00:40:55.960] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000827)  
Before we proceed with our interpolations, I would like to recognize our other members present here with us today.

**[00:41:02.780] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000828)  
We have Vice Chair Agustina Dominique.

**[00:41:06.080] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000829)  
Ditse Tina Pancho.

**[00:41:07.560] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000830)  
Vice Chair Niko Raul Daza.

**[00:41:11.180] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000831)  
And Vice Chair Maria Isabel Sagarbarria.

**[00:41:13.860] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000832)  
Good afternoon to everyone.

**[00:41:16.160] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000833)  
Now, we would like to open the floor for interpolations.

**[00:41:20.740] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000834)  
May I remind our distinguished colleagues that each member is given five minutes for questioning or interpolation,

**[00:41:28.340] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000835)  
excluding the response of the resource persons.

**[00:41:31.460] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000836)  
And when you hear the bell sound, it means...

**[00:41:34.520] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000837)  
Wow.

**[00:41:38.340] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000838)  
It means you only have 30 seconds to wind up with your questions.

**[00:41:47.500] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000839)  
And as part of a long-standing and respected tradition of the House of Representatives

**[00:41:52.260] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000840)  
to accord preferential treatment to the minority members of the House

**[00:41:57.140] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000841)  
and followed by the independent and majority members in an alternating manner.

**[00:42:02.580] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000842)  
So now, the Chair recognizes from the...

**[00:42:06.020] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000843)  
The minority.

**[00:42:08.880] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-000844)  
First, we have Representative Dada Ismula.

**[00:42:14.180] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000845)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Chair.

**[00:42:16.640] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000846)  
Assalamualaikum warahmatullahi wabarakatuhu.

**[00:42:19.640] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000847)  
Magandang hapon po sa inyong lahat, Secretary Hans, at sa ating Administrator ng OWWA.

**[00:42:26.440] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000848)  
Salamat po sa pagpunta ninyo and salamat po being our resource person.

**[00:42:31.180] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000849)  
At pagpapumanhin po in advance sa aking mga katanungan, ito po'y necessary.

**[00:42:35.980] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000850)  
Sige po.

**[00:42:36.940] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000851)  
Una po.

**[00:42:38.320] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000852)  
It has been a year since the DMW raised the minimum wage of migrant domestic workers

**[00:42:45.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000853)  
from 400 US dollar to 500 US dollar.

**[00:42:49.700] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000854)  
Ano ang ginagawa po ng DMW to ensure the full implementation of this standard

**[00:42:56.160] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000855)  
and ano rin po ang rate of compliance by countries of destination and recruitment agencies po?

**[00:43:04.640] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000856)  
Yes.

**[00:43:06.920] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000857)  
Magandang...

**[00:43:07.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000858)  
Araw pumulik, Congresswoman, Madam Chair.

**[00:43:13.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000859)  
Yes.

**[00:43:13.520] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000860)  
Ang pagtalaga po ng 500 US dollar wage for our domestic workers

**[00:43:20.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000861)  
was by virtue of the Department Order issued last August 2025, Department Order 25.

**[00:43:28.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000862)  
And since that time, ang sinagawa po natin ay bilang first phase ng ating implementation

**[00:43:35.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000863)  
is on a voluntary basis.

**[00:43:40.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000864)  
Since this will have implication on current contracts of OFWs,

**[00:43:45.580] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000865)  
ang ginawa po natin incentivization ang proseso na pinagdadaanan natin.

**[00:43:51.100] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000866)  
So we incentivize those who on their own provided domestic workers with a 500 US dollar salary per month.

**[00:44:01.240] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000867)  
Sa ngayon po, we have 17,375 job orders processed.

**[00:44:07.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000868)  
With a 500 US dollar salary for domestic workers.

**[00:44:12.720] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000869)  
And meron po tayong patuloy na pagpoprocess na mga job orders na ito.

**[00:44:21.660] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000870)  
Ito po ay supplemented with other measures within the Department Order.

**[00:44:27.420] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000871)  
For instance, we are braced to already implement yung tinatawag na video interview, yung know your employer.

**[00:44:35.440] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000872)  
Video interview.

**[00:44:37.480] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000873)  
Para po maisagawa yung mismong pagpapakilala ng domestic workers sa kanyang magiging future employer.

**[00:44:45.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000874)  
Para kilala na po niya.

**[00:44:46.880] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000875)  
So in this last quarter of 2026, we are braced to already fully implement itong video interview system.

**[00:44:56.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000876)  
Ang isa rin pong sinasabi,

**[00:44:58.940] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000877)  
ang isa rin pong sinasabi,

**[00:44:59.420] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000878)  
Isa rin pong sinasagawa natin yung upskilling.

**[00:45:00.920] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000879)  
Kailangan po kasing kasabayan ng pagpapatunay na may sapat na skillsets,

**[00:45:06.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000880)  
skills being the highest form of protection ng ating domestic workers.

**[00:45:11.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000881)  
At sa ngayon po, we are undergoing projects, training ng mga domestic workers,

**[00:45:17.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000882)  
turning them into other skillsets or skills options such as caregiving

**[00:45:23.280] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000883)  
and other types of skillsets in the restaurant sector, hospitality sector.

**[00:45:29.930] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000884)  
For instance, meron tayong ilan who are in caregiving from Israel

**[00:45:35.520] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000885)  
na na-i-place natin sa government-to-government hiring natin as hotel workers.

**[00:45:40.400] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000886)  
Some of them domestic workers also from the Middle East na naging hotel workers elsewhere,

**[00:45:47.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000887)  
most especially sa mga government-to-government hiring partners natin

**[00:45:51.660] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000888)  
sa Croatia.

**[00:45:55.720] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000889)  
Ngayon, the other thing we are doing is pagbibigay ng livelihood options para sa kanila.

**[00:46:02.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000890)  
Marami po na tayong nabihayaan ng livelihood capital from the National Reintegration Center

**[00:46:08.760] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000891)  
para hindi na sila mag-domestic work.

**[00:46:11.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000892)  
Dito na lang po nagtayo sila ng kanika nalang negosyo.

**[00:46:14.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000893)  
At ang dami na po nating success stories, most notably, yung nag-business plan competition tayo,

**[00:46:20.640] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000894)  
ang top three winners.

**[00:46:21.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000895)  
Ay mga puro former domestic workers.

**[00:46:25.500] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000896)  
So we are giving them options in terms of not just being domestic workers.

**[00:46:32.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000897)  
There's nothing wrong with being a domestic worker.

**[00:46:34.960] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000898)  
My own mother was a domestic worker when my dad lost his job as an OFW.

**[00:46:41.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000899)  
Pero kailangan lang meron options dapat ang ating mga kababayihang workers,

**[00:46:47.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000900)  
especially that they are not just caught in a recruitment trap of just being,

**[00:46:51.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000901)  
offered domestic work.

**[00:46:53.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000902)  
But the short answer to the question, ma'am, is we have 17,375 job orders processed

**[00:47:00.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000903)  
with 500 US dollars salary and counting.

**[00:47:04.820] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000904)  
Aaminin ko, medyo naantala ang implementasyon natin gawa nitong krisis sa Middle East.

**[00:47:10.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000905)  
Nag-focus tayo sa repatriation effort at assistance measures na binibigay sa atin

**[00:47:16.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000906)  
sa nanunumbalik na OFWs na, by the way, batay din sa datos ni Admin PY,

**[00:47:21.660] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000907)  
around 30 to 40 percent, kung maalala ko yung huling pagtingin ko sa datos,

**[00:47:26.700] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000908)  
ay mga domestic workers.

**[00:47:28.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000909)  
Yung nanunumbalik doon sa 12,000 na napauwi natin ay karamihan domestic workers.

**[00:47:33.300] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000910)  
So it is still a work in progress, Madam Chair, Congresswoman Dada,

**[00:47:37.960] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000911)  
and rest assured we will continue to implement the 500 US dollar salary

**[00:47:43.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000912)  
until it achieves a level of acceptability across the board

**[00:47:48.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000913)  
and then we can mandatorily impose the same.

**[00:47:52.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000914)  
Madam Chair, maraming salamat po.

**[00:47:55.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000915)  
Definitely understandable dahil sa naging conflict nga naman sa Middle East.

**[00:48:01.820] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000916)  
So nagagalak ako na marinig na it is still ongoing up to the present na madagdagan pa

**[00:48:07.680] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000917)  
at sana ay across the board na talaga na mataasan or ma-increase ang kanilang salary,

**[00:48:14.820] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000918)  
lalong-lalo na yung mga domestic workers po natin.

**[00:48:18.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000919)  
Dadako naman po ako, Madam Chair, sa on-action.

**[00:48:22.700] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000920)  
Ang agarang kalinga at saklolo para sa mga OFWs na nangangailangan o action fund

**[00:48:30.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000921)  
ay itinatag para mabigay ng timely and responsive services sa mga OFWs at kanilang mga pamilya.

**[00:48:38.540] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000922)  
Lalo na kapag sila ay nasa distress,

**[00:48:40.780] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000923)  
pero meron pong ilang reports mula sa ground na mabagal ang disbursement ng financial support under action fund.

**[00:48:48.040] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000924)  
Madalas daw po ay umaabot ito ng 3 to 6 months

**[00:48:51.820] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000925)  
kung ma-resolve ba.

**[00:48:52.640] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000926)  
May mga kaso pa ng OFWs na namatay sa chronic illness habang naghihintay pa rin ng agarang kalinga at saklolo.

**[00:49:02.640] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000927)  
Ano na po ang ginagawa para mabawasan ang red tape

**[00:49:06.200] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000928)  
at mapabilis ang disbursement at case management system ng DMW para sa action fund at sa iba pa nitong programa po?

**[00:49:15.880] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000929)  
Madam Chair, with the permission, I will make an initial comment.

**[00:49:21.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000930)  
But Yusek Feli Bay can also supplement the answer.

**[00:49:27.280] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000931)  
But at the outset, let me say na 80,000, as I reported, 80,000 na po ang nabigyan ng financial assistance.

**[00:49:35.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000932)  
However, tama po kayo, meron talagang hindi pa nabibigyan at meron na rin matagal-tagal nag-antay.

**[00:49:42.660] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000933)  
But the cause of this is once again, we don't use this as a common excuse

**[00:49:47.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000934)  
and I don't want to always get back to the crisis.

**[00:49:51.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000935)  
It's an excuse.

**[00:49:53.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000936)  
But ang nangyari po, yung 2 billion action fund natin na ubos po

**[00:49:58.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000937)  
because of our massive repatriation and financial assistance distribution effort nung krisis.

**[00:50:04.400] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000938)  
Nung krisis, the height of it in March, April, May, yung US-Israel-Iran war,

**[00:50:11.800] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000939)  
ay 20,000 po ang nabigyan natin ng action fund assistance on the ground.

**[00:50:16.100] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000940)  
Pero malamang po kung dadaang you're referring to those who are here,

**[00:50:21.880] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000941)  
who have returned.

**[00:50:22.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000942)  
So sa ngayon, naubos yung 2 billion, but as Yusef Niki mentioned,

**[00:50:27.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000943)  
no less than the President himself on June 23rd announced a 3 billion peso.

**[00:50:32.380] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000944)  
Hinigitan pa ni Pangulo yung 2026, yung 2025 budget.

**[00:50:37.800] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000945)  
If it was 2 billion in 2025 as set in the 2026 GAA,

**[00:50:43.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000946)  
sabi ni Pangulo, ang supplemental nyo, 3 billion.

**[00:50:46.820] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000947)  
So ongoing po ang distribution, rest assured.

**[00:50:52.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000948)  
Siyempre, mayroon lang usual COA procedures before we release,

**[00:50:58.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000949)  
documentation of the releases.

**[00:51:01.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000950)  
I wish we could just immediately unload the 3 billion that quickly,

**[00:51:06.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000951)  
but rest assured, the releases are ongoing.

**[00:51:10.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000952)  
We post this naman po, at least some of the releases on our Facebook page

**[00:51:14.820] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000953)  
just to make our people know that the releases are ongoing.

**[00:51:19.280] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000954)  
And rest assured po, makukompleto natin.

**[00:51:22.060] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000955)  
The 3 billion peso allocation of the President himself is definitely enough

**[00:51:26.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000956)  
to cover all the pending claims.

**[00:51:30.720] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000957)  
And rest assured, we will cover all the claims by the early 4th quarter, ma'am.

**[00:51:36.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000958)  
Yung pending at least.

**[00:51:37.760] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000959)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Chair and Mr. Secretary.

**[00:51:41.380] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000960)  
At least po, ito'y maibibigay pa rin sa kanila.

**[00:51:45.720] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000961)  
And naintindihan po natin na because of the crisis din,

**[00:51:48.960] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000962)  
at medyo late nga po yung mga downloads of funds.

**[00:51:51.720] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000963)  
Nga raw po ng mga iba-ibang departamento.

**[00:51:54.660] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000964)  
So, most likely, baka isa din po yun sa tinitignan na dahilan.

**[00:51:58.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000965)  
Kung bakit, nalilate din, no?

**[00:52:01.800] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000966)  
However, kaugnay nga po ng Action Fund,

**[00:52:04.500] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000967)  
maari ko bang matanong ang ating ginoong sekretary na

**[00:52:09.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000968)  
magkukumit ba po ang DMW na amyendahan ang Department Order 02-2025

**[00:52:16.760] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000969)  
o omnibus guidelines on the implementation of the Action Fund

**[00:52:21.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000970)  
para malinaw na ma-define ang ideal disbursement rate po.

**[00:52:27.840] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000971)  
Yes, right now, ang disbursement rates natin,

**[00:52:33.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000972)  
generally speaking, are 50,000 for the distressed cases,

**[00:52:39.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000973)  
75,000 kapag merong medical situation,

**[00:52:43.820] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000974)  
and 100,000 kapag nasawi ang ating OFW sa kanasamaang palad.

**[00:52:50.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000975)  
But yes, we are reviewing these rates kasi,

**[00:52:52.800] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000976)  
meron din tayong quick action na tinatawag po eh.

**[00:52:57.310] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000977)  
So, by way of example, pag may dumating na OFW,

**[00:53:00.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000978)  
at ang case niya is not as major as the others,

**[00:53:06.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000979)  
pero kailangan niya ng pamasahe, pauwi,

**[00:53:08.960] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000980)  
kailangan niya ng pantawid kahit papano,

**[00:53:11.800] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000981)  
may matutustos siya sa, halimbawa, bilang pocket money niya pauwi ng probinsya,

**[00:53:19.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000982)  
then we have that quick action.

**[00:53:22.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000983)  
So, we have a window where we provide 10,000 to around 5,000 to 10,000 to 30,000,

**[00:53:29.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000984)  
depende dun sa need.

**[00:53:30.280] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000985)  
Dumbawa, abutan ng pamasahe, di 5,000, 2,000.

**[00:53:33.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000986)  
So, but you're right, Mama, we need to rationalize and make sure that there is a definite schedule

**[00:53:40.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000987)  
para malinaw po sa taong bayan kung ano yung saan na pupunta,

**[00:53:44.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000988)  
saan na lalagay yung kanilang sitwasyon.

**[00:53:47.660] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000989)  
But there is another special rate which is around 30,000 pesos for the,

**[00:53:52.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000990)  
traffic victims.

**[00:53:56.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000991)  
Ito po yung mga either hindi nakaalis, illegal recruitment victims,

**[00:54:01.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000992)  
or sila po ina-uwi, halimbawa, dun sa scam hub sa Southeast Asia.

**[00:54:07.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000993)  
So, we have pegged the amount for them at 30,000 pesos.

**[00:54:13.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000994)  
So, meron pong ganong klaseng differentiation lang tayo.

**[00:54:16.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000995)  
And you're right, we are reviewing the same, Madam Congresswoman,

**[00:54:20.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-000996)  
and we'll issue the amendatory guidelines soon.

**[00:54:23.320] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000997)  
Salamat po, Madam Chair, Mr. Secretary.

**[00:54:26.460] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000998)  
Dada ko naman po ako sa repatriation.

**[00:54:29.900] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000999)  
Base sa naging karanasan natin sa emergency repatriations dahil sa US-Iran conflict,

**[00:54:36.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001000)  
ano po kaya ang assessment ng DMW patungkol po dito?

**[00:54:40.840] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001001)  
Yes, ang assessment po natin, unang-una, may kahandaan po tayo at the outset

**[00:54:51.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001002)  
kasi si Presidente po.

**[00:54:52.720] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001003)  
Unang-una niyang sinabi, day zero, kumbaga, Feb 28 kasi pumutok yung sigalot.

**[00:54:59.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001004)  
May clear instructions na si Pangulo sa amin ni Sec. Tes. Lazaro ng DFA

**[00:55:03.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001005)  
to provide the welfare services for our OFWs.

**[00:55:08.200] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001006)  
At kami ni Admin PY, ang unang-unang naming naisip nung lumabas itong pumutok itong sigalot

**[00:55:14.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001007)  
is yung mga masastranded gawa nung nasara yung airspace sa Gulf States, Dubai, Qatar, etc.

**[00:55:22.520] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001008)  
So kami ni Admin PY, nagtungo kagad kami sa airport.

**[00:55:25.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001009)  
Dahil alam namin magkakaroon ng embudo dun sa airport.

**[00:55:29.480] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001010)  
Dahil na-cancel ang flights, hindi na tuloy, and therefore may mga nasa labas ng airport

**[00:55:35.300] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001011)  
naging hintay ng tulong.

**[00:55:36.480] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001012)  
So binigyan na ho namin ng agarang tulong and Admin PY could provide.

**[00:55:39.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001013)  
May data rin ho sila dyan.

**[00:55:42.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001014)  
So that's one, the mechanisms, the systems.

**[00:55:48.520] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001015)  
On that particular day also, we met virtually all the ambassadors.

**[00:55:53.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001016)  
Sec. Tes. Lazaro and all the ambassadors on the ground with the labor attachés.

**[00:55:58.440] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001017)  
Nagpulong na po kami lahat and activated our crisis manuals, crisis management systems.

**[00:56:03.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001018)  
Existing na po ito. Marami nagtatanong kung meron bang ganito.

**[00:56:09.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001019)
