Meron na po talaga, nakaabang na ito. So it's all a matter of activating these systems.

**[00:56:14.280] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001020)  
And then, one key learning also is not all conflicts have the same size, so to speak.

**[00:56:22.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001021)  
Ibig sabihin na hindi one size fits all ang conflict.

**[00:56:26.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001022)  
And this particular conflict is most, is kumbaga the best example of this principle.

**[00:56:35.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001023)  
Kasi never pa ho nangyari na pitong bansa ang involved.

**[00:56:39.480] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001024)  
Usually, when it's Israel-Lebanon, dalawang bansa lang po yan.

**[00:56:43.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001025)  
Russia-Ukraine, dalawang bansa.

**[00:56:45.430] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001026)  
But ito, covering yung theater of military operations, covered seven countries.

**[00:56:50.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001027)  
And so that was the challenge.

**[00:56:53.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001028)  
And we learned now, now, it's in our rule book, it's in our manuals, what to do kapag ganong karaming bansa

**[00:57:02.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001029)  
ang literally involved sa drone attacks, missile attacks, dahil nandun nga rin po ang kababayan natin.

**[00:57:08.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001030)  
So there was a learning experience, but at the same time, we needed to learn fast and apply our systems fast.

**[00:57:17.300] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001031)  
So ang resulta nga po ay 12,000 ang nakauwi.

**[00:57:22.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001032)  
12,000 na hindi nakauwi, pero nabigyan ng financial assistance.

**[00:57:28.520] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001033)  
And the other learning is resources.

**[00:57:32.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001034)  
So I'll go back to my point, ang point namin ni Admin PY.

**[00:57:36.380] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001035)  
Talagang we need to have already, hindi, masasabi ko talagang hindi, I think Admin PY mentioned this already,

**[00:57:46.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001036)  
hindi po pwede talagang eksakto ang bit-bit namin sa bulsa namin.

**[00:57:51.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001037)  
At the beginning of the year or at any given time, because anything can happen, as we all know.

**[00:57:58.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001038)  
Unfortunately, the geopolitical situation is unpredictable.

**[00:58:04.280] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001039)  
And so we must have the necessary resources, because we can and do mobilize such resources,

**[00:58:12.460] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001040)  
and not just financial, I'm talking of material and human resource, when the time comes.

**[00:58:17.820] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001041)  
Handa naman po tayo, but we just really need the proper resources in our hands.

**[00:58:23.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001042)  
I agree with that, Madam Chair.

**[00:58:25.640] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001043)  
At follow-up na lang po dyan, Madam Chair, Mr. Secretary,

**[00:58:29.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001044)  
sa tingin niyo po ba may mga kailangang baguhin sa allocation, sa guidelines,

**[00:58:36.620] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001045)  
o kaya sa mga case management patungkol po sa pagiging mas responsive natin

**[00:58:43.840] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001046)  
at para mas maging sufficient din po ang pondo sa mga ganitong biglaang sitwasyon po?

**[00:58:52.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001047)  
I think, again,

**[00:58:54.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001048)  
more than anything, yung financial resources, capabilities namin.

**[00:59:01.760] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001049)  
Of course, yung human resources, dapat nakaabang, but that too is a function of our systems,

**[00:59:09.300] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001050)  
yung hiring processes and systems namin.

**[00:59:12.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001051)  
It's a good thing that before this conflict started, na-approve ng DBM on our part,

**[00:59:18.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001052)  
yung labing-labang additional items dun sa fund administration, action fund administration side.

**[00:59:24.840] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001053)  
So nagpapasalamat kami kay Presidente sa DBM kasi nagkaroon ng fund management system yung aming action fund

**[00:59:31.800] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001054)  
before this all happened. So that was in place.

**[00:59:36.760] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001055)  
And there were people who were dati kasi parang ad hoc task force lang,

**[00:59:40.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001056)  
but now there are really people and systems governing the action fund.

**[00:59:45.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001057)  
Parang mini OWA, if I may say so, within the DMW that governs.

**[00:59:49.960] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001058)  
Kung trust fund ng OWA, kami naman governing the action fund.

**[00:59:55.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001059)  
But again, I hate to be repetitive.

**[00:59:58.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001060)  
But really, it's a matter of having the right resources.

**[01:00:03.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001061)  
Kasi wala nang tatalo pa sa saying yes at the right moment.

**[01:00:09.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001062)  
Kesa sa ang sagot namin sa tao, ay pasensya na, wala kaming pondo.

**[01:00:16.840] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001063)  
And we always need to be ready in terms of our financial systems.

**[01:00:23.660] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001064)  
Now let me also add, na kami naman po are also in the process of enhancing our ISO systems.

**[01:00:29.660] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001065)  
So on the part of the DMW and the OWA, we are geared to sharpen our ISO systems

**[01:00:35.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001066)  
para mabilis ang disbursement ng pondo, selection of our human resources,

**[01:00:41.580] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001067)  
mobilization of our human resources when the time comes.

**[01:00:46.640] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001068)  
So right now, we're beefing up our human resources at the posts.

**[01:00:50.880] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001069)  
At dinadagdagan pa nga namin.

**[01:00:53.580] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001070)  
Ang aming mga posts, for instance, we recently opened our 43rd office in Cambodia.

**[01:01:01.560] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001071)  
So ngayon, may tao na tayo nakaposition doon together working with our DFA

**[01:01:05.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001072)  
to respond to distress calls from our OFWs who are victimized by the scam hubs

**[01:01:12.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001073)  
or employed, unfortunately, by the scam hubs.

**[01:01:16.520] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001074)  
So this is another learning that we've had.

**[01:01:20.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001075)  
It's always good to preposition our people.

**[01:01:24.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001076)  
And that will probably be my final point.

**[01:01:27.720] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001077)  
Pagdating ng emergency, etong huling sigalot,

**[01:01:31.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001078)  
admin PY and I were able to preposition our people sa fly-out points.

**[01:01:36.440] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001079)  
Not necessarily doon sa seven countries.

**[01:01:38.880] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001080)  
Sa labas nito.

**[01:01:39.840] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001081)  
Para nakaabang nasa border, sundo, fly out.

**[01:01:43.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001082)  
So ganon kahalaga rin yung resource, human and financial.

**[01:01:47.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001083)  
Because dapat makabili kami kagad ng ticket, makabuo kami kagad ng augmentation teams

**[01:01:52.660] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001084)  
para nakapreposition.

**[01:01:54.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001085)  
Sa countries kung saan sasalubungin na yung lumalabas ng mga kababayan natin sa border.

**[01:02:00.060] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001086)  
So yun po.

**[01:02:01.240] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001087)  
And I'm pretty sure, Madam Chair, Mr. Secretary, na kapag bumibili kayo ng ticket on the spot,

**[01:02:07.840] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001088)  
napakamahal po ng mga ticket na ito.

**[01:02:10.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001089)  
Yes. At hindi naman ho namin pwede sabihin sa inyo,

**[01:02:13.280] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001090)  
pasensya ho, hindi kami nakabili ng ticket kasi mahal.

**[01:02:19.400] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001091)  
That is true.

**[01:02:19.920] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001092)  
Siguro pagagalitan nyo kami pag yan ang explanation naming sasabihin.

**[01:02:23.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001093)  
Hindi kami umabot ng 12,000 kasi mahal ang ticket.

**[01:02:26.700] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001094)  
Pero the good news is, umabot kami ng 12,000 kasi we just basically brought people home.

**[01:02:31.520] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001095)  
That was the main goal under the directive of the President.

**[01:02:35.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001096)  
And we note, Madam Chair, Mr. Secretary,

**[01:02:40.060] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001097)  
present po si Secretary Kakdak noong nagkaroon kami ng konsultasyon sa ating mga ilang mga OFW.

**[01:02:49.860] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001098)  
Nagpapasalamat kami doon kay Secretary Hans.

**[01:02:52.360] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001099)  
Pero gusto lang.

**[01:02:54.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001100)  
Kailangan po namin na put into record na hindi lahat ng mga kababayan natin po abroad,

**[01:02:58.700] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001101)  
which is na-mention naman po ni Secretary Hans kanina,

**[01:03:01.540] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001102)  
ay gustong magpa-repatriate.

**[01:03:03.820] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001103)  
May mga ilang mga OFW po na nagre-request lang ng support for shelter

**[01:03:08.820] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001104)  
and other protective programs during the conflict.

**[01:03:11.940] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001105)  
We also agree with the OAPY statement earlier,

**[01:03:17.420] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001106)  
and that's why this representation would like to manifest that we support,

**[01:03:23.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001107)  
an increased budget for the Department of Migrant Workers and OWA.

**[01:03:27.920] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001108)  
Ngayon pa na may global conflict sa Middle East, ngayon pa talaga kayo binawasan.

**[01:03:33.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001109)  
Kung saan maraming mga OFW, dapat mas palakasin at palawakin pa natin

**[01:03:39.620] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001110)  
ang mga programang mag-aalaga sa ating mga kababayan abroad.

**[01:03:43.620] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001111)  
Lalo pa sila po ang isa sa mga nangungunang nagpapatakbo sa ating ekonomiya.

**[01:03:48.860] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001112)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Chair.

**[01:03:50.900] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001113)  
Maraming salamat po to the DMW family.

**[01:03:57.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001114)  
Yes, go ahead.

**[01:04:23.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001115)  
Take-home pay.

**[01:04:25.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001116)  
So nagbigay tayo ng financial assistance.

**[01:04:27.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001117)  
20,000 po yung naabutan natin noon.

**[01:04:30.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001118)  
And with admin PY, yung grocery food pack.

**[01:04:34.240] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001119)  
So hindi lang financial, kundi basic food and shelter, and then—

**[01:04:45.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001120)  
Temporary accommodation.

**[01:04:46.440] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001121)  
Temporary accommodation, tama, shelters that we provide.

**[01:04:49.700] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001122)  
Kasi nga po, ayaw nila umuwi.

**[01:04:55.240] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001123)  
Temporary accommodation.

**[01:04:56.240] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001124)  
Pero gusto muni nilang manirahan sa isang lugar kung saan they feel safe.

**[01:05:00.420] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001125)  
And minsan pa, pinupuntahan pa na.

**[01:05:02.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001126)  
At tapos kung may host country designated shelter, katulad ng Bahrain at Israel,

**[01:05:08.800] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001127)  
pinupuntahan ho namin sila doon.

**[01:05:11.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001128)  
Pinupuntahan namin sila doon sa government, host country government designated shelter

**[01:05:15.280] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001129)  
at inaabutan din ng tulong.

**[01:05:19.420] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001130)  
So hindi ibig sabihin may host country government designated shelter,

**[01:05:24.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001131)  
hinahayaan na lang namin sila.

**[01:05:25.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001132)  
Pinupuntahan pa ho namin sila doon at binibigyan pa ng basic needs.

**[01:05:31.580] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001133)  
Madam Chair, with your permission, just to add.

**[01:05:38.500] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001134)  
Yes, please proceed.

**[01:05:42.280] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001135)  
Gusto ko lang pong klaruhin na yung aming emergency at repatriation assistance ng DMW at ng OWA,

**[01:05:46.060] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001136)  
hindi lang po kami taga-bili ng ticket, Madam Chair.

**[01:05:50.660] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001137)  
Yung ating pong rapid response team, yung minensyon po ni Secretary.

**[01:05:54.380] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001138)  
Yung rapid response team, yung minensyon po ni sekretary.

**[01:05:55.000] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001139)  
Feb 28 po nagsimula ang gulo.

**[01:05:59.060] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001140)  
March 2 pa lang po nakapuesto na po.

**[01:06:01.240] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001141)  
Ngayon po siguro pwede nang sabihin.

**[01:06:03.180] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001142)  
Sa border ng Egypt, sa Oman, sa Bahrain po, sa Kuwait, sa Qatar, sa Saudi, sa UAE,

**[01:06:14.040] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001143)  
nagpadala po tayo ng mga tao po dito at literal po,

**[01:06:20.220] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001144)  
tinutulungan pong makatawid from one border to the next.

**[01:06:24.000] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001145)  
Yung ating pong mga flights out of Oman, meron po doon kasama ang mga kababayan natin from UAE.

**[01:06:31.820] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001146)  
Out of Riyadh, mga kababayan po natin from Kuwait, itinawid po natin.

**[01:06:37.200] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001147)  
Bahrain, Qatar, at syempre sa eastern region rin po ng Saudi.

**[01:06:43.740] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001148)  
Ito pong rapid response team po natin, of course, adopting a one country,

**[01:06:49.180] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001149)  
team approach with the DFA.

**[01:06:52.480] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001150)  
Doon na po makikita yung iba't ibang assistance.

**[01:06:55.780] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001151)  
Dito po napupunta yung pondo po.

**[01:06:58.780] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001152)  
Yung pong sa hotel nila, yung pong sa transportation, sa meals po nila,

**[01:07:03.960] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001153)  
hanggang po sa air tickets papuntang Maynila,

**[01:07:09.300] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001154)  
hanggang pong ticket po nila pauwing probinsya ay tinutulungan din po natin.

**[01:07:14.800] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001155)  
And on top of it, of course, Madam Chair,

**[01:07:17.460] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001156)  
ang financial assistance po na ibinibigay po natin ng DMW at saka ng OWA.

**[01:07:23.140] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001157)  
Napaka-importante po nitong RRT, yung sinasabi po ni SEC na personnel,

**[01:07:27.860] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001158)  
dahil po, kailangan pong i-augment yung ating pong mga teams abroad pag meron pong emergency.

**[01:07:35.460] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001159)  
So siguro, yung po rin ang maidagdag po, matingnan natin kung paano po mas mapalakas

**[01:07:40.480] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001160)  
yung ating pong personnel abroad kasi po ay sila po ang talagang nag-aaral.

**[01:07:47.240] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001161)  
Frontliners po, frontliners, lalong-lalo na po pag may emergency.

**[01:07:51.120] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001162)  
Sila rin po naka-experience nung mga, kami po ni SEC, nung pumunta po kami ng Romania recently SEC,

**[01:07:58.840] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001163)  
dahil po doon sa naging conflict din po that were faced by RC Ferrer sa Black Sea,

**[01:08:08.120] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001164)  
nakakatanggap din po kami nung emergency warning for drone attacks.

**[01:08:13.760] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001165)  
Ganyan po ka-totoo ang emergency warning.

**[01:08:18.300] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001166)  
Ang nangyayari at yan po ay regular na na-experience.

**[01:08:20.240] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001167)  
Hindi lang po ng ating mga OFWs, pati po ang ating mga deployed personnel po.

**[01:08:25.880] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001168)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Chair.

**[01:08:29.360] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-001169)  
Thank you, Honorable Dadah Ismula.

**[01:08:32.280] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-001170)  
Now, from the majority, the Chair now recognizes Representative Mica Gonzales.

**[01:08:40.840] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001171)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:08:43.360] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001172)  
Thank you, Sec. Hans, for coming.

**[01:08:45.780] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001173)  
And also the OWWA family and the DMW family.

**[01:08:49.180] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001174)  
I just have a few questions on the reintegration program.

**[01:08:54.700] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001175)  
Because from the previous hearing sa People's Center, I asked whether the people who asked for a repatriation,

**[01:09:03.060] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001176)  
do they have a chance to go back again to work?

**[01:09:06.300] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001177)  
Kasi siyempre, kaya nga sila nag-abroad kasi gusto nila magtrabaho abroad.

**[01:09:09.140] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001178)  
But given the situation, they cannot continue working there because of the conflict.

**[01:09:15.040] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001179)  
So I would like to ask on the update on the reintegration program doon po sa umuwi from Middle East to the Philippines.

**[01:09:26.560] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001180)  
Yes po.

**[01:09:28.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001181)  
And salamat po sa tanong.

**[01:09:30.500] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001182)  
And yes, this is a foremost directive and concern also of no less than the President.

**[01:09:37.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001183)  
So ang sinagawa po natin, unang-unang step po doon is the profiling of those who had returned.

**[01:09:43.880] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001184)  
So we need to know who they are, what their skill sets are.

**[01:09:47.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001185)  
And whether or not they have an intent to return.

**[01:09:50.440] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001186)  
A vast majority, based on the profile tools, yung digital profiling na ginawa ng OWWA at ng DMW, want to stay.

**[01:10:02.460] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001187)  
So dito po sa vast majority who want to stay, of the 12,000 po, roughly mga 9,000 were OFWs.

**[01:10:11.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001188)  
3,000 of the 12,000 were dependents of OFWs.

**[01:10:16.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001189)  
Mga anak po, kasi OFWs naman usually will bring their children or dependents and they will not go home without their children and dependents.

**[01:10:23.240] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001190)  
So of the 9,000, nagkaroon po tayo ng intervention sa kanila.

**[01:10:28.940] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001191)  
We knew who they were coming in.

**[01:10:31.760] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001192)  
And nag-conduct po tayo ng around nearly 20 National Reintegration Network Job, Livelihood, and Skills Fairs.

**[01:10:43.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001193)  
And dito po, una, aside from the job offers na binigay, nagkaroon din tayo ng active labor market interventions.

**[01:10:53.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001194)  
Ibig sabihin, based on their profiles, nalaman natin ang skill sets nila at hinanapan natin ng corresponding job orders overseas.

**[01:11:05.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001195)  
Overseas po ito, wala pa dito yung DOLE side, yung local.

**[01:11:08.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001196)  
So based on this framework, we had sent letters.

**[01:11:12.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001197)  
Emails to the returning OFWs and told them, okay, you are a barista in UAE.

**[01:11:20.060] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001198)  
Ito ang mga job orders natin sa Asia and Europe and Americas pertaining to the job of being a barista.

**[01:11:29.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001199)  
This is an ongoing process.

**[01:11:31.380] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001200)  
So we had sent them emails and the ongoing process po ito, admittedly, hindi instant ang acceptance nila.

**[01:11:39.200] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001201)  
But meron na po mga napaprocess for possible work overseas.

**[01:11:44.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001202)  
For those who wish to establish businesses, ang livelihood interventions naman po natin are through, of course, the DMW and OWWA reintegration funds,

**[01:11:58.500] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001203)  
but also DTI, Department of Trade and Industry, a Small Business Corporation, have their own loan facility, especially for returning women workers through Sec. Cris Roque.

**[01:12:08.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001205)  
Sec Kiko Laurel also has his own Department of Agriculture, Agribusiness Interventions, Startup Capital.

**[01:12:18.420] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001206)  
Sec. Dita of DOT, meron ding mga tourism-related businesses offered, among others.

**[01:12:27.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001207)  
DOST meron din, science and technology related.

**[01:12:30.480] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001208)  
Now, sa skills enhancement, nandun din si Sec Kiko Benitez, DG Kiko Benitez at ang TESDA.

**[01:12:38.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001209)  
So, sa mga national reintegration fairs po namin, nandun yung mga agencies na binanggit ko.

**[01:12:43.880] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001210)  
Tapos, nandun yung TESDA to offer skills-related certificates, training vouchers for the returnees.

**[01:12:56.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001211)  
Tapos, meron pa ho itong 160 pesos stipend. Libre yung training, tapos may stipend pa yan daily pag natanggap sila.

**[01:13:04.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001212)  
By way of example, galing kami sa Naga.

**[01:13:07.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001213)  
With Mayor Leni.

**[01:13:09.840] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001214)  
At doon, merong mga 25 na binigyan ng certificate to start their six-month AI course, AI-related course with STI.

**[01:13:23.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001215)  
So, we partnered with STI.

**[01:13:25.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001216)  
So that’s just an example of doon naman sa skills-related part.

**[01:13:30.700] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001217)  
Now, there would be around 30–40% in the category that you mentioned, Madam Congresswoman.

**[01:13:38.920] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001218)  
Yung mga gustong bumalik.

**[01:13:40.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001219)  
Ito naman po, meron na po rin pong nakabalik.

**[01:13:44.060] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001220)  
Kasi it takes six months from the time they leave UAE, for instance.

**[01:13:50.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001221)  
Buhay pa for six months yung kanilang valid employment visa.

**[01:13:53.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001222)  
And they can just easily return to re-assume their jobs.

**[01:13:58.820] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001223)  
Some were employed in businesses na talagang tumigil.

**[01:14:03.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001224)  
So, they are waiting for their businesses to resume.

**[01:14:07.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001226)  
But some were able to already come back, to go back to the work that they have.

**[01:14:12.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001227)  
For instance, mga hotel workers at mga office workers.

**[01:14:16.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001228)  
Meron naman po sila rin nabalikan ng mga trabaho.

**[01:14:21.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001229)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:14:22.500] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001230)  
So, from what I understood, kapag po yung isang OFW yun na repatriate from the UAE back to the Philippines,

**[01:14:30.580] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001231)  
you offer two options.

**[01:14:32.400] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001232)  
Whether they go back abroad, it may be Asia, it may be the Middle East or Europe,

**[01:14:37.460] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001233)  
or they can grow locally.

**[01:14:40.660] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001234)  
Right?

**[01:14:41.300] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001235)  
So, I think okay.

**[01:14:43.060] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001236)  
That's the first question po.

**[01:14:44.900] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001237)  
The second question is about OFW Hospital.

**[01:14:48.760] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001238)  
Because I represent po kasi third district of Pampanga and I cover San Fernando City, no?

**[01:14:53.940] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001239)  
So, I would like to ask because the last time I visited there,

**[01:14:58.380] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001253)  
there was a problem on the procurement.

**[01:15:01.540] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001254)  
So, ang tagal ng process.

**[01:15:04.180] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001255)  
Yung mga pasyente, siyempre they cannot wait.

**[01:15:07.120] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001256)  
So, ang daming reklamang dumadaan that time.

**[01:15:10.060] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001257)  
So, I would like to ask from your office, Sec. Hans, or anyone from DMW,

**[01:15:17.460] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001258)  
ano na po ang status ng procurement process?

**[01:15:20.400] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001259)  
And number two, how’s the plantilla positions in OFW Hospital?

**[01:15:27.200] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001260)  
Thank you.

**[01:15:30.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001261)  
Okay po, ma’am.

**[01:15:32.100] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001262)  
Meron tayong, on average, ongoing po yung procurement po at least on two major items.

**[01:15:41.280] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001263)  
One is yung sa medical supplies and one is on yung, should I say, the hard equipment,

**[01:15:47.640] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001264)  
yung mga medical machines such as CAT scans.

**[01:15:52.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001265)  
And other types, X-rays, etc.

**[01:15:55.580] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001266)  
So, doon po sa medical supplies, we had substantially resolved the problem.

**[01:16:03.200] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001267)  
There used to be pagkukulang-kakulangan ng mga gamot sa pharmacy.

**[01:16:08.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001268)  
But because we had partnered with a Philpharma group,

**[01:16:18.060] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001269)  
Sina Usec. Kim, which is an adjunct,

**[01:16:22.580] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001270)  
of the DTI, yung pharma tie-up ng DTI,

**[01:16:27.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001271)  
ay na-resolve na po ito kasi nagkaroon na ng steady delivery of medical supplies.

**[01:16:32.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001272)  
So the challenge is mainly doon sa equipment.

**[01:16:38.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001273)  
And one key development here is it took quite a while,

**[01:16:45.760] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001274)  
But finally may sarili nang BAC, Bids and Awards Committee, ang ospital.

**[01:16:50.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001275)  
For a while, it was centralized with the DMW.

**[01:16:54.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001276)  
But the problem there kasi is we needed to wait for the right moment

**[01:16:59.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001277)  
na na-position na yung mga regular employees that had been filled up.

**[01:17:07.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001278)  
And the number we have is around 198, regular positions that we had filled up.

**[01:17:15.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001279)  
Hinintay po muna namin sila mag-settle down upon hiring

**[01:17:18.720] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001280)  
para sila magkaroon na ng kumpiyansa at sistema sa kanilang procurement.

**[01:17:24.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001281)  
So, we had turned over procurement duties to the hospital.

**[01:17:28.720] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001282)  
And this will go a long way.

**[01:17:30.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001283)  
kasi mas, kumbaga obviously, mas autonomous sila at mas mabilis na silang umaksyon

**[01:17:35.580] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001284)  
And ongoing po sa ngayon,

**[01:17:39.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001285)  
ang review and posting,

**[01:17:41.660] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001286)  
41.4% ongoing ang procurement,

**[01:17:47.440] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001287)  
and 35.6% fully served.

**[01:17:51.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001288)  
So work in progress.

**[01:17:52.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001291)  
yung 41.4% and the rest are still in the preliminary process.

**[01:18:01.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001292)  
hindi po... kasi iba-iba naman ang timelines.

**[01:18:05.580] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001293)  
Still under process by the BAC.

**[01:18:07.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001294)  
But at release, 77% of our procurement items had been acted upon.

**[01:18:16.660] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001295)  
And the rest still under-processed yung paperwork sa BAC.

**[01:18:20.280] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001296)  
But rest assured, ma'am,

**[01:18:22.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001297)  
Bibilisan po natin ito.

**[01:18:24.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001298)  
You may be referring to the recent problem we had with our CAT scan.

**[01:18:29.940] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001299)  
But this had already been resolved.

**[01:18:32.100] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001300)  
We even sought the intervention of the Japanese authorities.

**[01:18:35.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001301)  
Kasi galing sa Japan yung makina, yung equipment.

**[01:18:39.060] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001302)  
So, this had been recently resolved.

**[01:18:42.560] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001303)  
And the key equipment part is now braced for delivery.

**[01:18:49.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001304)  
And we will...

**[01:18:50.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001305)  
Ah, delivered na?

**[01:18:51.800] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001307)  
Okay.

**[01:18:53.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001308)  
So we are now braced to testing na

**[01:18:56.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001309)  
and then we will now operate the CAT scan machine once again.

**[01:19:00.520] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001310)  
So, rest assured, ma'am.

**[01:19:02.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001311)  
And then, merong MRI machine that is up for procurement.

**[01:19:07.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001312)  
With your permission, can I ask you, Sec. Nikki, so I don’t keep looking at her, to respond

**[01:19:16.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001313)  
to the MRI and other concerns.

**[01:19:21.520] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001314)  
With the permission of the Secretary, po.

**[01:19:24.080] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001318)  
Tama po.

**[01:19:24.940] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001319)  
Yung mga equipment po, critical equipment, have already been delivered.

**[01:19:28.520] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001320)  
And under procurement po ngayon yung mga gagamitin din po ng ating cancer care facility

**[01:19:33.820] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001321)  
which hopefully by first quarter or second quarter of 2027 ma-operationalize na rin po natin.

**[01:19:42.840] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001322)  
And on top of the plantilla positions mentioned earlier by the Secretary, we were given

**[01:19:48.900] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001323)  
additional 110 plantilla positions.

**[01:19:51.580] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001324)  
So that makes it around

**[01:19:53.020] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001326)  
308 permanent plantilla positions under level 2 accreditation from the Department of Health.

**[01:20:01.980] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001327)  
And we continuously hire medical and administrative staff at the moment.

**[01:20:09.700] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001328)  
Thank you. Thank you for those answers.

**[01:20:12.820] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001329)  
Kasi po, I'm very into the OFW Hospital because I filed a bill

**[01:20:17.860] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001330)  
at approved na po ito sa Committee on Health to make it level 3.

**[01:20:22.020] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001331)  
Kaya I'm very much concerned about OFW Hospital.

**[01:20:25.140] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001332)  
But on that note, of course, I fully support the manifestation of Kong Dadah to increase our budget.

**[01:20:32.380] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001333)  
Kasi yung mga nandito nga sa Pinas, atat tayong tulungan, di ba?

**[01:20:37.360] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001334)  
What more yung mga nasa abroad na malayo sa pamilya.

**[01:20:40.400] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001335)  
So I think that's a point na kailangan nating make sure na they are catered for, they cared for,

**[01:20:49.320] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001336)  
and most importantly, dapat naramdaman nila.

**[01:20:51.360] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-001338)  
So that's all, Madam Chair. Thank you very much.

**[01:20:55.140] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-001339)  
Thank you, Honorable Mica Gonzales.

**[01:20:58.100] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-001340)  
Now, from the minority, the Chair recognizes Honorable Robert Nazal.

**[01:21:05.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001341)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:21:06.780] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001342)  
Good afternoon to the DMW.

**[01:21:09.280] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001343)  
Good afternoon, Secretary Cacdac.

**[01:21:11.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001344)  
And good afternoon to the DMW family.

**[01:21:15.920] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001345)  
Madam Chair, I refer to the August 25, 2026 letter of...

**[01:21:22.140] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001346)  
OWWA Administrator, Attorney Patricia Yvonne Caunan, requesting our support

**[01:21:27.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001347)  
for an additional 2 billion in OWWA's fiscal year 2027 budget.

**[01:21:34.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001348)  
Madam Chair, I am disappointed with the NEP.

**[01:21:38.320] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001349)  
OWWA's total budget appears to increase by 1.48% because personnel services increased.

**[01:21:45.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001350)  
But MOOE was cut by 64.812 million.

**[01:21:50.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001351)  
Capital outlay by 72 million.

**[01:21:53.440] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001352)  
And the Emergency Repatriation Fund by 308.778 million, or 24%.

**[01:22:02.980] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001353)  
Now, my question is, Madam Chair, through the Chair, may I ask Administrator Caunan,

**[01:22:12.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001354)  
OWWA proposed 12.8 billion, but only 4.3 billion was included in the NEP, despite the 8.533 billion gap.

**[01:22:22.610] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001355)  
OWWA is asking Congress to restore only 2 billion.

**[01:22:25.890] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001356)  
Is this already the irreducible minimum for statutory life and safety and implementation-ready requirements?

**[01:22:37.400] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001357)  
Madam Chair, with the permission of our Secretary,

**[01:22:42.700] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001358)  
At alam niyo po, siyempre po para sa OFWs, dapat itodo, Madam Chair, Cong.

**[01:22:51.860] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001359)  
Lalong-lalo na po yung nasabi na po ni Sec. Hans, ito pong krisis paiba-iba.

**[01:22:58.780] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001360)  
Meron po isang...

**[01:22:59.780] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001361)  
bansa lang, meron dalawang bansa.

**[01:23:04.680] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001362)  
Sa Middle East crisis, pitong bansa, Madam Chair.

**[01:23:08.180] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001363)  
At hindi lamang naman yun ang krisis na dinadaanan ng ating mga kababayan.

**[01:23:16.220] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001364)  
Marami din po tayong tinutulungan na medical repatriation at iba't ibang klase pong emergencies as defined.

**[01:23:26.930] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001365)  
Alam niyo po, Madam Chair, nasabi ko kanina,

**[01:23:29.600] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001366)  
halos tatlong taon po tayong

**[01:23:32.060] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001368)  
ang ERF ay nasa 1.2 billion.

**[01:23:35.960] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001369)  
At ngayong nasa gitna po tayo ng matinding, hindi pa ho tapos ang krisis ng US-Iran conflict.

**[01:23:44.260] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001370)  
Yung 900 million ho, Madam Chair, hindi sapat.

**[01:23:48.340] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001371)  
Ang hinihingi po namin, of course, at the very minimum po, i-restore yung 1.2.

**[01:23:55.140] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001372)  
Ngunit, siguro naman ho bilang administrator,

**[01:24:00.720] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001379)  
mandato ko rin ho talagang i-advocate.

**[01:24:04.480] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001380)  
Currently po ang nagastos na po natin ay 1.7 billion.

**[01:24:09.300] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001381)  
Ibig sabihin ang amount na 1.2 or on the part of DMW na 2 billion.

**[01:24:14.580] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001382)  
Pag may krisis na kasing laki ng hinarap po natin, Kong at Madam Chair,

**[01:24:22.120] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001383)  
tatlong buwan, anim na buwan, ubos na po agad.

**[01:24:24.920] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001384)  
Pag po krisis, ang ticket po walang promo fare.

**[01:24:30.000] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001385)  
Ang ticket po nakatodo po, lalong-lalo na kung limitado ang tickets

**[01:24:35.320] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001386)  
at ang mga aeroplano, hindi po sila lumilipad.

**[01:24:39.620] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001387)  
So, when we asked Madam Chair for additional budget of 2 billion,

**[01:24:48.240] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001388)  
of which close to 600 million is for the emergency repatriation,

**[01:24:53.400] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001389)  
we were just looking at what currently,

**[01:24:56.720] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001390)  
ay pinagdadaanan natin.

**[01:25:02.900] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001391)  
But aside from the Middle East crisis, there are other geopolitical conflicts

**[01:25:08.800] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001392)  
in other parts of the world, Madam Chair, even near us here in the Philippines,

**[01:25:14.720] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001393)  
that kailangan din po maging ready yung ating mga kababayan.

**[01:25:20.980] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001394)  
So, nung nag-propose po ang OWWA ng 12 billion po sa DBM,

**[01:25:26.640] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001395)  
Limang billion po doon sa 12 ay para po sa emergency repatriation fund.

**[01:25:33.600] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001396)  
Ngunit, syempre po, naiintindihan natin the limited fiscal space,

**[01:25:37.760] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001397)  
naiintindihan po natin lahat, kailangan ng additional budget, kailangan ng tulong.

**[01:25:43.960] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001398)  
Ngunit, sa gitna ng pinagdadaanan natin ngayon, na hindi pa tapos na krisis, Madam Chair,

**[01:25:49.460] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001399)  
ay hindi naman yata namin kakayanin na mas mababa pa po ang budget kesa noon na walang ganitong klaseng kaguluhan.

**[01:25:57.800] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001400)  
Yun po, Madam Chair.

**[01:26:01.780] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001401)  
Madam Chair, magkano po yung nagastos nyo before the crisis?

**[01:26:08.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001402)  
And how much did you spend after the crisis?

**[01:26:11.300] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001403)  
Maganda pong tanong yan, Madam Chair.

**[01:26:13.740] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001404)  
Normally po, for the last, I would say 2024 and 2025, out of the 1.2 billion,

**[01:26:20.840] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001405)  
so ibig sabihin po, walang aggravation ng regional conflict,

**[01:26:25.140] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001406)  
Our utilization for the ERF was already

**[01:26:28.740] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001407)  
at 60%.

**[01:26:32.700] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001408)  
Ngayon po, noon nagkaroon ng krisis, naubos po yung 1.2,

**[01:26:37.060] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001409)  
in fact, humingi po kami ng supplemental na 700.

**[01:26:40.580] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001410)  
So, normally po, kung walang ganitong klaseng intensification of conflict,

**[01:26:48.160] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001411)  
we're already utilizing 60% of the 1.2 billion.

**[01:26:52.260] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001412)  
And this was spent on what, particularly?

**[01:26:55.920] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001413)  
Um, if you...

**[01:26:56.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001414)  
Before the crisis po, no?

**[01:26:58.800] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001415)  
Before the crisis, still the same repatriation expenses,

**[01:27:03.380] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001416)  
meaning international airfare, hotel or temporary accommodation,

**[01:27:10.580] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001417)  
medical escorts if needed, medical repatriation, land transportation,

**[01:27:17.640] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001418)  
domestic flight tickets, hotel accommodation here in the Philippines,

**[01:27:22.300] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001419)  
financial assistance for the repatriated workers,

**[01:27:27.020] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001420)  
and medical assistance.

**[01:27:28.740] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001421)  
If it's needed po.

**[01:27:30.680] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001422)  
Karamihan po ng, Madam Chair, ng mga kaso prior to the crisis,

**[01:27:35.420] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001423)  
ano po yung kaso ng mga ni-repatriate natin?

**[01:27:39.000] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001424)  
Malaking porsyento po dito ang mga nawawalan po ng trabaho na ating mga kababayan,

**[01:27:44.620] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001425)  
distressed workers.

**[01:27:45.420] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001426)  
Ngunit ako na po ang unang aamin,

**[01:27:49.540] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001427)  
tumaas po ang medical repatriation din po natin.

**[01:27:53.260] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001428)  
Ito po ang mga kababayan natin na hindi po natin puwedeng iwan abroad,

**[01:27:58.580] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001430)  
dahil may sakit sila.

**[01:28:01.120] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001431)  
So alam nyo naman po, pag medical repatriation, Madam Chair,

**[01:28:04.880] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001432)  
nire-require po minsan ng ospital or doktor na kailangan po may medical escort,

**[01:28:10.540] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001433)  
ibig sabihin po nurse kasama sa aeroplano,

**[01:28:13.380] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001434)  
at minsan po pag, depende po sa attending physician,

**[01:28:19.100] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001435)  
sasabihin po nila baka kailangan nung ating worker ay tatlong upuan dahil hihiga po siya,

**[01:28:26.040] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001436)  
kung anong kailangan para maiuwi yung ating kababayan.

**[01:28:29.400] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001438)  
so this is also being paid by the emergency repatriation fund po.

**[01:28:34.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001439)  
Ano pong sinabi ng DBM bakit nire-reduce nila yung inyong repatriation fund and yung NEP?

**[01:28:44.360] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001440)  
Madam Chair, if I may po.

**[01:28:47.140] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001441)  
I believe that they're looking at utilization rate and the utilization rate of 2025.

**[01:28:55.340] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001442)  
However, the conflict arose,

**[01:28:57.980] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001443)  
starting Feb 28 of this year,

**[01:29:01.180] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001444)  
and it's still ongoing,

**[01:29:02.880] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001445)  
and this is, I would say, outside the control of the Department of Migrant Workers or OWWA.

**[01:29:15.120] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001446)  
The repatriation, emergency repatriation fund at ang action fund, ito po ay mga standby fund na pag at kung saka sakaling may,

**[01:29:27.320] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001447)  
Madam Chair, may I ask the Secretary,

**[01:29:30.460] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001448)  
does DMW fully endorse the entire 2 billion request and commit to defend it through final congressional action?

**[01:29:37.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001449)  
Yes, Mr. Congressman, Madam Chair.

**[01:29:39.940] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001450)  
We stand by all our requests between the OWWA and the DMW.

**[01:29:45.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001451)  
We share the same levels.

**[01:29:49.300] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001452)  
We are joined at the hip.

**[01:29:50.800] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001453)  
Kaya po kami ay magkatuwang at

**[01:29:54.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001454)  
sabay po.

**[01:29:55.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001455)  
Yes, the short answer is yes, we endorse this.

**[01:29:58.580] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001456)  
Thank you for that, Mr. Secretary.

**[01:30:01.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001457)  
Madam Chair, I strongly object to using utilization rate mechanically as a reason to reduce the Emergency Repatriation Fund.

**[01:30:09.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001458)  
We cannot predict when war, mass displacement, or evacuation will occur, but we can anticipate and prepare.

**[01:30:16.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001459)  
A contingency fund does not become unnecessary simply because the worst crisis did not occur during a particular year.

**[01:30:24.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001460)  
In any event, utilization is already high, as mentioned by Administrator Caunan's letter.

**[01:30:31.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001461)  
Sinabi po niya na 81.92% of the current fiscal year 2026 ERF appropriation has already been utilized as of July 31.

**[01:30:41.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001462)  
The separate OWWA table submitted to us as of August 25 shows total ERF expenses at 1.6 billion,

**[01:30:50.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001463)  
663.284 million,

**[01:30:54.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001464)  
for war-related expenses,

**[01:30:55.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001465)  
and 967 million for regular repatriation.

**[01:31:00.300] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001466)  
Madam Chair, may I ask the Secretary,

**[01:31:02.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001467)  
was the 24% reduction in ERF based wholly or partly on past utilization?

**[01:31:08.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001468)  
If so, does the DMW agree that this is an unsafe basis for determining the required level of a life and safety contingency fund?

**[01:31:17.580] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001469)  
I can respond.

**[01:31:19.560] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001470)  
The numerics po, I think PY can perhaps respond to the details.

**[01:31:24.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001471)  
But I can fully subscribe to the principle that utilization is a difficult basis.

**[01:31:31.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001472)  
And having said that, utilization anyway will be dependent on the geopolitical situation.

**[01:31:40.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001473)  
And as the February 28 US-Israel-Iran war had clearly proven,

**[01:31:47.640] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001474)  
any geopolitical crisis can happen anytime.

**[01:31:52.630] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001475)  
It will always be...

**[01:31:54.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001476)  
beyond our control in terms of the powers that be,

**[01:31:57.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001477)  
geopolitical powers that be.

**[01:31:59.440] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001478)  
But, however, it is definitely within our control

**[01:32:02.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001479)  
to have the necessary resources ready to be mobilized when the time comes.

**[01:32:08.460] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001480)  
And the President has clearly given us clear instructions

**[01:32:12.700] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001481)  
to have the necessary resources.

**[01:32:15.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001482)  
Ito pong pagbigay sa amin ng Pangulo

**[01:32:17.820] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001483)  
ng 3 billion peso supplemental fund is a very, very clear statement

**[01:32:23.400] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001484)  
from no less than...

**[01:32:24.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001485)  
the President himself na napakahalaga ng resources.

**[01:32:27.480] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001486)  
Kaya for our part, Mr. Congressman, if I may speak also for the DMW,

**[01:32:32.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001487)  
ang gusto rin naman na hoon naming level sa action fund namin

**[01:32:35.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001488)  
is 3 billion as set by the President himself.

**[01:32:39.060] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001489)  
Kasi 2 billion lang hoon kami sa NEP.

**[01:32:41.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001490)  
And then there are items din po to round up the 2 billion figure from the DMW side

**[01:32:46.640] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001491)  
that had already also been justified in our letter to the Speaker.

**[01:32:51.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001492)  
So, the short answer is,

**[01:32:53.640] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001493)  
tama po kayo, Mr. Congressman.

**[01:32:56.520] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001494)  
When it comes to crisis responses,

**[01:32:59.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001495)  
precisely, there is no crystal ball that will tell us when a crisis will occur.

**[01:33:09.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001496)  
And so, we must be sure that there are resources always ready 100% of the time.

**[01:33:14.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001497)  
Natigil na po ba yung Middle East conflict?

**[01:33:18.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001498)  
I believe the political expert to conclude on this matter,

**[01:33:23.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001499)  
Mr. Congressman.

**[01:33:26.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001500)  
However, we can say for ourselves that while there is a ceasefire,

**[01:33:33.440] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001501)  
there was a ceasefire, and it appears to be implemented at the present,

**[01:33:39.700] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001502)  
we can be sure, based on the incidents that happened 3 weeks ago,

**[01:33:45.460] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001503)  
that it may reignite or resume at any given time.

**[01:33:51.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001504)  
One side had attacked the other.

**[01:33:53.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001505)  
And to retaliate, once again, we saw attacks in the Gulf states.

**[01:33:59.700] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001506)  
While we are so thankful to the Gulf states' host governments, countries,

**[01:34:04.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001507)  
for literally, through their defense systems, defending, protecting our workers, our people,

**[01:34:10.420] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001508)  
again, on the Philippine side, we must also be ready.

**[01:34:14.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001509)  
More than any other origin country, migrant worker origin country, Mr. Chair,

**[01:34:19.840] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001510)  
tayo po ang nag-respond for our citizens.

**[01:34:22.880] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001511)  
And we want to keep it that way.

**[01:34:25.720] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001512)  
We do not want to lose this opportunity to help our Filipinos in crisis.

**[01:34:32.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001513)  
So basically, di pa natitigil ang krisis in the Middle East.

**[01:34:36.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001514)  
And I have to add that we must also prepare for a possible Taiwan-Strait conflict

**[01:34:42.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001515)  
affecting nearly 200,000 Filipinos.

**[01:34:45.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001516)  
The government already has contingency planning,

**[01:34:48.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001517)  
but Congress must know whether the plan is fully costed and funded.

**[01:34:54.060] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001518)  
So, there is also a risk of stricter or reciprocal immigration enforcement

**[01:34:58.940] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001519)  
arising from our tensions with China.

**[01:35:00.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001520)  
More than 100 Filipinos recently, based on reports po,

**[01:35:04.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001521)  
ang 100 Filipinos faced immigration action in China

**[01:35:10.240] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001522)  
following the controversy over the Chinese nationals detained dito po sa Pilipinas.

**[01:35:15.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001523)  
So, we cannot conclusively call this retaliation,

**[01:35:18.320] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001524)  
but we cannot ignore the possibility of,

**[01:35:22.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001525)  
tit-for-tat enforcement.

**[01:35:24.460] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001526)  
Tama po ba?

**[01:35:25.920] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001527)  
Hong Kong has a separate immigration system,

**[01:35:28.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001528)  
but it is strictly enforcing its prohibition against unauthorized second or part-time jobs.

**[01:35:34.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001529)  
Filipino workers have been arrested or jailed for illegal employment.

**[01:35:39.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001530)  
Marami po kasi kami nakausap sa Hong Kong eh.

**[01:35:41.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001531)  
Tapos ang sinasabi po nila, karamihan sa kanila,

**[01:35:44.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001532)  
kumukuha ng pangalawa, pangatlong trabaho.

**[01:35:46.440] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001533)  
Noon, pinapayagan sila ng Hong Kong, pero ngayon, parang ang nangyayari,

**[01:35:51.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001534)  
hinihigpitan po sila, dahil yata,

**[01:35:52.740] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001535)  
mahigpit tayo sa mga Chinese workers dito.

**[01:35:55.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001536)  
So, whatever our position, our government position maintained towards China is,

**[01:36:01.140] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001537)  
we must also fund the protection of OFWs who may bear the consequences.

**[01:36:06.920] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001538)  
Madam Chair, may I ask the Secretary,

**[01:36:09.300] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001539)  
how many Filipinos in China and Hong Kong presently have visa,

**[01:36:13.440] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001540)  
are overstaying, or are unauthorized secondary, have secondary work problems?

**[01:36:20.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001541)  
And what would be the minimum ERF and WASP funding na kailangan

**[01:36:27.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001542)  
for lawyers, detention visits, documentation, medical assistance, and repatriation?

**[01:36:33.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001543)  
And Madam Chair, what is the DMW's fully-costed evacuation plan for Filipinos in Taiwan

**[01:36:39.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001544)  
if commercial air and sea routes are disrupted?

**[01:36:43.320] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001545)  
What minimum standby ERF is required so that the government can act immediately

**[01:36:47.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001546)  
instead of waiting for a supplemental release

**[01:36:50.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001547)  
after a crisis has already begun?

**[01:36:54.520] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001548)  
Mr. Chair, we do have the figures of OFWs in both Hong Kong and China.

**[01:37:01.940] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001549)  
However, we do not have figures as to who are overstaying,

**[01:37:06.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001550)  
nor those who have secondary lines of work.

**[01:37:11.240] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001551)  
There would be difficulty gathering this data.

**[01:37:13.940] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001552)  
But rest assured, over the weekend,

**[01:37:17.100] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001553)  
we just had an Undersecretary visit Hong Kong

**[01:37:20.380] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001554)  
and meet with the Filipino community there

**[01:37:22.580] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001555)  
to assure the community of our support.

**[01:37:27.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001556)  
And thus far, at this stage, Mr. Congressman,

**[01:37:32.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001557)  
we do not see any distress calls that are unusual in Hong Kong at this stage.

**[01:37:42.460] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001558)  
Of course, there are OFWs who need to be helped.

**[01:37:45.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001559)  
We have our Migrant Resource Center ready always

**[01:37:47.800] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001560)  
to help our OFWs there.

**[01:37:50.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001561)  
We stand ready always.

**[01:37:51.700] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001562)  
As to how much will be needed,

**[01:37:53.880] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001563)  
we feel that, once again,

**[01:37:56.380] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001564)  
the three billion support of the President

**[01:37:58.500] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001565)  
really set the benchmark at the very least

**[01:38:01.820] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001566)  
of what should be provided to our Action Fund.

**[01:38:04.100] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001567)  
Kaya nga humihingi kami ng karagdagan din sa aming AKSYON Fund

**[01:38:08.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001568)  
so that magiging sapat po.

**[01:38:11.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001569)  
With respect to Taiwan,

**[01:38:13.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001570)  
for these last three years,

**[01:38:14.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001571)  
we have been meeting not just DMW and OWWA.

**[01:38:18.440] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001572)  
DMW, OWWA, and other relevant agencies

**[01:38:21.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001573)  
with respect to crisis management manuals and processes

**[01:38:26.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001574)  
in the event that a crisis emerges.

**[01:38:31.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001575)  
Hindi ko lang po masabi yung exact details,

**[01:38:33.720] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001576)  
but rest assured, Mr. Congressman,

**[01:38:35.480] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001577)  
we can provide the crisis manuals that are in place

**[01:38:39.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001578)  
Saan po makikita, ano ang transport modalities

**[01:38:42.240] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001579)  
kapag may nangyari in that part of the world.

**[01:38:45.560] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001580)  
Pero hindi ko po ma-disclose yung detalya at this stage.

**[01:38:49.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001581)  
I hope you understand

**[01:38:50.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001582)  
for security and confidentiality reasons.

**[01:38:52.940] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001583)  
But rest assured,

**[01:38:53.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001584)  
magtatatlong taon na po

**[01:38:57.500] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001585)  
kasi isa po ito sa maagang instructions

**[01:39:00.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001586)  
ng ating Pangulo

**[01:39:01.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001587)  
na umupo kami as a whole-of-government

**[01:39:04.420] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001588)  
aid entity endeavor

**[01:39:09.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001589)  
para handa po tayo

**[01:39:10.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001590)  
kapag nagka-crisis sa Taiwan.

**[01:39:14.340] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001591)  
Madam Chair,

**[01:39:15.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001593)  
just a last question.

**[01:39:18.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001595)  
Kung sakali po na maubos ang pondo niyo,

**[01:39:22.460] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001596)  
tapos biglang nagkaroon ng crisis,

**[01:39:25.300] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001597)  
ganong katagal po ba ang tingin niyo?

**[01:39:29.940] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001598)  
Halimbawa,

**[01:39:30.700] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001599)  
nagpasa pa ng supplemental budget

**[01:39:32.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001600)  
para magkaroon kayo ng additional funding,

**[01:39:35.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001601)  
ganong katagal ang magiging response niyo

**[01:39:37.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001602)  
kung nagkaroon ng gera sa Taiwan?

**[01:39:41.580] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001603)  
Sa tingin ko po, dahil nga sa paghahanda namin

**[01:39:44.800] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001604)  
itong huling tatlong taon

**[01:39:46.460] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001605)  
ay magiging maagap po ito.

**[01:39:48.280] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001606)  
Again, this is an inter-agency effort.

**[01:39:51.280] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001607)  
Of course, geographically,

**[01:39:53.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001608)  
malapit ang Taiwan.

**[01:39:55.380] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001609)  
So the logistics will not be as difficult, say,

**[01:39:59.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001610)  
as a repatriation effort from the Middle East.

**[01:40:02.440] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001611)  
And then, of course,

**[01:40:03.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001612)  
merong other government agencies involved.

**[01:40:08.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001613)  
We can disclose this perhaps in confidence.

**[01:40:14.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001614)  
Hindi ko lang po ma-disclose yung precise

**[01:40:16.720] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001615)  
appointed actions

**[01:40:20.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001616)  
ng bawat government agency.

**[01:40:24.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001617)  
But rest assured po,

**[01:40:26.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001618)  
based on the instructions of the President,

**[01:40:28.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001619)  
we have been meeting

**[01:40:29.200] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001620)  
and are prepared for this.

**[01:40:31.500] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001621)  
And our response time will be swift

**[01:40:33.640] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001622)  
kasi nga po may mga crisis manuals in place na po.

**[01:40:36.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001623)  
Kahit po maubos ang pondo ninyo, Madam Chair?

**[01:40:39.300] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001624)  
Kung maubos ang pondo natin,

**[01:40:43.480] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001626)  
Well, jointly, of course,

**[01:40:45.240] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001627)  
with OWWA and the DMW.

**[01:40:47.960] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001628)  
we will be ready po

**[01:40:50.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001629)  
kasi kasama po

**[01:40:51.100] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001630)  
sa discussions, ang DBM,

**[01:40:53.280] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001631)  
ang budget,

**[01:40:54.480] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001632)  
Office of the Executive Secretary.

**[01:40:56.810] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001633)  
at we stand ready po

**[01:40:59.240] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001634)  
to submit the corresponding budget proposals.

**[01:41:02.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001635)  
nakaabang na po yan

**[01:41:03.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001636)  
yung mga necessities natin.

**[01:41:05.100] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001637)  
Depende po kasi ang crisis management systems natin,

**[01:41:08.640] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001638)  
depende sa stages and gravity ng crisis.

**[01:41:11.970] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001639)  
So

**[01:41:12.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001640)  
as the crisis unfolds,

**[01:41:14.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001641)  
deepens or worsens,

**[01:41:16.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001642)  
ay meron pong corresponding resources

**[01:41:18.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001643)  
na nakaabang na napapasok

**[01:41:20.420] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001644)  
at hihingin.

**[01:41:21.450] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001645)  
So,

**[01:41:21.840] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001646)  
naka-itemized na po yung mga bagay na ganyan.

**[01:41:26.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001647)  
So ang turnaround time natin magiging mabilis.

**[01:41:29.530] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001648)  
Madam Chair,

**[01:41:30.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001649)  
I would just like to urge

**[01:41:31.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001650)

**[01:41:35.760] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001651)  
all members of the House

**[01:41:37.440] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001652)  
to support the budget of the DMW,

**[01:41:39.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001653)  
yung hinihingi po nila.

**[01:41:41.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001654)  
Tandaan po natin na

**[01:41:42.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001655)  
ang ating mga OFW,

**[01:41:44.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001656)  
ang ating mga bayani ng bayan,

**[01:41:46.240] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001657)  
at sila po, they contribute a large chunk

**[01:41:48.700] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001658)  
of our GDP.

**[01:41:50.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001659)  
So they contribute a large chunk

**[01:41:53.060] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001660)  
to our GDP.

**[01:41:53.920] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001661)  
So huwag po natin silang pabayaan.

**[01:41:56.440] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001662)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:41:57.920] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001663)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[01:42:01.700] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-001664)

**[01:42:03.200] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-001665)

**[01:42:25.520] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-001676)
