the Chair recognizes

**[01:42:29.320] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-001678)  
Representative Bryan Revilla.

**[01:42:32.840] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001679)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:42:35.360] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001680)  
Kitang-kita naman po natin lahat ng

**[01:42:37.300] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001681)  
lifeline po ng ating mga departamento.

**[01:42:40.020] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001682)  
Ang DMW ay

**[01:42:41.460] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001683)  
ang AKSYON Fund

**[01:42:43.080] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001684)  
at ang ERF natin.

**[01:42:44.860] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001685)  
And kita naman po din natin

**[01:42:46.520] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001686)  
kung paano po nila ginastos po ito

**[01:42:48.360] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001687)  
para makatulong po sa lahat ng mga kababayan natin

**[01:42:50.880] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001688)  
na nangangailangan ng assistance

**[01:42:52.220] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001689)  
noong krisis po

**[01:42:53.980] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001690)  
na kinakaharapan po natin.

**[01:42:57.160] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001691)  
In regards po sa

**[01:42:58.420] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001692)  
healthcare po ng ating mga

**[01:43:00.540] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001693)  
OFWs,

**[01:43:03.840] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001694)  
alam ko po

**[01:43:04.600] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001695)  
gumagastos, ginagastos niyo din po

**[01:43:06.600] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001696)  
ang AKSYON Fund para po dito.

**[01:43:08.100] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001697)  
Tama po ba?

**[01:43:09.360] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001698)  
And gusto ko lang po malaman

**[01:43:11.160] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001699)  
ang ating mga OFWs po ba

**[01:43:13.620] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001700)  
mandatory po ba ang pagbayad nila

**[01:43:15.720] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001701)  
sa PhilHealth?

**[01:43:16.580] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001702)  
And kung

**[01:43:17.400] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001703)  
nakakatulong po ba ang PhilHealth sa pagbawas po

**[01:43:22.180] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001704)  
o sa pagbigay po ng

**[01:43:24.580] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001705)  
panggastos po sa

**[01:43:25.560] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001706)  
pangangailangan medikal ng ating mga OFWs.

**[01:43:28.660] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001707)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:43:30.700] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001708)  
Yes po.

**[01:43:31.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001709)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Chair,

**[01:43:33.700] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001710)  
Mr. Chair, Mr. Congressman,

**[01:43:35.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001711)  
sa tanong.

**[01:43:36.380] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001712)  
At yung pong PhilHealth is mandatory.

**[01:43:40.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001713)  
It's prescribed by law.

**[01:43:43.960] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001714)  
Siguro masasabi ko lang dito muna

**[01:43:46.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001715)  
at the outset

**[01:43:46.820] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001716)  
ay hindi ho kami miyembro

**[01:43:48.640] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001717)  
ng PhilHealth Board,

**[01:43:49.500] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001718)  
but we will make a proposal

**[01:43:51.880] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001719)  
to amend the PhilHealth law

**[01:43:53.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001720)  
kasi DOLE po, nung nasa DOLE pa kami,

**[01:43:56.800] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001721)  
DOLE represented

**[01:43:57.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001722)  
the overseas Filipino workers

**[01:43:59.720] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001723)  
at the PhilHealth Board.

**[01:44:00.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001724)  
So baka mainam po na

**[01:44:02.700] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001725)  
bigyan kami ng seat ng OWWA

**[01:44:04.880] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001726)  
sa PhilHealth Board

**[01:44:05.920] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001727)  
to represent the OFW interest kasi nagko-contribute din ng OFW

**[01:44:10.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001728)  
sa PhilHealth Fund.

**[01:44:12.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001729)  
And pangalawa,

**[01:44:15.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001730)  
admittedly po,

**[01:44:16.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001731)  
perhaps we need

**[01:44:18.240] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001732)  
of course

**[01:44:19.880] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001733)  
Of course, President Beverly Ho is known

**[01:44:22.200] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001734)  
to us. She's a good colleague

**[01:44:24.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001735)  
from way back during the

**[01:44:26.100] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001736)  
health crisis, previous health crisis

**[01:44:28.420] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001737)  
pandemic.

**[01:44:31.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001738)  
But perhaps

**[01:44:32.200] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001739)  
we need to make a call to

**[01:44:33.560] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001740)  
President Ho and find out

**[01:44:36.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001741)  
exactly how we could partner

**[01:44:38.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001742)  
with PhilHealth. But the way

**[01:44:40.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001743)  
the Overseas Action Fund works

**[01:44:41.960] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001744)  
is that kami po,

**[01:44:43.640] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001745)  
naglalabas kami ng

**[01:44:46.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001746)  
pera at wala ho kaming

**[01:44:47.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001747)  
ceiling sa tinutulungan

**[01:44:50.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001748)  
ng ating OFWs na kailangan magbayad

**[01:44:52.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001749)  
ng hospital bills at gamot.

**[01:44:55.060] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001750)  
But, ang kailangan ko lang ho i-emphasize dito, kapag may insurance coverage ang OFW, at karamihan naman po, majority of OFWs do have host country insurance coverage in fairness, hinahayaan ho muna natin magbayad yung insurance cover nila.

**[01:45:12.200] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001751)  
Kaya meron din hong mandatory insurance from our side, so hinahayaan din natin magbayad yung insurance, private insurance from our side. Kung wala pong insurance coverage on either side, doon po papasok ang AKSYON Fund.

**[01:45:27.960] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001752)  
And I must say, pag nagbayad po tayo ng hospital bills, assuming that we had negotiated with the hospital at a barest minimum kung ano yung maitatalaga nilang hospital bill expense,

**[01:45:41.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001753)  
binabayaran po natin, Mr. Chair, we do not even set a ceiling as I mentioned. So by way of example, kung malubha yung isang kaso nating stage 4 cancer, more than a million pesos ang hospital bill, binayaran po natin ang hospital bill para makauwi po yung kababayan natin may stage 4 cancer at makapiling niya yung pamilya niya in his dying moments kasi nasawi rin po siya sa kinasamaang-palad.

**[01:46:08.380] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001754)  
But at least nung mga huling sandali niya, huling...

**[01:46:11.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001755)  
himlayan, kapiling niya yung kanyang pamilya at binayaran natin yung more than 1 million peso hospital bill niya.

**[01:46:19.100] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001756)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary, sa inyong sagot. Kita naman po natin na action fund po ay hindi lang po para sa repatriation kundi malaki din po itong pakinabang po sa healthcare po ng ating mga kababayan.

**[01:46:31.460] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001757)  
And kaya naman po dapat supportahan po talaga natin ang pagtaas po ng action fund at ng ERF ng Departamento ng DMW.

**[01:46:39.880] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001758)  
At kita din po natin sila po ang mga frontliners, sila po ang sumasagot sa mga saklolo ng ating mga kababayan diyan po sa gera po na kinaharap natin sa Middle East.

**[01:46:50.880] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001759)  
And nakakasama po ng loob na nakikita po natin na ultimo po yung sweldo po ng ating mga JOs po dyan po sa OWWA ay hindi pa po tama.

**[01:47:04.340] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001760)  
Tama ba na 888 pa kayo, diba? Nakakahiya naman po yun.

**[01:47:10.040] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001761)  
And nabisita ko po ang kanilang tanggapan.

**[01:47:13.120] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001762)  
Nakita ko po ang kagamitan po nila doon.

**[01:47:15.960] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001763)  
Paano ka po makakasagot po sa mga iyak ng mga kababayan natin kung ang kagamitan po nila ay 19 kopong-kopong pa po.

**[01:47:27.260] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001764)  
Yung iba po doon napakaluma na para makapagsilbi lang po sa taong bayan,

**[01:47:32.380] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001765)  
ang mga empleyado po o mga JO po natin na sumasagot po sa call center po ng OWWA ay gamit po nila ang kanilang personal na kagamitan.

**[01:47:41.960] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001766)  
Hindi lang yun, madami na rin po kailangan pagawa din sa kanilang building.

**[01:47:46.440] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001767)  
Balita ko po may water feature na po kayo doon na waterfall sa loob po ng inyong building.

**[01:47:51.180] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001768)  
And wala na.

**[01:47:54.740] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001769)  
So yun po, isa po sa mga kailangan po natin talaga is dagdagan po at bigyan po ng natamang supporta ang Department of Migrant Workers at ang OWWA

**[01:48:05.160] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001770)  
dahil yun nga po kailangan po natin pangalagaan ang ating OFWs tulad po ng sabi po ni

**[01:48:12.320] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001771)  
Congressman Nazal, kanina na malaki po ang ambag ng ating mga kababayang OFWs

**[01:48:17.340] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001772)  
and dapat naman po talaga we need to go the extra mile for these kababayans

**[01:48:22.360] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001773)  
who travel across the world para mabigyan po ng kabuhayan ang kanilang mga pamilya.

**[01:48:27.860] Rep. Bryan B. Revilla** (asr-001774)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Chair, yun lang po.

**[01:48:33.660] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-001775)  
Thank you so much, Honorable Bryan Revilla.

**[01:48:37.120] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-001776)  
Now, from the majority, we recognize Honorable Ditse Tina Pancho.

**[01:48:44.940] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001777)  
Good afternoon po, Mr. Chair.

**[01:48:47.340] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001778)  
Mr. Chair, nakita po natin dito na we have hospitals for OFW patients.

**[01:48:53.060] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001779)  
So, like in our case po, in Bulacan, we have OFWs po na pag-uwi, may mga sakit.

**[01:49:00.400] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001780)  
So, may we know kung ano na po mga hospital na makaka-avail po yung ating mga pasyente?

**[01:49:06.900] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001781)  
Kasi with DOH, they have list ng government hospitals.

**[01:49:11.820] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001782)  
So, do you have the same list with the DOH?

**[01:49:15.380] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001783)  
Yes po, Madam Chair, Madam Congresswoman.

**[01:49:18.400] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001784)  
Two things po. Una is we have an OFW hospital in San Fernando, Pampanga.

**[01:49:23.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001785)  
At marami po sa kanila doon ay mga...

**[01:49:25.740] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001786)  
Mr. Chair, is that JBL or another?

**[01:49:28.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001787)  
It's another hospital po. It's maybe a few kilometer, less than one kilometer away from JBL.

**[01:49:35.200] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001788)  
It's in San Fernando po.

**[01:49:38.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001789)  
And...

**[01:49:40.400] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001790)  
OFW.

**[01:49:40.960] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001791)  
Sindalan, no?

**[01:49:42.280] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-001792)  
Sindalan.

**[01:49:43.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001794)  
And marami po mga Bulacan OFWs na idadala, nakikinabang din po dito.

**[01:49:51.700] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001795)  
Paano po yung process nun?

**[01:49:53.140] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001796)  
The same with DOH, may MOA po sa inyo?

**[01:49:57.093] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001797)  
Kami po ang...

**[01:49:57.970] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001798)  
Or they have to go there directly, tapos...

**[01:49:59.960] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001799)  
Yes, they can go there directly, ma'am, through our regional office or directly walk in sa hospital.

**[01:50:06.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001800)  
At tutulungan po namin sila.

**[01:50:10.300] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001801)  
Meron pong urgent care facility for those needing

**[01:50:13.760] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001802)  
Emergency care.

**[01:50:15.000] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001803)  
May requirements po o...

**[01:50:17.748] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001804)  
Ma'am...

**[01:50:18.271] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001805)  
Basta may ID lang sila na they work for...

**[01:50:21.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001806)  
Ma'am, simple lang ang requirement.

**[01:50:23.400] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001807)  
Basta current or former OFW, hindi na nga ho namin tinitignan, ma'am, gano'ng katagal naging OFW.

**[01:50:30.820] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001808)  
Kahit 2003 lang, halimbawa, tinatanggap po namin.

**[01:50:34.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001809)  
And then, ang dependents po nila, puwede rin po.

**[01:50:36.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001810)  
By dependents, we mean immediate ascendants, so parents, and then immediate descendants, mga anak.

**[01:50:43.540] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001811)  
Ano po yung hospital bill nila, fully paid lahat o...

**[01:50:47.240] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001812)  
Yes.

**[01:50:48.200] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001813)  
Fully paid, ma'am.

**[01:50:49.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001814)  
By way of example, we have had around 100 childbirths.

**[01:50:54.960] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001815)  
Mga OFWs po ito na nanganak sa ospital.

**[01:50:57.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001816)  
Libring-libre po lahat.

**[01:50:59.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001817)  
Wala silang sinasagot kahit isang kusing.

**[01:51:01.760] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001818)  
And then, of course, may outpatient facility, blood work.

**[01:51:06.280] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001819)  
And then, ang realidad lang po, we are level 2 hospital.

**[01:51:11.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001820)  
So hindi pa ho ganap ang

**[01:51:13.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001821)  
aming services.

**[01:51:15.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001822)  
So, meron pa rin kaming referrals to JBL and other hospitals.

**[01:51:19.740] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001823)  
So, sa JBL pa rin po yung...

**[01:51:21.500] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001824)  
Kasi pag sa JBL, usually, JBL na po sila.

**[01:51:25.020] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001825)  
They won't transfer to other hospitals.

**[01:51:26.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001826)  
Kapag major na po at wala po doon sa services that we provide,

**[01:51:34.920] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001827)  
ang surgery, for instance, then we refer accordingly to JBL.

**[01:51:39.960] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001828)  
So, ang facilities, hindi pa po kumpleto?

**[01:51:43.500] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001830)  
To the extent that it is a level 2 hospital, kumpleto po, ma'am.

**[01:51:48.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001831)  
Pero hindi nga po talaga pa level 3.

**[01:51:51.400] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001832)  
Ma'am, can I ask USEC Nikki to also help me respond to the query?

**[01:51:56.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001833)  
USEC Nikki is the USEC in charge of the hospital.

**[01:52:00.880] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001834)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:52:02.220] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001835)  
With the permission of the Secretary po.

**[01:52:03.940] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001836)  
Yung nabanggit po kanina ni Secretary Hans,

**[01:52:06.840] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001837)  
papaano pumupunta ang mga OFWs sa OFW hospital.

**[01:52:10.920] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001838)  
May mga cases po na tinatawag lang.

**[01:52:13.940] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001839)  
po sa amin.

**[01:52:14.700] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001840)  
And then the patient do not have the capacity like pamasahe or hindi makalakad.

**[01:52:20.460] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001841)  
So, pinapasundo po namin yan ng ambulance po namin.

**[01:52:24.360] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001842)  
Either from Pampanga po, manggagaling yung ambulance.

**[01:52:27.520] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001843)  
O meron din po kami dito sa OWWA at saka po sa DMW.

**[01:52:31.820] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001844)  
So, iahatid po yung patient sa OFW hospital, urgent care, sa emergency room po.

**[01:52:37.440] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001845)  
And then, i-assess po yan ng doctor.

**[01:52:39.840] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001846)  
So, may cases po na talagang they have to...

**[01:52:43.800] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001847)  
stay at the hospital for complete medical checks po.

**[01:52:47.640] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001848)  
And then, after assessment, kung okay na po siya, ma'am,

**[01:52:51.500] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001849)  
ibabalik din po namin siya sa bahay nila.

**[01:52:53.560] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001850)  
And then, there's follow-through by phone po.

**[01:52:57.600] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001851)  
In terms po of the services, as mentioned po by the Secretary,

**[01:53:01.600] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001852)  
while it's not level 3 yet, may mga cases po na halimbawa,

**[01:53:07.120] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001853)  
patient ng OFW hospital, i-refer namin sa JBL.

**[01:53:11.980] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001854)  
But in the same manner, dahil we know,

**[01:53:13.800] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001855)  
the cases also sa JBL na medyo, ano na rin po, punong-puno.

**[01:53:20.620] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001856)  
So, pagka po may mga cases na kaya for level 2,

**[01:53:24.020] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001857)  
si JBL po, dadalhin naman po sa OFW hospital yung pasyente.

**[01:53:28.700] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001858)  
So, that's how it works.

**[01:53:29.900] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001859)  
And zero billing, yes, because we're also supported by PhilHealth,

**[01:53:34.800] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001860)  
by MAIPIF, and then OWWA.

**[01:53:36.780] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-001861)  
And yung pondo po mismo ng hospital, yung MOOE po.

**[01:53:42.000] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001862)  
Ma'am, with all the services that you give,

**[01:53:43.860] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001863)  
to the OFWs, nakapagtataka, bakit binabawasan pa kayo?

**[01:53:49.820] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001864)  
Di ba? Kasi nakakaawa naman po,

**[01:53:52.060] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001865)  
kasi yung puro sacrifices na po on the part of the OFWs,

**[01:53:57.220] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001866)  
pero hindi nabibigyan ng magandang budget.

**[01:54:00.660] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001867)  
So, sana po mapakinggan tayo ng DBM,

**[01:54:02.740] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001868)  
para may pagpatuloy po natin yung support natin to our kababayans.

**[01:54:07.840] Rep. Agustina Dominique "Ditse Tina" C. Pancho** (asr-001869)  
Thank you po, Mr. Chair.

**[01:54:13.220] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-001870)  
Thank you so much, Honorable Tina Pancho.

**[01:54:20.580] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-001871)  
Now, we recognize from the majority, Honorable Johanne Bautista.

**[01:54:27.880] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001872)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:54:29.860] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001873)  
Good afternoon po to the Honorable Committee,

**[01:54:32.900] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001874)  
Sec. Hans, Admin PY, the DMW, and the OWWA families.

**[01:54:36.760] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001875)  
Magandang hapon po sa inyo.

**[01:54:38.520] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001876)  
Habang pasan po na ating mga OFW ang paglilingkod at pagsaservisyo sa mundo,

**[01:54:43.990] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001877)  
sino po ang nag-aalaga sa kanila?

**[01:54:46.160] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001878)  
Ito po ang mahalagang papel ng DMW at ng OWA.

**[01:54:50.360] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001879)  
The DMW's mandate is to ensure that policy becomes protection

**[01:54:54.580] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001880)  
and that protection becomes tangible relief on the ground.

**[01:54:58.620] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001881)  
Pinatunayan po ito ng DMW at ng OWA, lalo na po noong mga nakaraang buwan.

**[01:55:03.880] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001882)  
Sa gitna po ng digmaan at panganib na riyan po ang DMW at ang OWA.

**[01:55:09.340] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001883)  
Hindi po madali ang inyong gawain, and yet you persist with quiet courage and resolve.

**[01:55:16.160] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001884)  
At patunay po kayo na ang serbisyo publiko,

**[01:55:19.020] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001885)  
ay may puso mapagkalinga at matapang na paninindigan

**[01:55:22.780] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001886)  
pagdating po sa pagtatanggol ng karapatan at kapakanan ng ating mga kababayan.

**[01:55:27.400] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001887)  
Maraming salamat po sa inyong serbisyo.

**[01:55:30.160] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001888)  
Now, I will be touching on the, I think, yung mga pinaka-importante po

**[01:55:36.580] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001889)  
at mga paborito ko pong programa ng DMW at ng OWWA.

**[01:55:40.500] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001890)  
Specifically po sa Action Fund na lagi pong in action.

**[01:55:45.080] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001891)  
We have seen Action Fund in action in many ways, in many times.

**[01:55:49.580] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001892)  
At personally, nakita ko po talaga yan.

**[01:55:51.680] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001893)  
Gusto lang po natin bigyan emphasis on record.

**[01:55:54.500] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001894)  
Ilan po ba ang naging benepisyaryo so far ng Action Fund for 2026?

**[01:56:01.740] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001895)  
And maybe po, Madam Chair, the department can share with us kung ano po ang kanilang magiging pondo so far for 2027.

**[01:56:10.720] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001896)  
And sa tingin po ba nila, ay sapat nila ito?

**[01:56:13.560] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001897)  
At kung hindi, let's make it of record, ilan po ba ang kailangan po ng DMW to service?

**[01:56:18.840] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001898)  
All our Filipino OFWs abroad, hindi lamang po yung nasa gitna po ng Middle East crisis,

**[01:56:24.660] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001899)  
kundi pati po yung mga nabibiktima na ating mga, yung mga scam hubs, nasa Asia po yan so far.

**[01:56:30.820] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001900)  
Ang dami rin po nating kailangan serbisyohan.

**[01:56:34.180] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001901)  
Maybe we can start po with the Action Fund, Madam Chair.

**[01:56:39.280] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001902)  
Yes, thank you, Madam Chair. Thank you, our dear budget sponsor last year.

**[01:56:46.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001903)  
And congratulations po on your chairship

**[01:56:49.380] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001904)  
of the Committee on Social Services as we welcome our new budget sponsor, Madam Chair, Vice Chair.

**[01:56:56.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001905)  
Doon po sa Action Fund, as you know, it's a legal, medical, and financial assistance fund.

**[01:57:04.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001906)  
Meron po tayong 80,000, as I mentioned, financially assisted, 14,000 OFWs with pending or past legal cases

**[01:57:14.660] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001907)  
handled by our law firms and in-house lawyers.

**[01:57:19.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001908)  
And then meron din tayong around 5,000 medical cases na natulungan.

**[01:57:24.660] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001909)  
As I said, hindi po natin tinutulungan pagka-covered upon ng insurance at nababayaran ng insurance yung medical coverage nila.

**[01:57:35.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-001910)  
Having said this, with your permission, Madam Chair, can I ask Undersecretary Felicitas Bay, who handles the Action Fund, on the details of the budget?

**[01:57:45.800] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001911)  
Magandang araw po, Madam Chair, and also to...

**[01:57:49.760] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001912)  
Representative Johanne Bautista.

**[01:57:53.320] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001913)  
For the Action Fund po, nabanggit na po kanina ni Secretary, doon sa aming financial assistance, only for the period January to July of 2026.

**[01:58:04.480] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001914)  
This includes po our data for our migrant workers offices, our regional offices, and also the central office.

**[01:58:13.160] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001915)  
Sa financial assistance po ay umabot na po tayo sa mga nabigyan natin at natulungan.

**[01:58:19.420] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001916)  
na 44,445 OFWs.

**[01:58:23.580] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001917)  
For medical, 1,173.

**[01:58:28.800] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001918)  
Legal assistance po ay 2,610.

**[01:58:33.140] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001919)  
And for the repatriation, 7,329.

**[01:58:37.750] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001920)  
Kasama din po yung ating assistance in welfare cases, 89,891.

**[01:58:43.920] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001921)  
Just for the period of January to July 2026,

**[01:58:48.280] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001922)  
the total services...

**[01:58:50.500] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001923)  
and assistance that we have provided, sourced from the Action Fund, 145,448 po yung ating beneficiaries.

**[01:59:00.060] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001924)  
If ang Action Fund po ay nasimula namin ng 2023, and from July 2023 up to July of 2026,

**[01:59:11.200] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001925)  
the total assistance and beneficiaries, umabot na po tayo sa 606,921.

**[01:59:17.840] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001926)  
I wish also, Ma'am, to emphasize,

**[01:59:20.700] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001927)  
aside from the financial assistance,

**[01:59:22.920] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001928)  
we also have our legal retainers because of the ongoing legal pending and cases we have in our various posts.

**[01:59:34.720] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001929)  
We have 32 legal retainers.

**[01:59:37.220] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001930)  
These are the law firms in the respective jurisdiction.

**[01:59:41.850] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001931)  
And we have our in-house counsels, labing tatlo po yan.

**[01:59:45.800] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001932)  
Sila po ay mga locals.

**[01:59:48.620] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001933)  
The lawyers who are employed and who are engaged right at our migrant workers' offices.

**[02:00:00.050] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001938)  
So, yun po yung ating mga tinutulungan.

**[02:00:03.020] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001939)  
Ganon din po, nabanggit kanina yung medical retainer.

**[02:00:06.120] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001940)  
We have existing special medical arrangements in Abu Dhabi, Jordan, in Qatar,

**[02:00:12.600] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001941)  
and we have a medical retainer in Dubai.

**[02:00:17.620] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001942)  
So, we wish to have more, the engagement of more medical retainers

**[02:00:23.840] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001943)  
para po sa ating mga kababayan na nagkakasakit.

**[02:00:26.840] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001944)  
Doon po sa medical, halos karamihan po sa ating mga kaso sa Middle East and in Asia Pacific,

**[02:00:35.540] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001945)  
yun pong medical na nagkulang sa insurance or the insurance is not sufficient or cannot be covered.

**[02:00:43.580] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001946)  
And at the same time, sa Middle East po,

**[02:00:46.920] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001947)  
ay yung mga

**[02:00:47.440] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001948)  
huroob cases or the undocumented Filipino OFWs,

**[02:00:52.760] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001949)  
which is covered by the Action Fund.

**[02:00:54.480] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001950)  
As the Honorable Chair and Representative Bautista would know,

**[02:00:59.660] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001951)  
ang Action Fund po ay covering documented and undocumented OFWs.

**[02:01:07.260] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001952)  
So, we cater to all our OFWs and we don't discriminate.

**[02:01:12.180] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001953)  
Magandang hapon po muli, ma'am.

**[02:01:14.720] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001954)  
Yes, ma'am. Thank you po.

**[02:01:16.420] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001955)  
Follow-up ko lang po.

**[02:01:17.420] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001956)  
So, kung magkano po ba ang Action Fund currently doon po sa NEP?

**[02:01:23.200] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001957)  
And if based on your assessment, sapat na po ba?

**[02:01:26.020] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001958)  
And kung kailangan po, sa tingin niyo po ay hindi sapat, magkano pa po ba ang kulang?

**[02:01:30.560] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001959)  
Sa tingin niyo, maybe we can justify ngayon po and make it of record.

**[02:01:33.960] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001960)  
Salamat po.

**[02:01:36.680] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001961)  
Ma'am, sa NEP po ay 2 billion yung sa ating Action Fund.

**[02:01:41.200] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001962)  
At kung kami po ay hihiling, at ito po ay additional 1 billion po.

**[02:01:48.100] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001963)  
So, can we explain bakit po natin nire-request yung additional 1 billion fund?

**[02:01:54.680] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001964)  
I believe last year po, 2 billion po ang naibigay.

**[02:01:57.700] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001965)  
Nakita po natin malinaw, kulang na kulang po yan.

**[02:02:00.460] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001966)  
Kaya po nagkaroon ng supplemental.

**[02:02:01.560] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001967)  
Maraming salamat po kay President.

**[02:02:03.580] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001968)  
Pero hopefully, hindi po kailanganin.

**[02:02:05.900] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001969)  
But at the rate that you've seen when we have to respond into emergency crisis such as that,

**[02:02:11.920] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001970)  
maybe meron po tayong justification bakit po 1 billion ang additional na hinihiling po natin.

**[02:02:18.520] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001971)  
Yes, ma'am, the NEP is for 2 billion and ang kahilingan po ng ating Departamento ay an additional of 1 billion

**[02:02:27.720] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001972)  
to cover welfare assistance, financial, medical, repatriation, and our legal assistance.

**[02:02:35.060] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001973)  
Gusto din po namin sanang matapatan or if it can be equivalent or corresponding to what the President has given,

**[02:02:43.720] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001974)  
the additional 3 billion fund.

**[02:02:45.780] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001975)  
Which is to answer and to help us manage with the ongoing geopolitical crisis,

**[02:02:54.580] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001976)  
which also not covers only the land base because we also have a lot of seafaring concerns affecting our sea-based workers

**[02:03:05.380] DMW Undersecretary Felicitas Q. Bay** (asr-001977)  
and other concerns affecting our Filipino workers.

**[02:03:11.280] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001978)  
Yes, ma'am. Thank you, ma'am. Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[02:04:57.260] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002001)

**[02:05:30.720] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002007)

**[02:05:37.180] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002008)

**[02:06:04.740] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002012)

**[02:06:15.140] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002014)

**[02:06:17.660] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002015)  
Ubus na po ang 1.2.

**[02:06:20.120] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002016)

**[02:06:26.120] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002017)

**[02:06:33.960] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002018)

**[02:06:45.100] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002019)

**[02:06:59.140] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002022)

**[02:07:06.240] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002023)  
Ngunit, ang hinihingi po namin, base dun sa aming sulat na ipinadala po sa ating mahal na speaker,

**[02:07:12.660] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002024)

**[02:07:15.060] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002025)  
million to the 900 plus million.

**[02:07:20.980] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002026)

**[02:07:27.320] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002027)

**[02:07:33.100] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002028)

**[02:07:42.320] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002030)

**[02:07:50.440] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002031)

**[02:08:01.760] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-002032)

**[02:08:03.300] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-002033)

**[02:08:29.480] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-002037)

**[02:08:31.900] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-002038)

**[02:08:42.020] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-002040)

**[02:08:44.140] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-002041)

**[02:09:09.560] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-002045)

**[02:09:11.780] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-002046)

**[02:09:23.960] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-002048)

**[02:09:29.680] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-002049)

**[02:09:31.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002050)  
With your permission, Madam Chair, I will start things off.

**[02:09:37.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002051)  
But we have our Assistant Secretary for Reintegration here who can supply the details.

**[02:09:41.580] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002052)  
But essentially, ang framework po natin, kalinga, kaalaman, kabuhayan, at kaagapay.

**[02:09:48.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002053)  
So, yung kalinga is yung psychosocial welfare side sa mga nanunumbalik.

**[02:09:53.200] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002054)  
As I mentioned, when you speak of the U.S.-Israel-Iran war,

**[02:09:58.510] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002055)  
mismo si Presidente, nung sumalubong sa mga nanumbalik,

**[02:10:02.560] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002056)  
nagtanong at may mga nagkwento, iba pa nga sa kanila, mga bata,

**[02:10:05.960] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002057)  
na nakadinig ng pagyanig ng lupa, ng bahay,

**[02:10:10.480] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002058)  
kapag merong falling debris ng projectiles or missile attacks na nainkwentro sila sa host country,

**[02:10:17.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002059)  
sa kasagsagan ng hidwaan.

**[02:10:22.700] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002060)  
So, that's the kalinga portion.

**[02:10:24.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002061)  
And since that time, around 6,000 had been provided this particular welfare assistance,

**[02:10:30.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002062)  
psychosocial counseling, and other welfare interventions.

**[02:10:33.460] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002063)  
Yung kaalaman is yung skills training interventions with DG Kiko Benitez.

**[02:10:39.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002064)  
So, mga also, nearly around 5,000 naman po nabigyan ng serbisyo along these lines.

**[02:10:45.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002065)  
Yung mga nanumbalik para mag-retool, mag-retrain.

**[02:10:47.940] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002066)  
Halimbawa, domestic worker who were 30% of those who returned,

**[02:10:53.420] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002067)  
binigyan ng bagong panimula by way of a barista training sa TESDA.

**[02:10:58.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002068)  
Para siya naman ay hindi lamang domestic worker, kundi maging barista sa isang coffee shop or restaurant.

**[02:11:04.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002069)  
So, meron pong ganitong efforts, 5,000 ay nabiyayaan along these lines.

**[02:11:08.480] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002070)  
And then, meron pong kabuhayan.

**[02:11:10.480] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002071)  
In the first semester, mga higit kumulang 2,000 ang nakapag-avail ng capital assistance,

**[02:11:17.200] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002072)  
not just from DMW OWA, but other agencies such as DTI, DOT, and DA Agriculture.

**[02:11:23.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002073)  
Merong kaagapay, kasi ang ibig sabihin naman ng kaagapay, so 4Ks po ito, no?

**[02:11:28.920] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002074)  
Yung pang-apat na K is kaagapay, kasi si Pangulong Marcos, bago pa nangyari itong sigalot,

**[02:11:35.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002075)  
binuklod na kaming lahat na government agencies.

**[02:11:38.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002076)  
into a national reintegration network.

**[02:11:41.400] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002077)  
Kaya nakita na po ni Presidente na hindi lamang DMW-OWWA, but obviously other government agencies

**[02:11:49.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002078)  
should provide the necessary assistance by way of re-employment, livelihood, or retooling or reskilling.

**[02:11:56.700] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002079)  
For the details dun sa budget, the good news is for the first time during your time in the President's term

**[02:12:05.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002080)  
and this particular Congress.

**[02:12:07.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002081)  
So, naging nine figures in the 100 million.

**[02:12:14.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002082)  
So, maniwala po kayo't hindi, below 100 million lang dati ang MOOE ng National Reintegration Center.

**[02:12:20.820] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002083)  
At hindi lamang po tumuntong ng nine figures, kundi 300 million na po ang incoming budget sa NEP

**[02:12:26.580] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002084)  
ng MOOE ng National Reintegration Center.

**[02:12:29.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002085)  
However, humihingi pa po kami ng additional 300 para tuluyan maging ganap.

**[02:12:35.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002086)  
Kasi yung binabanggit po kanina, pangangailangan,

**[02:12:37.880] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002087)

**[02:12:46.440] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002088)  
So, with your permission, Madam Chair, can I request Asec. Kiko de Guzman

**[02:12:51.440] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002089)

**[02:13:05.480] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002090)  
With the permission of the Secretary, thank you very much to the members of the Committee,

**[02:13:11.940] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002091)  
Doon po sa kasalukuyan, binigyan na po kami ng, ang binibigay po sa amin is 309 million.

**[02:13:19.460] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002092)  
We're projecting approximately 51,000 beneficiaries, both OFW and OFW families.

**[02:13:25.580] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002093)  
And then doon naman po sa karagdagang hiling namin would be 321 million,

**[02:13:32.140] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002094)  
roughly the same number din po ng beneficiary count ang ina-expect.

**[02:13:37.080] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002095)  
For context po, Madam Chair,

**[02:13:39.140] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002096)  
it is, I believe,

**[02:13:40.840] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002097)  
in this same room last November

**[02:13:43.360] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002098)

**[02:13:46.840] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002099)

**[02:13:51.020] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002100)  
Ang sabi po ng iba nating mga kagalang-galang na kongresista,

**[02:13:54.960] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002101)  
ang sabi nila, parang ang hina naman yata

**[02:13:57.580] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002102)  
yung magbigay at magbalik ng benepisyo

**[02:14:01.200] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002103)  
para doon sa mga OFW natin.

**[02:14:03.600] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002104)  
At that time po, ang livelihood assistance po ng DMW

**[02:14:07.120] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002105)

**[02:14:09.420] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002106)

**[02:14:12.680] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002107)

**[02:14:15.980] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002108)

**[02:14:17.780] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002109)

**[02:14:20.800] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002110)

**[02:14:23.540] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002111)  
Pinakinggan po namin iyon,

**[02:14:24.920] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002112)  
as early as January and February of this year,

**[02:14:29.020] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002113)

**[02:14:32.260] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002114)

**[02:14:36.060] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002115)

**[02:14:39.400] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002116)

**[02:14:42.100] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002117)

**[02:14:46.180] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002118)

**[02:14:50.080] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002119)

**[02:14:53.480] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002120)  
sa Kongreso

**[02:14:54.660] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002121)  
at sa Senado na rin kung paano

**[02:14:56.740] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002122)

**[02:14:58.660] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002123)  
ng pagkakataon ng ating mga kababayang

OFW. The programs are already

**[02:15:02.480] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002125)

**[02:15:04.760] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002126)  
And we again

**[02:15:06.460] DMW Assistant Secretary Francis Ron C. De Guzman** (asr-002127)

**[02:23:33.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002379)

**[02:23:38.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002383)

**[02:23:41.520] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002384)

**[02:23:43.420] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002385)  
Ah, yes, ah,

**[02:23:44.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002386)  
we had disclosed figures

**[02:23:46.820] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002387)

**[02:23:47.580] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002388)

**[02:23:50.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002389)  
our, ah, former budget

**[02:23:52.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002390)

**[02:23:56.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002391)

**[02:23:58.380] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002392)

**[02:24:02.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002395)

**[02:24:10.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002396)

**[02:24:16.380] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002397)  
Si Admin PY would have, because they had a study on the impact,

**[02:24:24.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002398)

**[02:24:30.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002399)  
but I will request, if permitted by the Chair, the good administrator to report on this.

**[02:24:37.200] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002400)

**[02:24:39.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002401)

**[02:24:47.380] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002402)

**[02:24:53.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002403)

**[02:24:58.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002404)

**[02:24:59.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002405)

**[02:25:00.580] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002406)

**[02:25:03.280] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002407)

**[02:25:13.240] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002408)

**[02:25:19.520] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002409)  
but the re-employment interventions,

**[02:25:22.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002410)

**[02:25:28.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002411)

**[02:25:29.760] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002412)

**[02:25:36.200] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002413)

**[02:25:40.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002414)

**[02:25:46.440] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002415)

**[02:25:51.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002416)

**[02:25:58.520] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002417)  
So, as

**[02:26:00.380] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002418)

**[02:26:10.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002419)

**[02:26:19.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002420)

**[02:26:29.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002421)

**[02:26:34.580] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002422)  
Thank you very much, Madam Chair, and to our former chair of the committee,

**[02:26:40.260] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002423)  
maraming salamat po sa supporta po ninyo over the years sa amin po ng DMW at OWA,

**[02:26:47.560] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002424)  
at lalong-lalong na po sa ating mga OFWs, hindi ho namin makakalimutan yan.

**[02:26:52.500] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002425)  
To your question, sir, actually po, of course, as you know,

**[02:26:56.820] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002426)  
OWA has the Balik Pinas Balik Hanap Buhay program,

**[02:26:59.760] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002427)  
and the Tulong Puso program po natin, both are sourced from the Trust Fund, Madam Chair.

**[02:27:07.820] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002428)  
Hindi po ito GAA program.

**[02:27:10.200] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002429)  
This is sourced from the contribution of our members.

**[02:27:13.500] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002430)  
Doon po sa aming monitoring, Madam Chair,

**[02:27:19.120] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002431)  
ay close to over 60% ang after 12 months,

**[02:27:25.580] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002432)  
ay meron pa rin po silang existing, no, na,

**[02:27:29.000] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002433)  
negosyo, or meron po silang pathway papunta sa employment.

**[02:27:34.360] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002434)  
Ang nagiging problema po, Madam Chair, or nagiging malaking obstacle,

**[02:27:40.500] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002435)  
is pag nakakaroon po ng expansion, yung kanilang negosyo,

**[02:27:45.220] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002436)  
or pag nakakaroon po ng need for additional technical assistance, no,

**[02:27:52.520] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002437)  
kaya ho napakaganda yung National Reintegration Network.

**[02:27:56.320] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002438)  
Kasi doon ho sa humihingi ng additional capital,

**[02:27:59.300] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002439)  
na ilalapit po namin sila sa Land Bank of the Philippines, sa SB Corp.,

**[02:28:04.920] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002440)  
Madam Chair, na may inooffer na magandang rate para sa ating mga OFWs, no.

**[02:28:10.120] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002441)  
Aside from that, Madam Chair, yun po naman sa training natin, no,

**[02:28:16.300] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002442)  
we have particular upskilling training for our seafarers,

**[02:28:21.260] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002443)  
at para po sa ating mga land-based workers.

**[02:28:24.400] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002444)  
Mabibigay po namin yung mga numero pong ito,

**[02:28:28.260] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002445)  
so, Madam Chair, ang sa seafarers po sa taon pa lang na ito,

**[02:28:33.540] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002446)  
ay umabot na po ng 13,000 ang nakapag-avail nitong seafarers upskilling program po namin.

**[02:28:40.440] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002447)  
At pagdating naman po sa skills for employment, no, na scholarship program po,

**[02:28:45.240] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002448)  
ay halos 3,000 na po ang nakapag-avail nito.

**[02:28:49.260] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002449)  
Ngayon naman po, meron po tayong nilunsad na partnership po ng DMW, CHED,

**[02:28:55.360] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002450)  
at sa atin po sa OWWA,

**[02:28:56.920] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002451)

**[02:29:02.180] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002452)  
Ibig sabihin po, hindi lamang po dependents or anak ng OFW ang tinutulungan ngayon natin

**[02:29:09.940] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002453)  
na magkaroon ng scholarship para magkatapos ng kolehyo,

**[02:29:13.380] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002454)  
pati po yung mga OFWs mismo, kahit po abroad pa sila,

**[02:29:18.820] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002455)  
ay may chance na po silang makatapos ng tertiary education

**[02:29:23.060] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002456)  
at masuportahan rin po natin through scholarship.

**[02:29:26.700] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-002457)  
through the Alagang OWWA Step-Up Program din po, Madam Chair.

**[02:29:31.240] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002458)  
Madam Chair, while we find these interventions commendable, no,

**[02:29:34.940] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002459)  
if I were to suggest to the DMW,

**[02:29:37.820] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002460)  
baka pwedeng as we implement the reintegration policy,

**[02:29:42.200] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002461)  
baka pwedeng ma-anticipate na natin maging proactive yung proposal sana na

**[02:29:47.980] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002462)  
the reintegration pathways are offered before they even

**[02:29:51.420] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002463)  
come to the Philippines.

**[02:29:52.980] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002464)  
Kasi alam ho namin, limitado yung budget na na-assign natin for integration.

**[02:29:58.940] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002465)  
However, if you were more proactive at alam na natin,

**[02:30:01.980] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002466)  
kumbaga meron tayong anticipated number of OFWs na gustong magtrabaho,

**[02:30:07.640] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002467)  
gustong magnegosyo, gustong mag-aral ulit,

**[02:30:10.600] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002468)  
these resources could even be approximated even before the actual return na sa Pilipinas.

**[02:30:18.500] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002469)  
Alam naman ho natin, mga dalawang taon ng kontrata,

**[02:30:22.040] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002470)  
kung wala nang planong mag-renew in the last five months,

**[02:30:24.960] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002471)  
siguro that data should already be relayed so that we really have a more effective.

**[02:30:31.280] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002472)  
Kasi minsan, kung nagbibilang lang tayo ng beneficiaries,

**[02:30:34.960] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002473)  
like yung sinabi kanina ni Asek Kiko,

**[02:30:39.010] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002474)  
yung amount mismo hindi tayo sigurado kung talagang ganun ka-impactful yun

**[02:30:43.760] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002475)  
sa nakasanayang living standard ng ating mga OFWs.

**[02:30:49.100] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002476)  
I really find it unfair na,

**[02:30:51.380] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002477)  
considering their economic contributions,

**[02:30:54.200] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002478)  
if they will have to settle for what only we can afford,

**[02:30:56.980] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002479)  
siguro if we had a more proactive way of determining,

**[02:31:01.580] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002480)  
especially if the full reintegration policy implemented and anticipate the numbers,

**[02:31:07.520] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002481)  
that would be really helpful, even for Congress,

**[02:31:09.860] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-002482)  
for assigning the sufficient funds needed to run the policy.

**[02:31:15.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002483)  
Madam Chair, yes, precisely.

**[02:31:18.280] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002484)  
Maraming salamat po sa tanong, Madam Chair, Mr. Congress.

**[02:31:21.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002485)  
Yan po mismo ang direktiba din ng ating Pangulo

**[02:31:24.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002486)  
nung pinag-bookload kami sa National Reintegration Network.

**[02:31:28.960] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002487)  
At kung naalala niyo po, sa San Juan City,

**[02:31:31.760] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002488)  
the President nagpasinaya ng paglunsad ng DICT ng eGov app

**[02:31:39.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002489)  
kung saan nandun po kami lahat ng National Reintegration Network Agencies.

**[02:31:44.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002490)  
At ang direksyon po na ito ay papunta sa isang virtual one-stop shop for reintegration.

**[02:31:51.300] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002491)  
This will be the foremost tool, Madam Chair, Mr. Congressman,

**[02:31:56.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002492)  
for the workers to know the pathways.

**[02:31:59.520] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002493)  
Kung may virtual one-stop shop,

**[02:32:01.720] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002494)  
halimbawa kung ako po'y one weekend ako po sa Saudi Arabia

**[02:32:05.400] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002495)  
at in my spare time, habang nasa coffee shop ako,

**[02:32:10.100] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002496)  
I surf and go through EGOV or a virtual one-stop shop,

**[02:32:15.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002497)  
doon ko malalaman yung pathways antimano habang nandun pa ako sa Saudi

**[02:32:19.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-002498)
