**[02:57:20.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003069)  
Saan po ba napupunta yung mga kinukolekta nating verification fees? At magkano ang nakolekta?

**[02:57:28.300] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003070)  
Yes, with your permission, I will ask Usec. Niki to respond.

**[02:57:32.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003071)  
But let me respond first to the matter on gender sensitivity training.

**[02:57:36.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003072)  
Rest assured, Madam Congresswoman, Madam Chair,

**[02:57:38.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003073)  
wala po tayong personel na umaalis na hindi nakakadaan sa gender sensitivity, gender responsiveness training.

**[02:57:48.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003074)  
Napakahalaga po sa amin yan.

**[02:57:50.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003075)  
As we always like to say, a substantial number, more than a vast majority of our workers assisted are female workers.

**[02:58:02.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003076)  
And not just female workers, but those who also belong in the LGBTQ plus sector.

**[02:58:09.250] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003077)  
Kaya dito rin po ang napakahalaga ng gender responsiveness on our part.

**[02:58:15.520] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003078)  
And we do not tolerate any form of mishandling of any case along these lines, sa gender insensitivity ng ating personnel.

**[02:58:27.760] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003079)  
At kaduktong din po dito yung aspeto ng medical retainership at yung binabanggit ni Admin PY na mental health issues.

**[02:58:34.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003080)  
Kasi, when we are in the hands of expert

**[02:58:37.500] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003081)  
medical care, such as a medical retainer,

**[02:58:40.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003082)  
then we are assured na ang handling ng kaso, let's say physical or emotional abuse,

**[02:58:47.760] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003083)  
ng isang female worker or LGBTQ plus worker is well handled or managed kapag medical expert ang nagahandle.

**[02:58:56.700] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003084)  
Kaya mahalaga po yung binabanggit na medical retainership because we want the medical expertise of a person,

**[02:59:05.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003085)  
of a health worker, to take care of these types of cases.

**[02:59:08.100] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003086)  
Now, dun po sa verification fee, yes, it is under the DMW.

**[02:59:13.960] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003087)  
For every contract verified, there is a US$10 fee.

**[02:59:21.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003088)  
This is under the auspices of the DMW.

**[02:59:24.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003089)  
It is a collected fee that partially reverts back to our operations.

**[02:59:32.520] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003090)  
And I will ask Usec. Niki

**[02:59:34.060] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003091)  
to discuss this matter.

**[02:59:38.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003092)  
So, with the permission of the Chair,

**[02:59:40.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003093)  
Usec. Niki can take up the matter on the verification fees.

**[02:59:46.740] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003094)  
Thank you, Madam Chair, and with the permission of the Secretary po.

**[02:59:50.200] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003095)  
Ang current po natin ngayon for verification fee from fiscal year 2023 to 2026 present, meaning January to July po, it's around 5,851,298,096.

**[03:00:12.520] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003096)  
And these are deposited in the Bureau of Treasury po. And from that amount po, meron po tayong in-earmarked under our GAA.

**[03:00:24.200] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003097)  
So for 2024 po, we earmarked around 163 million. And then in 2025, that's 580 million. And for 2026, another 580 million.

**[03:00:41.500] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003098)  
But under the 2027 NEP po, around 700 million po, 777 to be exact ang in-earmarked po under the 2027 NEP.

**[03:00:54.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003099)  
Madam Chair, meron pang more than 2 billion pesos na nasa Treasury na hindi pa po earmarked for particular purposes.

**[03:01:03.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003100)  
Ngayon Madam Chair, kasi meron din po tayong mga reports ng increases sa verification fees.

**[03:01:09.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003101)  
Ano po yung mga factors kung bakit kailangan

**[03:01:11.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003102)  
pong magtaas nitong mga verification fees?

**[03:01:16.800] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003103)  
From what we've known, Your Honor, we have not increased the verification fee. It's pegged at 10 US dollars as mentioned by the Secretary.

**[03:01:28.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003104)  
Alright. Kung maaari niyo pong magbibigay po ako sa inyo ng ulat tungkol po sa mga nakuha namin na reports ng increase.

**[03:01:35.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003105)  
Halimbawa po sa Hong Kong, sa Singapore, meron pong reports ng increases.

**[03:01:41.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003106)  
Pwede pong ma-review na lang po ito ng DMW.

**[03:01:44.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003107)  
Ngayon, atungkol naman Madam Chair sa cut sa budget ng Maritime Research and Skills Competency Program na nakapasok ang Maritime Training and Maritime Assessment Service,

**[03:01:56.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003108)  
paano po maapekto ka ng budget cut na ito ang existing services natin para sa international seafarers?

**[03:02:04.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003109)  
We have the Executive Director of the National Maritime Polytechnic here, but let me start it off as well.

**[03:02:12.520] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003110)  
We have made significant strides sa 19th and 20th Congresses sa budget ng NMP. So we're very thankful.

**[03:02:21.060] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003111)  
Dahil dito, nagkaroon ng early phase 1 ng infrastructure improvements for the first time in 50 years.

**[03:02:30.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003112)  
Started during Marcos Sr.'s administration and finally resumed under Marcos Jr.'s administration na improve ang facilities sa National Maritime Polytechnic.

**[03:02:41.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003113)  
For the first time, meron mga certain training programs that have been rendered free to our seafarers.

**[03:02:49.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003114)  
Hindi lang discounted or subsidized, completely free na certain programs and our Executive Director will provide the details on this.

**[03:02:57.460] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003115)  
So without further ado, Madam Chair, Madam Congresswoman, may request ED Victor Rosario to explain yung budget adjustments effect ng NEP sa NMP.

**[03:03:10.140] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003116)  
Thank you.

**[03:03:11.080] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003117)  
Madam Chair, Your Honors, with the permission of the Good Secretary, first of all, we would like to express our profound gratitude to Congress because it is only in this year that we received the highest budgetary allocation for the National Maritime Polytechnic since our inception in 1978.

**[03:03:32.780] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003118)  
We have received 406 million for this year and 271 million of which will...

**[03:03:41.200] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003119)  
Fund the implementation of our modernization plan at NMP Tacloban, basically to improve our infrastructure for our training facilities.

**[03:03:52.780] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003120)  
This October, we will be groundbreaking our 200 million dormitory with the President and the Secretary.

**[03:03:59.940] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003121)  
We will also be constructing a new training pool of 32 million, another new firefighting area and many other infrastructure program.

**[03:04:09.300] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003122)  
To implement our...

**[03:04:11.380] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003123)  
modernization plan.

**[03:04:12.620] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003124)  
And to answer the question of the Good Congresswoman Elago as to the decrease of the budget of NMP, primarily it's because of the reduced capital outlay.

**[03:04:24.020] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003125)  
Kasi napagbigyan na po tayo ng DBM ng 271 million for this year.

**[03:04:28.980] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003126)  
Now for next year, binigyan po tayo ng 53 million, 50 million of which will go to the construction of another training building.

**[03:04:38.220] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003127)  
Sapagkat kailangan po talaga natin, Congresswoman, yung...

**[03:04:41.520] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003128)  
Mga bagong...

**[03:04:42.900] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003129)  
Nang karagdagang training classroom sapagkat, as we speak, mayroon po tayong 40-year-old main training building sa NMP na ginawa pa ng mga Japanese, ng JICA.

**[03:04:57.260] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003130)  
At currently, we have 26 rooms.

**[03:05:00.220] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003131)  
Nine rooms are used for simulator and our laboratory rooms where we store and house our simulator.

**[03:05:09.860] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003132)  
Sixteen lang po yung...

**[03:05:11.200] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003133)  
nagagamit natin for instruction.

**[03:05:13.200] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003134)  
And with the approval of the Good Secretary for our free training program, we expect more enrollment next year.

**[03:05:21.240] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003135)  
That is why kailangan po talaga natin ng mga karagdagang classrooms.

**[03:05:25.780] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003136)  
Pero yung napagbigyan po sa DBM is only 50 million of the 125 million that we proposed that will enable us sana to construct additional 15 classrooms.

**[03:05:40.100] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003137)  
Kaya po nakikiusap...

**[03:05:41.560] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003138)  
po tayo sa Kongreso.

**[03:05:42.500] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003139)  
Let me take this opportunity.

**[03:05:43.720] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003140)  
Isa lang po yung wish list ng NMP na sana ma-restore yung 76 million na kakulangan para makonstruct natin yung bagong training building.

**[03:05:53.960] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003141)  
Kasi nga po, dun sa free training program na in-approve po ni Secretary pursuant to DMW Memorandum Order 360,

**[03:06:03.440] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003142)  
simula po nung August 3, lahat po ng regular training program ng NMP ay libre na po.

**[03:06:09.560] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003143)  
Libre na po.

**[03:06:10.480] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003144)  
Wala na pong bayad.

**[03:06:11.920] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003145)  
Talagang libre.

**[03:06:13.440] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003146)  
We have 55 courses.

**[03:06:15.180] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003147)  
And as a matter of fact, marami pong trainees na nanggagaling pa sa Zamboanga, pumupunta sa Tacloban.

**[03:06:23.960] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003148)  
It's a long story bakit yung NMP ay nasa Tacloban, but to cut it short, it's because of the former First Lady Imelda Romaldes Marcos.

**[03:06:32.040] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003149)  
That is why it's situated at the foot of San Juanico.

**[03:06:34.780] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003150)  
And that is why...

**[03:06:38.760] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003151)  
Mr. Chair.

**[03:06:39.935] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003152)  
Sir, pwede ako?

**[03:06:42.567] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003153)  
Mr. Chair, let me...

**[03:06:44.540] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003154)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[03:06:45.520] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003155)  
And as of August 3, we have already trained 3,026 seafarers that availed our free training program.

**[03:06:58.280] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003156)  
And we are expecting, with the support of Secretary Cacdac, we've been given additional funding for our honoraria.

**[03:07:04.540] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003157)  
We will be training maximum of 12,000 seafarers by the end of December.

**[03:07:11.040] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003158)  
From August to...

**[03:07:13.060] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003159)  
December.

**[03:07:16.360] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003160)  
Mr. Congressman, to answer your question, we are also exerting effort for our expansion plan.

**[03:07:25.000] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003161)  
As a matter of fact, Congressman Revilla, your mother, attended our presentation of our feasibility study for the establishment of a regional training center in Tanza, Cavite.

**[03:07:39.700] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003162)  
Yes, kasi may lupa po yung NMP doon sa Cavite.

**[03:07:43.630] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003163)  
And another feasibility study, Mr. Congressman, Madam Chair, we will conduct it this year with the Development Academy of the Philippines.

**[03:07:52.800] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003164)  
We will also be constructing, establishing an RTC in Davao City, and thereafter, CDO.

**[03:07:59.300] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003165)  
We will also take into consideration Cagayan de Oro City, Madam Chair.

**[03:08:05.540] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003166)  
That would be all, Madam Chair.

**[03:08:07.340] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003167)  
I hope I was able to satisfy the question of the good Congresswoman.

**[03:08:10.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003168)  
Yes, maraming salamat.

**[03:08:12.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003169)  
Nagalala rin po tayo, hindi lang doon sa mga pangangailangan sa bagong infrastruktura, para sa mas maraming training centers.

**[03:08:19.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003170)  
Pero yung pambayad po natin sa mga personnel, syempre marami po dyan.

**[03:08:23.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003171)  
Hindi naman po dapat lang na honorarya, pero kung maaari, meron din tayong mga personnel na regular, may security of tenure,

**[03:08:33.800] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003172)  
at talagang mapagkakatiwalaan natin sa iba't ibang mga pangangailangan na may kinalaman sa maritime training and maritime assessment services.

**[03:08:42.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003173)  
Ngayon naman, Madam Chair, panghuli na lang po ng mga katanungan tungkol po sa mga services para sa ating seafarers.

**[03:08:50.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003174)  
Bago ho ako magsimula sa mga katanungan na ito, meron na po bang update tungkol po doon sa ating search operations sa nawawala po na seafarer natin, si Sir George Miranda,

**[03:09:04.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003175)  
na napabalita kung nawawalaan itong March 2026 dahil po sa pag-atake sa kanilang tugboat sa Strait of Hormuz.

**[03:09:15.380] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003176)  
Yes, unfortunately, wala pa po tayong kaukulang update.

**[03:09:22.100] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003177)  
It's a function of kung saan po nangyari yung attack.

**[03:09:26.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003178)  
It's in the Strait of Hormuz, kaya admittedly may kahirapan po sa pag-salvage nung tugboat kung saan siya naroon.

**[03:09:38.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003179)  
So we still are hoping na kahit pa pano, darating ang panahon na magkaroon din ng...

**[03:09:45.820] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003180)  
salvaging ng tugboat kapag ligtas na ang sitwasyon sa Strait of Hormuz.

**[03:09:52.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003181)  
Ang sinasagawa po natin, I had personally met with Mrs. Miranda,

**[03:09:56.580] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003182)  
at ang sinasagawa po natin ngayon is tinutulungan natin ng pamilya ng filing ng legal claims.

**[03:10:06.400] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003183)  
Kasi po, meron na pong discussion with the ship owner in Dubai.

**[03:10:13.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003184)  
Kaya meron na po tayong abogadong

**[03:10:16.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003185)  
naatasan with the acceptance of Mrs. Miranda, kung saan yung lawyer po natin na nakikipag-usap ngayon sa ship owner doon.

**[03:10:24.560] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003186)  
Kaya ongoing po ito, Madam Congresswoman, and rest assured, I personally have been in contact with Mrs. Miranda.

**[03:10:32.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003187)  
Naka-WhatsApp exchange ko lang po siya nung nakaraang araw.

**[03:10:37.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003188)  
At hindi po natin ito bibitawan until magkaroon ng conclusion itong legal claims ng pamilya sa shipowner.

**[03:10:47.200] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003189)  
Pero aaminin ko po, sa ngayon po ay hindi po talaga na-recover yung human remains ni Oiler Miranda.

**[03:10:58.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003190)  
Kaya patuloy pa rin tayong nag-umaasa na darating ang panahon na masasalvage kahit pa pano yung tugboat na kinalalagyan niya doon sa Strait of Hormuz.

**[03:11:19.660] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003191)  
Yes, there is a document po here and I think we can provide...

**[03:11:25.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003192)  
you a copy of a note verbal along these lines, but for the sanctity of the grief that Mrs. Miranda is going through,

**[03:11:36.240] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003193)  
we will just furnish you a copy of this document from the Omani government, Madam Congresswoman.

**[03:11:43.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003194)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Chair.

**[03:11:45.800] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003195)  
At hindi po tayo nawawala ng pag-asa.

**[03:11:50.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003196)  
Pagdating po sa mga kaso ng nawawala na seafarer,

**[03:11:55.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003197)  
pati na rin po sa kaso ng iba pa nating OFWs in distress,

**[03:12:00.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003198)  
nagpapaabot din po tayo ng pakikiramay doon sa recent lang po na napabalita na meron din po si seafarer na isang deceased.

**[03:12:09.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003199)  
Habang meron po tayong mga na-rescue, meron din po isang namatay dahil nga sa kalagayan sa Strait of Hormuz.

**[03:12:18.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003200)  
Kaya po, sa tingin ko, Madam Chair, habang tayo po ay nanghihingi dito ng status update,

**[03:12:25.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003201)  
Mga kinakailangan na information para mapalakas yung mga programa natin para sa OFW at seafarer's protection.

**[03:12:34.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003202)  
Kinakailangan din po talaga yung pondo para sa reintegration program.

**[03:12:40.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003203)  
Kasi sa huli't huli, yung kailangan naman natin talaga regular at disenteng trabaho dito sa Pilipinas para sa ating mga kababayan.

**[03:12:50.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003204)  
Nakabubuhay na sahod.

**[03:12:51.620] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003205)  
Kaya malaki ang role ng reintegration program dyan.

**[03:12:56.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003206)  
Dahil po naubusan ako ng oras, ah, hingin na lang po natin yung report mula sa DMW.

**[03:13:03.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003207)  
Bukod dun sa mga proposed nila na mga programs na hindi po nakasama sa NEP para sa reintegration program at para sa action fund,

**[03:13:13.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003208)  
hingin din po natin yung mga kasulukuyan nilang pakikipagtulungan sa DOLE, sa DTI,

**[03:13:18.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003209)  
para ho matiyak na meron talagang trabaho dito sa Pilipinas, may tulong para sa pangkabuhayan yung ating mga kababayan.

**[03:13:26.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003210)  
At panghuli, yung mga kababayan naman po natin naghihintay pa po ng sagot ng pagdating sa request nila sa action fund mula pa po January-February nitong taong 2026.

**[03:13:41.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003211)  
Sana po ay matugunan na ng ating DMW, ng OWWA at lahat ng mga kasama nating agencies ngayong hapon.

**[03:13:49.380] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003212)  
Maraming salamat Madam Chair.

**[03:13:50.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003213)  
At sana po hindi tayo pumayag na

**[03:13:54.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003214)  
hindi po tumaas

**[03:13:55.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003215)  
Ang pondo ng DMW, ng OWWA, ng lahat po na nandirito mula sa 2027 proposed NEP.

**[03:14:02.800] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003216)  
Sana po may magandang balita pagdating po sa 2027 General Appropriations Bill sa plenaryo.

**[03:14:08.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003217)  
Maraming salamat.

**[03:14:11.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003218)  
Madam Chair, if I may respond.

**[03:14:15.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003219)  
Yes, magbigay na rin po ako ng kabuong ulat about the Strait of Hormuz.

**[03:14:21.420] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003220)  
While around 3,000 seafarers have already exited out of the Strait of Hormuz,

**[03:14:26.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003221)  
ay merong mga 28 ships that had been attacked the Strait with around 380 seafarers on board.

**[03:14:38.240] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003222)  
Karamihan po sa kanila ay naka-uwi na, 256 ay naka-uwi na.

**[03:14:45.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003223)  
Most of them are unharmed.

**[03:14:47.720] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003224)  
367 are unharmed out of the 379.

**[03:14:50.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003225)  
however, 10 injured, one missing nga po, and we will provide the note verbal

**[03:14:56.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003226)  
from the Omani government on the latest.

**[03:15:00.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003227)  
And yes, I can confirm na three days ago, we had our second incident na, and this time

**[03:15:08.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003228)  
confirmed fatality po, sa Strait of Hormuz.

**[03:15:11.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003229)  
But rest assured, I personally had conveyed the condolences in behalf of the President

**[03:15:18.580] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003230)  
to the family.

**[03:15:20.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003231)  
I have met the family for myself and assured them of sympathies and support.

**[03:15:25.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003232)  
And meron pong bilang pong na awaiting repatriation pa rin po, around 53.

**[03:15:34.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003233)  
So 256 have been repatriated with 53 awaiting repatriation dito sa Strait of Hormuz.

**[03:15:42.580] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003234)  
And then doon naman po sa mga barko sa Persian Gulf, we continue.

**[03:15:48.380] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003235)  
To provide, there are still around 3,000 seafarers remaining in the Persian Gulf.

**[03:15:53.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003236)  
At meron naman po tayong patuloy na coordination with the ship owners to provide basic food

**[03:15:58.660] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003237)  
and supplies doon sa mga ships na nag-carrying Filipino seafarers still within the Gulf,

**[03:16:05.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003238)  
Persian Gulf, para patuloy po ang pag-replenish ng kanilang food and water and basic supplies

**[03:16:10.880] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003239)  
habang nandun sila sa Persian Gulf, awaiting exit.

**[03:16:15.440] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-003240)  
Thank you so much, Sec. Hans.

**[03:16:17.780] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-003242)

**[03:16:28.940] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-003243)

**[03:16:30.100] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-003244)

Mas mura na pong bumili kesa i-maintain yung ganito pong klaseng sasakyan.

**[03:30:53.900] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003620)  
Pangalawa po is when it comes to MOOE, so this is 322.006 million.

**[03:31:00.540] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003621)  
Number two is the MOOE operations at 1.180 billion.

**[03:31:07.080] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003622)  
Ano pong kasama dito?

**[03:31:08.540] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003623)  
Yung kanina po po nating inuulit na emergency repatriation fund at 575 million

**[03:31:15.860] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003624)  
at ang Alagang OWWA na programa.

**[03:31:18.540] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003625)  
Ito po, Madam Chair, dear Congressman, dito po kinukuha ang mga binabayad natin

**[03:31:25.600] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003626)  
para po sa mga OFW lounges at ang Seafarer Welfare Center

**[03:31:31.700] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003627)  
na nakamandato po under the Magna Carta for Seafarers.

**[03:31:36.140] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003628)  
Sa ngayon po, nasa NCR pa lang po tayo when it comes to the Seafarer Welfare Center

**[03:31:41.920] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003629)  
at ayon po sa Magna Carta for Seafarers,

**[03:31:45.260] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003630)  
kailangan ho natin magtayo.

**[03:31:47.100] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003631)  
ng walo pa pong Seafarer Welfare Center.

**[03:31:50.580] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003632)  
Isa na ho dyan ang sa Cagayan de Oro City.

**[03:31:57.160] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003633)  
Isa rin po dyan ay sa Cavite.

**[03:32:07.260] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003634)  
Importante po rin, Madam Chair and dear Congressman,

**[03:32:13.120] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003635)  
ito pong ating step-up na programa,

**[03:32:16.220] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003636)  
ang upskilling po ng ating mga OFWs na bumalik po sa ating bayan

**[03:32:21.320] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003637)  
para po makahanap sila ng magandang oportunidad.

**[03:32:24.120] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003638)  
Ang Alagang OWWA po ay nasa 475 million.

**[03:32:28.260] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003639)  
At maaasahan kung maibibigay ho sa OWWA.

**[03:32:31.480] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003640)  
Ang alagang OWWA, additional 475 million.

**[03:32:35.760] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003641)  
Pitong Seafarer Welfare Center po ang itatayo natin next year

**[03:32:40.140] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003642)  
sa Luzon, Visayas at Mindanao para sa ating mga marino

**[03:32:44.640] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003643)  
at supporta sa iba't ibang airport lounges,

**[03:32:48.260] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003644)  
lalong-lalo na po sa mga regional airport lounges

**[03:32:51.620] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003645)  
na tumatanggap sa ating mga OFWs din pong bumalik.

**[03:32:55.400] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003648)  
meron din po tayo,

**[03:32:56.800] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003649)  
Madam Chair,

**[03:32:58.930] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003650)  
Dear Congressman,

**[03:33:00.060] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003651)  
ang tinatawag po nating Global Centers Abroad,

**[03:33:02.860] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003652)  
nakita po ito ng iba po nating miyembro ng House of Representatives

**[03:33:06.740] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003653)  
sa Hong Kong, sa Taipei, sa Malaysia.

**[03:33:11.930] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003654)  
At bubuksan na rin po natin ang sa Oman

**[03:33:14.680] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003655)  
at sana po ay makabukas din po tayo sa Dubai at Singapore.

**[03:33:19.280] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003656)  
When can the other members like us here present visit those places?

**[03:33:24.120] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003657)  
As soon as possible po.

**[03:33:26.640] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003658)  
Pwedeng-pwede na po dahil ongoing po ito.

**[03:33:29.800] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003659)  
We will request the Speaker to finance those present today

**[03:33:33.400] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003660)  
to go around because you have stayed on until the very last of this committee.

**[03:33:39.460] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003661)  
Thank you. So please proceed.

**[03:33:40.840] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003662)  
Thank you very much po, Madam Chair, Dear Congressman.

**[03:33:44.300] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003663)  
Dito po, napaka-importante po ng Global Centers.

**[03:33:47.840] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003664)  
Ma-example ko lang po ang sa Hong Kong.

**[03:33:50.800] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003665)  
Over 250,000 po nating mga kababayan.

**[03:33:54.960] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003666)  
Pag Sunday po,

**[03:33:55.860] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003667)  
ay nag-o-offer.

**[03:33:57.160] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003668)  
Actually, di lang po Sunday,

**[03:33:58.900] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003669)  
pero alam nyo naman po pag day-off nila, pag Sunday.

**[03:34:01.500] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003670)  
Doon po tulong-tulong ang iba't-ibang ahensya ng gobyerno.

**[03:34:05.200] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003671)  
Nagbibigay po tayo ng training.

**[03:34:06.640] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003672)  
Kahit na ho assessment, doon na rin ho ginagawa.

**[03:34:09.380] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003673)  
Para po sa ating mga kababayan,

**[03:34:11.760] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003674)  
para pagbalik nila, upskilled na sila.

**[03:34:14.000] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003675)  
Hindi nasisimulan pa lang sa ating, dito sa ating bansa.

**[03:34:18.340] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003676)  
At hindi lamang po yun.

**[03:34:20.180] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003677)  
Meron din po tayo doong mga medical mission,

**[03:34:23.300] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003678)  
care of mga volunteer, doctors,

**[03:34:25.480] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003679)  
po, na sa Hong Kong.

**[03:34:27.200] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003680)  
Meron din po tayong, of course, legal assistance.

**[03:34:30.120] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003681)  
At yung iba't-iba po nating mga community events para sa Philcom.

**[03:34:34.960] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003682)  
Dito na po sa center natin, sa Hong Kong,

**[03:34:37.540] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003683)  
ginagawa similar to Taipei at saka sa Kuala Lumpur,

**[03:34:41.400] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003684)  
na sana ho, mabisita po ninyo.

**[03:34:45.920] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003685)  
Pangatlo po ay ang Workers' Auxiliary Support Project.

**[03:34:50.440] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003686)  
Madam Chair, dito po papasok ang medical retainers na gustong idagdag ng OWWA.

**[03:34:55.460] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003687)  
Para po sa mga kababayan natin na yung iba ho,

**[03:34:59.340] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003688)  
hindi na covered ng insurance,

**[03:35:01.080] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003689)  
ay kailangan po ng medical assistance, no.

**[03:35:04.800] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003690)  
Katuwang ang Department of Migrant Workers.

**[03:35:07.740] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003691)  
So, maasahan niyo, aside from legal assistance,

**[03:35:11.240] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003692)  
makapag-provide na rin po tayo ng medical retainers, no.

**[03:35:16.120] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003693)  
Pangatlo po, katulad po ng DMW, Capital Outlay po.

**[03:35:21.860] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003694)  
Lumang-luma na ho ang building ng OWWA.

**[03:35:24.860] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003695)  
Madam Chair, meron na po kami mga built-in waterfalls dahil sa bagyo.

**[03:35:31.160] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003696)  
Actually po, to be serious about it,

**[03:35:36.000] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003697)  
we had two assessments made by the Department of Public Works and Highways, DPWH.

**[03:35:43.660] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003698)  
The assessment po came back.

**[03:35:46.300] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003699)  
And we will present to the committee.

**[03:35:50.100] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003700)  
So, apparently, we are only at

**[03:35:54.060] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003701)  
10% of the minimum standard for the structural integrity to face seismic risk.

**[03:36:03.670] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003702)  
So, it's more of the safety na po ng ating mga frontliners,

**[03:36:07.840] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003703)  
especially po yung ating mga OFW clients.

**[03:36:10.860] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003704)  
So, we are seeking as well support, Madam Chair.

**[03:36:14.980] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003705)  
Is this for the construction, 50, or renovation?

**[03:36:17.100] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003706)  
This is for renovation.

**[03:36:20.220] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003707)  
And of course, construction also of

**[03:36:24.500] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003708)  
Regional offices, particularly Region 5 po, nabigyan po tayo ng lupa para dito.

**[03:36:31.580] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003709)  
And then, of course, the extension for the training center and yung iba pa po nating facilities for Region 2.

**[03:36:42.220] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003710)  
The Seafarer Welfare Center, sir.

**[03:36:44.980] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003711)  
And the new office, sabi po ni Eugene.

**[03:36:50.490] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003712)  
Number 4 po, Madam Chair, dear Congressman,

**[03:36:55.640] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003713)  
I invite you to reminisce a little bit.

**[03:36:58.900] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003714)  
RA 8042-1995, the Congress created a program called the Congressional Migrant Worker Scholarship Program.

**[03:37:09.580] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003715)  
But at that time, it was funded by PCSO, 150 million and 50 million from the CDF back then.

**[03:37:20.660] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003716)  
But from that time until now, wala na pong naging restoration of this budget.

**[03:37:27.000] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003717)  
This is supporting po, not just dependents, but also OFWs themselves.

**[03:37:32.720] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003718)  
Up to 60,000 po a year ang binibigay for 4 years.

**[03:37:36.580] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003719)  
Malaki pong tulong ito.

**[03:37:37.940] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003720)  
Ito po ay compared to the regular scholarship program of OWWA, which is sourced from the trust fund, the private trust fund of OWWA.

**[03:37:47.300] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003721)  
Ito po ay GAA-sourced.

**[03:37:49.260] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003722)  
Dito po ay pwede nating i-open up at maging mas accessible.

**[03:37:55.600] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003723)  
Ang scholarship para po sa ating pong mga kababayan, marami po talaga ang humihingi ng tulong, Madam Chair,

**[03:38:03.620] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003724)  
sa pagpapaaral ng kanilang mga anak at syempre sa sarili po rin nila.

**[03:38:08.640] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003725)  
With that po, our total is at 2 billion pesos.

**[03:38:13.220] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003726)  
Again, MOOE gas at 322 million.

**[03:38:16.840] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003727)  
MOOE operations at 1.18 billion.

**[03:38:21.040] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003728)  
Capital outlay at 447.99 million.

**[03:38:25.440] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003729)  
And the Congressional Migrant Workers Scholarship Program at an additional 50 million.

**[03:38:30.720] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-003730)  
Thank you very much, Madam Chair, dear Congressman.

**[03:38:32.120] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003731)  
Thank you, PY Caunan.

**[03:38:34.040] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003732)  
Before I would move for the approval of the increase of 2 billion each, I would like to just state I have here a constituent.

**[03:38:44.360] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003733)  
The problem in the Kingdom of Saudi Arabia, there is the non-issuance of resident permit, ICAMA.

**[03:38:52.920] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003734)  
Could you explain this one first?

**[03:38:54.600] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003735)  
What is this ICAMA not issued by the government of the Kingdom?

**[03:39:01.960] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003736)  
Who should be able to have our residents there work and transact business?

**[03:39:10.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003737)  
Yes, in general po, this is required for every OFW who goes there.

**[03:39:15.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003738)  
So every OFW has what you call an ICAMA, residence permit.

**[03:39:19.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003739)  
However, it can happen na nagkakaroon ng legal problems.

**[03:39:24.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003740)  
Ang mga kababayan natin, most likely related sa kanilang stay, maaring nag-overstay, o di kaya perceived violations of their stay.

**[03:39:39.770] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003741)  
So, ang key po dito, in brief, Mr. Chair, is the legal assistance through the AKSYON Fund.

**[03:39:45.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003742)  
So, the good news is kami ni Usec. Jun Rasul just came from Jeddah and we met our lawyers in Saudi.

**[03:39:54.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003743)  
The Tebiah Law Office who provides legal assistance, they have an array of 30 lawyers in their law firm who provide assistance to our workers.

**[03:40:05.860] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003744)  
So, we easily refer these cases na hindi maka-renew ng ICAMA for immigration-related problems or labor-related problems one way or another.

**[03:40:17.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003745)  
So, rest assured that we address this situation.

**[03:40:20.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003746)  
Some cases take longer than others, admittedly.

**[03:40:23.880] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003747)  
But rest assured, Mr. Congressman, Madam Chair, we are on top of the legal cases that are being handled by our law firm, our legal retainers, our in-house lawyers.

**[03:40:38.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003748)  
And also, kung merong utang po, halimbawa, sa ospital or immigration-related penalties, we shoulder these expenses for our workers.

**[03:40:50.680] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003749)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[03:40:54.300] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003750)  
I now move for the approval of the budget of the DMW and the OWWA, plus 2 billion each.

**[03:41:05.820] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003751)  
Because we have to convince the Speaker and our Chairperson that this is a department which should all help.

**[03:41:12.520] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003752)  
After all, we call our workers, overseas workers, our heroes.

**[03:41:16.360] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003753)  
And so, we should be able to work on that.

**[03:41:19.160] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003754)  
So, with our Chair, we will be able to do that.

**[03:41:21.460] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003755)  
So, move, Madam Chair.

**[03:41:23.060] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-003756)  
Thank you so much, Honorable Rufus Rodriguez.

**[03:41:30.600] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-003757)  
Though we very much appreciate the motion, we want to remind everyone that this is, our proceeding today is just a budget briefing.

**[03:41:38.540] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-003758)  
And, of course, the ruling would be considered in the proper time.

**[03:41:47.300] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003759)  
Proper time.

**[03:41:47.840] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-003760)  
Yes.

**[03:41:48.300] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003761)  
Thank you.

**[03:41:49.480] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-003762)  
Thank you.

**[03:41:50.920] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-003763)  
Thank you so much, Honorable Rufus Rodriguez.

**[03:41:53.340] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-003764)  
Not just for your questions, but also for giving.

**[03:41:56.860] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-003766)  
life to this proceeding.

**[03:42:00.180] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-003768)  
Even Secretary Hans was excited to answer your questions.

**[03:42:06.760] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-003769)  
I guess now we move on to our next interpolator.

**[03:42:09.880] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-003770)  
Sorry, before we proceed with the next interpolator, I would like to recognize our House members present here today.

**[03:42:16.820] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-003771)  
We have Vice Chair Bella Vanessa Suansing and Representative Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan.

**[03:42:25.180] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-003772)  
Magandang hapon po.

**[03:42:28.940] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-003773)  
All right.

**[03:42:29.540] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-003774)  
Next to interpellate from the majority, we have Representative Florabel Yatco.

**[03:42:36.680] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003775)  
Good afternoon, Secretary, Admin PY.

**[03:42:40.980] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003776)  
Itatanong ko lang naman yung tulad nung nabanggit kanina ni Congressman Tina.

**[03:42:46.240] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003777)  
It's about the OFW.

**[03:42:47.800] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003778)  
Like during 25-26, ilang pamilya at OFW ang nasilbihan natin dito sa hospital na ito?

**[03:42:57.320] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003779)  
At for...

**[03:42:58.480] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003780)  
2027 na budget natin, ano po ang mga kongkretong improvements na maaaring nilang asahan sa pagdating sa mga access services at sa quality care ng OFW?

**[03:43:14.960] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003781)  
I will request Usec. Niki Tutay who handles the hospital to answer the details.

**[03:43:21.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003782)  
But at the outset, 184,870 po ang napagsilbihan.

**[03:43:28.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003783)  
But this is beginning...

**[03:43:30.040] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003784)  
Yung term ng ating mahal na Pangulo, ang panahon po natin sa Department of Migrant Workers.

**[03:43:37.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003785)  
We can provide the necessary breakdowns on a year-to-year basis.

**[03:43:42.820] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003786)  
And yung kaukulang budget po na binigay sa atin is 423.59 million.

**[03:43:52.420] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003787)  
However, as mentioned during the question of Congressman Rodriguez,

**[03:43:58.940] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003788)  
we are seeking an additional...

**[03:44:00.800] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003789)  
200.

**[03:44:01.960] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003790)  
million.

**[03:44:04.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003791)  
Can I request Usec. Niki, Madam Chair, to provide some more details?

**[03:44:12.860] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003792)  
'Yun pong nabanggit po ng ating kalihim ay 'yung number of patients, unique patients po ito.

**[03:44:18.940] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003793)  
So it could be the OFW herself, either a family also, or pwede rin po yung parents po ng OFWs.

**[03:44:26.160] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003794)  
So over the years po, tumataas naman po yung ating unique patient, sir.

**[03:44:32.640] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003795)  
So we started with around 7,383 back in second half of 2022.

**[03:44:40.100] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003796)  
And then it rose to 34,634 patients.

**[03:44:46.980] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003797)  
And then for...

**[03:44:47.960] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003798)  
2024, it's 44,516.

**[03:44:52.020] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003799)  
And in 2025, 59,000 or close to 60,000 po.

**[03:44:57.620] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003800)  
For 2026 po, January until July, it's close to 40,000 na po na patients. And the services po, the diagnostics and the other medical services ay pataas po.

**[03:45:14.280] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003801)  
Sa ngayon po, this year, we were able to introduce the pre-employment medical exam at a lower rate compared to the other clinics or hospitals for our overseas Filipino workers before they depart.

**[03:45:30.180] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003802)  
The other services po that we have ay yung pong cancer care, we are preparing it na.

**[03:45:40.040] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003803)  
It's currently being constructed and we do hope po that by the first...

**[03:45:44.720] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003804)  
To be launched on 2027, right?

**[03:45:46.520] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003805)  
2027 po.

**[03:45:47.780] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003806)  
And then we want, we're targeting a level 3 because the bed capacity is around 102.

**[03:45:56.800] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003807)  
Sa ngayon po, we are at a level 2 with around 78 beds approved by the Department of Health.

**[03:46:05.260] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003808)  
But for the number of patients, do you think, is it all coming from the central Luzon?

**[03:46:12.060] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003809)  
No.

**[03:46:13.285] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003811)  
Based on our statistics, Your Honor, we have around 90 to 95% coming from Luzon, particularly po from central Luzon.

**[03:46:23.360] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003812)  
But we have patients coming from Mindanao, Visayas, and also as far as Bicol region po.

**[03:46:32.440] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003813)  
Actually, sana nga magkaroon tayo, Mr. Chair, ng Luzon, Visayas, Mindanao na OFW Hospital.

**[03:46:39.980] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003814)  
But for my second question,

**[03:46:42.440] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003815)  
since nabanggit niyo na nga po yung cancer care center,

**[03:46:48.160] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003816)  
can the department tell us how much is the 2027 budget specifically intended for the cancer care?

**[03:46:54.500] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003817)  
Aside from the 200 million, kasama na ba yung 200 million doon?

**[03:46:58.860] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003818)  
Enough ba siya to serve all the cancer patients, to pay the salaries of the special doctors?

**[03:47:05.740] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003819)  
It may not be enough, Your Honor, but for

**[03:47:09.520] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003820)  
2027 NEP, our budget is around 400 million po.

**[03:47:21.080] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003821)  
So kaya nagpapadagdag po sana kami ng 200 million pa.

**[03:47:26.880] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003822)  
And then, meron po kaming...

**[03:47:28.980] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003823)  
We're thinking of a dialysis services also because most of the patients sa OFW Hospital po ay meron pong chronic disease,

**[03:47:42.200] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003824)  
Kidney disease, that's the top health or medical needs.

**[03:47:47.960] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003825)  
And then, yung cancer po ay tumataas din po yung cases po ng cancer na pumupunta po sa aming hospital.

**[03:47:57.660] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003826)  
Sana ma-request niyo na yung dialysis center din, maisabay next year?

**[03:48:02.860] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003827)  
Kasi, Mr. Chair, I've been in OFW and nag-medical mission na rin kami sa OFW.

**[03:48:10.100] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003828)  
And it's a very beautiful hospital.

**[03:48:13.700] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003829)  
Medyo may mga kulang, kaya dapat mataasan natin yung budget.

**[03:48:18.540] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003830)  
Pero maayos, na-maintain, na papatakbo ng maayos.

**[03:48:23.780] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003831)  
And we're thankful, Madam Chair.

**[03:48:26.680] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003832)  
She helped us po in one of the medical missions.

**[03:48:29.380] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-003833)  
And we would like to pursue that on a regular basis together with the Makati Medical Center.

**[03:48:36.080] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003834)  
We work with Makati Med for that for every year.

**[03:48:39.340] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003835)  
So, sana hindi lang OFW, but yung mga...

**[03:48:41.260] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003836)  
but yung mga kalapit probinsya ng malapit sa OFW, napagsilbihan natin 'yon.

**[03:48:51.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003837)  
Just a quick thank you.

**[03:48:53.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003838)  
Nasabi na ni Usec Niki, but I wish for it to come from myself.

**[03:48:58.420] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003839)  
Thank you, Congresswoman Yatco.

**[03:49:00.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003840)  
Of course, she, the good Congresswoman brought in the Makati Med doctors.

**[03:49:03.440] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003841)  
who teamed up with our just as able OFW hospital medical staff,

**[03:49:09.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003842)  
joined forces to conduct the medical mission.

**[03:49:13.200] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003843)  
And...

**[03:49:13.800] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003844)  
Expanded services ang kanilang ibinigay.

**[03:49:18.280] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003845)  
May surgical pa eh, no?

**[03:49:19.480] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003846)  
Yes, major surgery.

**[03:49:23.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003847)  
It was a major surgical medical mission.

**[03:49:26.500] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003848)  
It was not an ordinary medical mission.

**[03:49:28.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-003849)  
So, maraming salamat po.

**[03:49:30.380] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003850)  
Kaya sana lahat ng request niyo mapagbigyan.

**[03:49:34.340] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003851)  
Especially the cancer.

**[03:49:35.760] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003852)  
Very excited kami sa cancer center.

**[03:49:37.840] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003853)  
Tsaka sa dialysis.

**[03:49:38.780] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003854)  
Sa daming tumataas na numbers na may sakit sa mga kidney.

**[03:49:43.040] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003856)  
Kailangan talaga ng dialysis.

**[03:49:47.000] Rep. Florabel C. Yatco** (asr-003857)  
Thank you po.

**[03:49:49.758] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-003858)  
Thank you so much, Representative Florabel Yatco

**[03:49:53.900] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-003859)  
Next to interpolate from the majority, we have Honorable Niko Daza.

**[03:50:02.260] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003860)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[03:50:03.660] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003861)  
Good afternoon to our DMW Secretary, Sec. Cacdac.

**[03:50:07.560] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003862)  
Sa ating DMW family, to my colleagues, to our Chairwoman, Aking Kambal.

**[03:50:15.300] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003863)  
On the onset, I would like to commend Sec. Cacdac and our OWA admin, PY, for all the success, full repatriations, all the services you have provided our OFWs in areas where they've been living in areas of struggle and conflict.

**[03:50:35.240] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003864)  
You have been the stability and the support that they have required.

**[03:50:38.080] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003865)  
So, from the top of this interpellation, I would like to commend the DMW family.

**[03:50:44.140] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003866)  
Under the leadership of Sec. Cacdac.

**[03:50:47.060] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003867)  
I just have very few questions.

**[03:50:49.560] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003868)  
I believe last year, I raised this concern with the National Maritime Polytechnic.

**[03:50:59.200] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003869)  
In fact, for some background, the NMP is the only government publicly run maritime training center.

**[03:51:11.160] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003870)  
So, being the single and sole

**[03:51:13.780] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003871)  
Public university for our maritime workers.

**[03:51:19.160] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003872)  
It, to me, is so important that we support NMP and their continued expansion.

**[03:51:25.260] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003873)  
So, I learned recently, in August 3, I believe, is that correct, Madam Chair?

**[03:51:31.040] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003874)  
If I may ask Executive Director Del Rosario, that the NMP has begun to offer free maritime training courses.

**[03:51:38.680] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003875)  
Is that correct?

**[03:51:44.520] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003876)  
With the permission of the good Secretary, Madam Chair.

**[03:51:48.780] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003877)  
First of all,

**[03:51:50.520] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003878)  
I would like to take this opportunity to thank Congressman Niko Daza,

**[03:51:53.460] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003879)  
because in my recollection, he was the only congressman last year who stood for NMP,

**[03:52:00.240] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003880)  
both in the committee hearing and in the plenary.

**[03:52:03.160] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003881)  
Thank you so much, Congressman Daza.

**[03:52:06.460] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003883)  
And to answer your question that is correct, Congressman,

**[03:52:11.080] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003884)  
effective August 3, this year, we have started the implementation of our free training program in all our

**[03:52:19.080] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003885)  
regular training courses

**[03:52:21.020] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003886)  
at NMP. Presently, we have

**[03:52:22.760] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003887)  
55 courses,

**[03:52:24.280] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003888)  
23 of which are mandatory

**[03:52:26.860] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003889)  
STCW courses and the rest

**[03:52:28.900] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003890)  
are recommended courses.

**[03:52:31.000] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003891)  
And that is completely for free

**[03:52:33.040] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003892)  
based on the pursuant to

**[03:52:34.920] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003893)  
the memorandum order issued by

**[03:52:36.720] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003894)  
our good Secretary Cacdac,

**[03:52:39.020] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003895)  
Memorandum Order 360.

**[03:52:42.160] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003896)  
Madam Chair,

**[03:52:43.080] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003897)  
may I ask, how do we fund

**[03:52:44.920] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003898)  
these free courses? Is this subsidized

**[03:52:46.800] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003899)  
by the government?

**[03:52:48.980] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003900)  
Yes, the National

**[03:52:51.020] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003901)  
Maritime Polytechnic.

**[03:52:52.520] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003902)  
We used to be an attached agency of the

**[03:52:54.780] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003903)  
Department of Labor and Employment

**[03:52:56.740] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003904)  
until we were subsumed in

**[03:52:58.860] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003905)  
February 2022 with

**[03:53:00.780] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003906)  
the creation of the Department of Migrant

**[03:53:02.760] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003907)  
Workers. We are fully subsidized

**[03:53:04.660] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003908)  
by the National Government,

**[03:53:06.660] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003909)  
Madam Chair.

**[03:53:08.340] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003910)  
So, Madam Chair, since we are subsidizing

**[03:53:11.120] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003911)  
these free maritime courses,

**[03:53:12.860] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003912)  
which I believe is

**[03:53:14.500] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003913)  
such a powerful

**[03:53:16.640] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003914)  
and will deliver such

**[03:53:18.700] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003915)  
great impact to our

**[03:53:20.660] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003916)  
maritime or those who

**[03:53:22.760] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003917)  
aspire to be a maritime worker

**[03:53:24.600] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003918)  
or a seafarer, I'm concerned

**[03:53:27.280] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003919)  
no, kasi as we look at

**[03:53:28.420] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003920)  
the budget of the NMP,

**[03:53:31.140] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003921)  
there's a

**[03:53:32.600] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003922)  
difference of

**[03:53:33.720] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003923)  
from the 2026

**[03:53:36.800] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003924)  
GAA to its 2027

**[03:53:38.540] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003925)  
NEP, there's a difference of

**[03:53:40.360] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003926)  
210 million or the equivalent of a

**[03:53:42.440] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003927)  
slash of 52%.

**[03:53:44.060] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003928)  
So, of course, after hearing from the

**[03:53:46.540] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003929)  
good executive director that

**[03:53:48.600] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003930)  
we're now offering free maritime

**[03:53:50.380] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003931)  
courses, naturally it's this

**[03:53:52.460] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003932)  
representation's concern, is

**[03:53:54.160] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003933)  
this something that is sustainable?

**[03:53:56.300] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003934)  
As it currently stands,

**[03:53:58.120] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003935)  
your 2027 NEP

**[03:53:59.780] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003936)  
has been slashed 52%.

**[03:54:02.060] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003937)  
So,

**[03:54:03.360] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003938)  
my question to the executive

**[03:54:06.320] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003939)  
director,

**[03:54:07.140] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003940)  
in your perspective, Madam

**[03:54:12.380] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003941)  
Chair,

**[03:54:12.820] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003942)  
what are the funding requirements to

**[03:54:16.200] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003943)  
maintain or sustain these

**[03:54:18.280] Rep. Niko Raul S.J. Daza** (asr-003944)  
free maritime courses?

**[03:54:20.520] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003945)  
First of all, Madam Chair,

**[03:54:22.220] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003946)  
Mr. Congressman, the 51%

**[03:54:24.020] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003947)  
reduction is primarily

**[03:54:26.080] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003948)  
on our capital outlay

**[03:54:28.440] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003949)  
because this year we've been

**[03:54:30.420] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003950)  
given already 271

**[03:54:32.380] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003951)  
million for our infrastructure project.

**[03:54:34.840] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003952)  
So, that is why it's

**[03:54:36.360] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003953)  
heavily reduced by 51%.

**[03:54:38.380] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003954)  
But as far as our

**[03:54:39.800] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003955)  
PS requirement on our

**[03:54:42.360] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003956)  
MOOE, they are

**[03:54:44.100] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003957)  
maintained at a

**[03:54:46.440] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003958)  
comfortable level that

**[03:54:48.140] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003959)  
we will be able to implement

**[03:54:50.080] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003960)  
the free training program by

**[03:54:52.500] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003961)  
next year. As a matter of fact,

**[03:54:54.520] NMP Executive Director III Victor A. Del Rosario** (asr-003962)  
Madam Chair, Mr. Congressman,

Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[04:00:29.000] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-004116)  
Magandang hapon sa ating DMW family and of course the OWWA family. Alam niyo po kanina habang nandito ako halos naitanong na lahat itong mga, lalo na sa reintegration. Mahirap talaga pag huli, halos wala ka nang maitanong eh. So naitanong po na lahat. So maganda naman yung programa ng returning OFWs natin. Naging successful naman yung binibigay tulong ng gobyerno. So meron lang akong katanungan doon, parang hindi ito naitanong. Paano po yung ating OFW na bigyan, tapos ika nga hindi nag-materialize yung binigay sa kanyang pondo? Ano po ang susunod na gagawin ng ating OWWA o ang DMW?

**[04:01:29.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004117)  
Sige po, kami po ni Admin PY ang sasagot sa katanungan.

**[04:01:34.060] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004126)  
It can happen nga po na binigyan ng pondong pang-negosyo, halimbawa. At it can happen na hindi nag-prosper yung negosyo, pero hindi naman po sarado ang pinto.

**[04:01:46.880] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004127)  
Kasi ang batayan namang prinsipyo po diyan is, as long as merong determinasyon at sound business proposal yung ating OFW, naniniwala din po kasi kami na sa isang beses na pagbagsak ng isang OFW sa attempt niya, posibleng dalawang beses siya makakabangon.

**[04:02:07.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004128)  
Ang kailangan pong gawin ay suportahan siya kung karapat-dapat naman pong suportahan.

**[04:02:13.800] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004129)  
We will also reinforce this through business mentorship.

**[04:02:18.200] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004130)  
May mga private sector and government partners po tayo sa DTI, halimbawa, who can provide coaching para mabigyan ng kumpiyansa at saka tulong na rin sa technical na aspeto ng pagnenegosyo.

**[04:02:31.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004131)  
Pangalawa po, kung ito ay sa larangan ng medikal o legal, hindi po natin siya bibitawan kahit anong mangyari.

**[04:02:40.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004132)  
Kung patuloy niyang kailangan ng tulong,

**[04:02:43.140] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004133)  
kahit hindi nag-work yung kanyang negosyo pero patuloy ang kanyang medical assistance na kinakailangan, hindi po natin siya bibitawan.

**[04:02:51.460] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004134)  
Kaya walang magiging impact yung hindi pagtagumpay sa negosyo sa pag-treat ng kanyang medical condition.

**[04:02:59.840] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004135)  
And so goes with legal. We continue to provide legal assistance.

**[04:03:04.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004136)  
Hindi po magiging, hindi magdudulot ng kawalan ng pag-asa sa legal at medical na aspeto o financial na aspeto dahil nandoon pa rin tayo para gabayan siya.

**[04:03:15.700] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004138)  
And of course, as I said, Mr. Congressman, Madam Chair, Admin PY will also have her say on this matter.
