**[04:13:55.580] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-004334)  
Or simple manifestation,

**[04:13:56.920] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-004335)  
which have...

**[04:13:57.940] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-004336)  
Okay, also a few questions.

**[04:14:00.560] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-004337)  
From the majority,

**[04:14:01.840] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-004338)  
we have Representative Jan Chan.

**[04:14:06.780] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004339)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[04:14:08.160] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004340)  
And good afternoon po,

**[04:14:09.320] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004341)  
Secretary, Administrator,

**[04:14:11.620] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004342)  
and everyone who attended.

**[04:14:13.040] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004343)  
So, as a labor law practitioner

**[04:14:15.880] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004344)  
before entering Congress,

**[04:14:17.880] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004345)  
I have conducted a survey

**[04:14:20.080] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004346)  
of the Philippine cases

**[04:14:21.580] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004347)  
na have been decided

**[04:14:23.360] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004348)  
regarding labor law.

**[04:14:25.300] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004349)  
And nakakabahala lang po

**[04:14:27.140] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004350)  
na isa sa mga usual na tinatalakay

**[04:14:29.520] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004351)  
pagdating sa mga kaso

**[04:14:31.380] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004352)  
na umaabot sa Supreme Court,

**[04:14:32.600] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004353)  
palagi po tungkol sa medical repatriation

**[04:14:36.120] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004354)  
Dahil pag nakita po

**[04:14:37.440] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004355)  
natin yung mga

**[04:14:38.300] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004356)  
rules tungkol dito,

**[04:14:40.980] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004357)  
napaka-stringent

**[04:14:42.420] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004358)  
or confusing minsan

**[04:14:43.880] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004359)  
para sa ating mga

**[04:14:44.920] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004360)  
repatriated na mga...

**[04:14:46.540] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004361)  
OFWs, kung paano ba susundin yung mga third-party doctors,

**[04:14:52.480] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004362)  
paano po ba yung mga susundin nila na timeline when it comes to securing the opinion of a third-party doctor.

**[04:15:00.520] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004363)  
So makikita po natin na maraming beses po na hindi po nakakaklaim ang ating OFWs

**[04:15:07.040] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004364)  
ng mga medical reimbursements na dapat nakukuha po nila sa mga kumpanya.

**[04:15:11.500] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004365)  
So yung tanong ko lang po, Secretary, ano po ba yung mga measures natin para masiguro na natutulungan po natin

**[04:15:18.720] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004366)  
yung mga ganitong medically repatriated OFWs?

**[04:15:21.400] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004367)  
And meron po ba tayong figures kung gano'ng karami po yung mga applicants for assistance

**[04:15:28.340] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004368)  
kung meron pong binibigay po ang DMW sa kanila?

**[04:15:32.460] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004369)  
Salamat po.

**[04:15:34.200] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004370)  
Thank you, Mr. Congressman and Madam Chair.

**[04:15:39.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004371)  
Definitely, you are known.

**[04:15:41.360] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004372)  
As a legal labor practitioner. And we very well could work with your good office, Mr. Congressman,

**[04:15:50.800] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004373)  
along the lines of helping our OFWs overcome yung legal challenges nila, lalo na yung mga tripulante natin.

**[04:15:58.200] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004374)  
Indeed, the Magna Carta for Seafarers has resolved in terms of policy, practice,

**[04:16:05.840] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004375)  
itong problema sa recovery of compensation claims.

**[04:16:10.080] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004376)  
So we have undertaken the IRR, the rules and regulations to implement the institutionalization of third-party doctors

**[04:16:20.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004377)  
as well as working closely with the DOH along these lines.

**[04:16:27.060] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004378)  
With your permission, Mr. Congressman, Madam Chair, may I ask Usec. Bernard Olalia,

**[04:16:32.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004379)  
to also provide us further details as he has been on top of the IRR development implementation as well of the Magna Carta.

**[04:16:43.040] DMW Undersecretary Bernard P. Olalia** (asr-004380)  
With the kind permission of the Secretary, one of the greatest accomplishments of this administration,

**[04:16:48.360] DMW Undersecretary Bernard P. Olalia** (asr-004381)  
including this Congress, is yung the passage of the Magna Carta for Seafarers.

**[04:16:52.580] DMW Undersecretary Bernard P. Olalia** (asr-004382)  
At tama po si Sec, meron na pong IRR niyan.

**[04:16:55.620] DMW Undersecretary Bernard P. Olalia** (asr-004383)  
And under the rules ng Magna Carta, first would be the assessment, medical assessment to be conducted by the company doctor.

**[04:17:02.840] DMW Undersecretary Bernard P. Olalia** (asr-004384)  
If the company doctor's assessment is not objected to by the OFW, that will be a final assessment.

**[04:17:09.360] DMW Undersecretary Bernard P. Olalia** (asr-004385)  
However, kung meron ang counter-assessment si OFW,

**[04:17:12.820] DMW Undersecretary Bernard P. Olalia** (asr-004386)  
medical doctor at conflict doon sa company doctor, we need a third doctor.

**[04:17:17.920] DMW Undersecretary Bernard P. Olalia** (asr-004387)  
doon po papasok yung DMW intervention.

**[04:17:20.400] DMW Undersecretary Bernard P. Olalia** (asr-004388)  
Because the DMW will craft the guidelines for the selection of a third doctor to be chosen from among the doctors ng DOH.

**[04:17:31.660] DMW Undersecretary Bernard P. Olalia** (asr-004389)  
And currently, meron po kaming kinacraft na joint guidelines with the DOH for the selection of the third doctor.

**[04:17:39.520] DMW Undersecretary Bernard P. Olalia** (asr-004390)  
Ang isa pong kagandahan ng Magna Carta is the

**[04:17:42.840] DMW Undersecretary Bernard P. Olalia** (asr-004391)  
action fund to be utilized as a surety bond in case na magkaroon po ng appeal,

**[04:17:50.400] DMW Undersecretary Bernard P. Olalia** (asr-004392)  
yung decision to be issued by the NLRC or NCMB.

**[04:17:54.340] DMW Undersecretary Bernard P. Olalia** (asr-004393)  
So along these lines, the DMW will be ready to assist in the full implementation of the provisions of the Magna Carta po.

**[04:18:02.800] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004394)  
Maraming salamat po, Usec. and Secretary.

**[04:18:05.760] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004395)  
One last question lang po.

**[04:18:07.460] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004396)  
So, bali doon po sa action fund,

**[04:18:10.620] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004397)  
would that be sufficient po sa dami po ng

**[04:18:13.160] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004398)  
mga possible cases po na lumalapit po sa DMW at saka sa mga ganito po yung mga conflicting na mga opinions of the doctors,

**[04:18:20.400] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004399)  
Would the AKSYON Fund be enough po to assist these repatriated OFWs?

**[04:18:30.100] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004400)  
Yes, it would be sufficient.

**[04:18:33.560] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004401)  
But of course, having said that,

**[04:18:34.980] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004402)  
we are yet to determine the, to benchmark the experience.

**[04:18:40.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004403)  
But as we estimate it,

**[04:18:43.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004404)  
ang makukover po dito would be sufficient in terms of the number of those who need to be legally assisted.

**[04:18:52.180] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004405)  
Because ang isang confidence level po namin ay yung increasing number of conciliated cases.

**[04:18:57.960] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004406)  
Whereas, you know, Mr. Congressman,

**[04:19:01.500] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004407)  
hindi kailangan ng lawyers or adjudicatory services of a lawyer.

**[04:19:06.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004408)  
So, in which case, with conciliation as a main mode of dispute settlement,

**[04:19:13.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004409)  
hindi na nga ho kailangan umabot sa legal intervention,

**[04:19:18.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004410)  
let's say sa NLRC or elsewhere, voluntary arbitration.

**[04:19:21.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004411)  
So, we're confident that may increasing, as confirmed by the sector, the industry,

**[04:19:28.000] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004412)  
may increasing number of conciliated cases and settled through conciliation.

**[04:19:33.640] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004413)  
So, we're confident na this won't necessitate the hiring of a lawyer.

**[04:19:38.260] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-004414)  
Sir, if I may add as well

**[04:19:39.900] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-004415)  
as regards the efforts of OWWA

**[04:19:43.580] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-004416)  
when it comes to conciliation, specifically of sea-based workers,

**[04:19:49.420] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-004417)  
we have reached last year for 2025,

**[04:19:52.480] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-004418)  
99.23% settlement rate.

**[04:19:56.480] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-004419)  
The monetary claims awarded is close to 180 million.

**[04:20:01.600] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-004420)  
If you add that to a land base,

**[04:20:05.240] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-004421)  
our total settlement rate is at 94%.

**[04:20:08.200] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-004422)  
So, it's quite a good number.

**[04:20:10.320] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-004423)  
And

**[04:20:11.220] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-004424)  
close to 200,

**[04:20:14.440] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-004425)  
over 202 million in settlement amount.

**[04:20:18.180] OWWA Administrator Patricia Yvonne "PY" M. Caunan** (asr-004426)  
Thank you so much.

**[04:20:29.220] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004427)  
Bale, yun lang po Madam Chair,

**[04:20:30.900] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004428)  
Secretary, Administrator, and USEC.

**[04:20:33.080] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004429)

**[04:20:34.260] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004430)  
And nakakatuwa po marinig na

**[04:20:35.720] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004431)

**[04:20:39.960] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004432)  
So, maraming salamat po.

**[04:20:41.180] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004433)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[04:20:44.700] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-004434)  
Thank you so much, Representative Jan Chan.

**[04:20:48.400] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-004435)  
Next to interpolate,

**[04:20:49.800] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-004436)  
and maybe last but not the least na naman,

**[04:20:53.420] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-004437)  
I don't want to jinx it.

**[04:20:54.880] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-004438)  
Yes.

**[04:20:56.180] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-004439)  
We have our Vice Chair,

**[04:20:57.760] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-004440)  
Vice Chair Bella Suansing.

**[04:21:00.860] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004441)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[04:21:02.120] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004442)  
Good afternoon to the DMW family,

**[04:21:04.580] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004443)  
Sec. Hans,

**[04:21:05.460] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004444)  
Admin PY,

**[04:21:07.720] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004445)  
the rest of the very hardworking Department of Migrant Workers.

**[04:21:12.500] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004446)  
My line of questioning would be about OFW dependents.

**[04:21:18.340] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004447)  
This happens a lot in our district where

**[04:21:21.120] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004448)  
unfortunately,

**[04:21:23.460] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004449)  
the children,

**[04:21:24.820] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004450)  
the women of our OFWs are those most at risk

**[04:21:30.080] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004451)  
to online threats.

**[04:21:32.780] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004452)  
I'm not going to mention,

**[04:21:35.100] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004453)  
but I think everyone is well-versed on what has been happening,

**[04:21:39.140] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004454)  
all of the threats that emerge for our children in the online space.

**[04:21:44.300] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004455)  
I just want to ask the DMW family,

**[04:21:48.820] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004456)  
if under the Action Fund,

**[04:21:52.460] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004457)  
if we have to repatriate,

**[04:21:54.600] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004458)  
because this happens a lot,

**[04:21:55.960] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004459)  
where the children and the dependents OFWs

**[04:22:00.760] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004460)  
fall to abuse,

**[04:22:05.740] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004461)  
and we have to repatriate our OFWs,

**[04:22:09.700] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004462)  
does that fall under the Action Fund?

**[04:22:13.740] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004463)  
Yes, and definitely,

**[04:22:15.880] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004464)  
Madam Chair, Madam Congresswoman,

**[04:22:20.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004465)  
OFW families are at the top of the President's agenda.

**[04:22:25.120] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004466)  
Insofar as protection is concerned.

**[04:22:27.640] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004467)  
In fact,

**[04:22:28.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004468)  
No. 1 marching order po sa amin ni Secretary Toots

**[04:22:31.260] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004469)  
Ople ng Presidente,

**[04:22:32.480] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004470)  
ang protection of OFW families.

**[04:22:35.340] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004472)  
And this is always, this has always been his concern.

**[04:22:39.240] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004473)  
Every time there is a welfare case,

**[04:22:40.940] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004474)  
kamusta ang pamilya, dependents?

**[04:22:43.440] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004475)  
So, short answer is yes,

**[04:22:45.800] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004476)  
kasama ang OFW dependents sa ating Action Fund coverage,

**[04:22:50.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004477)  
lalo na if they are victims of abuse.

**[04:22:54.160] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004478)  
Very recently,

**[04:22:55.600] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004479)  
I won't name the country for the protection of the family,

**[04:22:59.480] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004480)  
but we had an OFW married to a foreign national

**[04:23:03.780] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004481)  
who was escaping an abusive situation,

**[04:23:07.920] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004482)  
and together with the DFA,

**[04:23:10.100] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004483)  
napa-uwi po natin yung mag-ina.

**[04:23:12.280] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004484)  
So, we are involved in these types of situations.

**[04:23:16.900] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004485)  
As I mentioned at the outset in my intro,

**[04:23:19.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004486)  
even OFW families who are affected by the distressful situations

**[04:23:24.460] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004487)  
that their loved ones,

**[04:23:26.480] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004488)  
OFWs,

**[04:23:27.560] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004489)  
moms or dads go through,

**[04:23:29.380] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004490)  
tinutulungan po rin natin.

**[04:23:31.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004491)  
Of course, we need the inter-agency cooperation of DSWD

**[04:23:35.620] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004492)  
and DepEd kung kailangan may school interventions dun sa bata,

**[04:23:39.320] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004493)  
guidance, counselling, etc.

**[04:23:41.020] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004494)  
So, short answer is yes.

**[04:23:43.540] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004495)  
But Madam Chair,

**[04:23:45.100] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004496)  
I'd like to also request you, Sec. Nicky,

**[04:23:47.520] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004497)  
kasi we have an impending proposal with the social cluster ng kabinet.

**[04:23:53.800] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004498)  
We will propose to put this forward with the cluster that is sub-headed,

**[04:23:58.680] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004499)  
it's a sub-cluster headed by Sec. Arsi Balisacan

**[04:24:02.220] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004500)  
So, can I just ask Usec. Nicky,

**[04:24:05.940] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004501)  
to briefly walk us through the framework of what we will propose

**[04:24:10.760] DMW Secretary Hans Leo J. Cacdac** (asr-004502)  
kasi napakahalaga po talaga ng OFW dependents.

**[04:24:15.000] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004503)  
Madam Chair and honorable members,

**[04:24:18.060] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004504)  
with the permission of the Secretary po,

**[04:24:20.480] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004506)  
the Secretary has planned to have an aftercare for children left behind

**[04:24:27.700] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004507)  
knowing that yung mga incidents of violence involving children po

**[04:24:33.280] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004508)  
na nababalitaan na po natin.

**[04:24:35.940] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004509)  
So, it's still a convergence effort,

**[04:24:38.100] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004510)  
but the care would,

**[04:24:40.980] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004511)  
because there's a care drain for children left behind

**[04:24:44.100] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004512)  
and therefore,

**[04:24:45.260] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004513)  
yung approach po natin would involve at home,

**[04:24:50.440] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004514)  
level,

**[04:24:52.100] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004515)  
and then at the community level

**[04:24:54.220] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004516)  
and also at the national government level.

**[04:24:57.700] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004517)  
So, marami pong programa ang ating mga iba't-ibang ahensya ng pamahalaan

**[04:25:02.140] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004518)  
but how to tie them together

**[04:25:03.780] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004519)  
to look after the children left behind,

**[04:25:07.020] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004520)  
yun po yung gusto nating marating.

**[04:25:10.020] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004521)  
And the goal is to establish a coordinated,

**[04:25:13.420] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004522)  
continuous and child-centered support system

**[04:25:17.380] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004523)  
that safeguards not just the physical,

**[04:25:19.640] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004524)  
but also the mental, psychological,

**[04:25:22.500] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004525)  
social and economic well-being of the children left behind.

**[04:25:26.260] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004526)  
So, meron po tayong mga iba't-ibang support,

**[04:25:32.080] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004527)  
temporary caregiving and early identification

**[04:25:35.820] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004528)  
of probably the problems of these children left behind.

**[04:25:41.280] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004529)  
It also involves child protection from abuse,

**[04:25:45.800] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004530)  
especially sexual abuse,

**[04:25:47.880] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004531)  
violence,

**[04:25:49.100] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004532)  
and also yung pong neglect and bullying.

**[04:25:51.240] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004533)  
And there's also a caregiver capacity component

**[04:25:56.040] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004534)  
para po doon sa mga naiiwan sa bata,

**[04:26:00.420] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004535)  
whether it's at home or at the community,

**[04:26:02.720] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004536)  
na mabigyan din po sila ng capacity building

**[04:26:06.740] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004537)  
for caregiving because of the parental absence.

**[04:26:10.740] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004538)  
And then yung service convergence na po

**[04:26:13.580] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004539)  
na sinasabi natin involving, for instance,

**[04:26:16.460] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004540)  
the DSWD,

**[04:26:17.700] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004541)  
which Sec. Hans already discussed with Secretary Rex.

**[04:26:21.400] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004542)  
And then we will also be involving TESDA.

**[04:26:24.660] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004543)  
The DILG because at the local community level,

**[04:26:29.380] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004544)  
yun po yung mas pangangailangan ng intervention.

**[04:26:32.620] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004545)  
And the Department of Education po

**[04:26:34.740] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004546)  
because yung sa school incidents po na nangyayari.

**[04:34:39.560] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004774)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[04:34:41.580] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004775)  
Your Honors,

**[04:34:43.080] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004777)  
and with the permission of the Secretary po.

**[04:34:45.940] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004778)  
Meron po tayong tinatawag na telemedicine.

**[04:34:49.580] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004779)  
It started way back COVID pandemic po

**[04:34:53.200] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004780)  
when OFW Hospital offered that services.

**[04:34:55.900] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004781)  
Given the rising need for mental health and wellness po,

**[04:35:03.140] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004782)  
meron po tayong partnership, for instance,

**[04:35:04.920] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004783)  
with the DSWD and also DOH

**[04:35:08.720] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004784)  
who also conducts online mamampu ito,

**[04:35:12.140] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004785)  
especially for DSWD,

**[04:35:13.160] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004786)  
DOH para po dun sa mental health and wellness

**[04:35:17.540] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004787)  
of our OFWs overseas.

**[04:35:20.220] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004788)  
But for people here in the country po,

**[04:35:23.600] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004789)  
we have a psychologist in the OFW Hospital.

**[04:35:28.180] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004790)  
May mga, ano po tayo,

**[04:35:29.000] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004791)  
sessions on mental wellness,

**[04:35:33.540] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004792)  
but there's still a need to improve it.

**[04:35:36.160] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004793)  
We need to have a psychiatrist

**[04:35:37.820] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004794)  
because mas maganda po na psychiatrist po.

**[04:35:41.880] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004796)  
So, we, we started working with or talking to

**[04:35:45.740] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004797)  
a number of private sector providers

**[04:35:49.220] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004798)  
para po ma-augment po yung

**[04:35:51.520] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004799)  
pangangailangan po ng OFW Hospital.

**[04:35:54.940] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004800)  
And upon the instruction din po of the Secretary,

**[04:35:57.840] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004801)  
yung field health po kasi meron din po silang

**[04:36:00.000] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004802)  
component on mental health.

**[04:36:03.080] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004803)  
And we engaged with them.

**[04:36:06.060] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004804)  
Similar to our problem,

**[04:36:07.720] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004805)  
medyo kakaunti pa lang po yung mga psychiatrist na

**[04:36:10.620] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004806)

**[04:36:14.440] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004808)  
But we endeavor po to strengthen this

**[04:36:18.440] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004809)  
because this is really a need.

**[04:36:19.980] DMW Undersecretary Dominique Rubia-Tutay** (asr-004810)  
Thank you.

**[04:36:35.460] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004816)  
When the war struck the Middle East,

**[04:36:40.120] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004817)  
we have thousands of OFWs

**[04:36:42.680] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004818)  
in the 2nd District of Sultan Kudarat.

**[04:36:45.100] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004819)  
Everyone was panicking.

**[04:36:47.180] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004820)  
Kasi they couldn't contact their parents.

**[04:36:51.160] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004821)  
They couldn't contact everyone.

**[04:36:56.680] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004822)  
And they contacted me

**[04:37:01.480] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004823)  
kasi they didn't know where else to turn.

**[04:37:03.500] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004824)  
And I contacted the DMW in a week.

**[04:37:06.760] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004825)  
Nag-repatriate po yung mga

**[04:37:09.600] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004826)  
pinapasa po namin sa inyong mga pangalan.

**[04:37:12.120] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004827)  
Thank you so much for the work that you do.

**[04:37:16.460] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004829)  
Thank you so much for the work that you do

**[04:37:19.540] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004830)  
for our unsung heroes.

**[04:37:21.040] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004831)  
Thank you so much.

**[04:37:22.880] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004832)
